- **Authors**: Junkai Chen, Yuhao He, Junxiang You, Ruiqi Liu, Chenyu Wang, Shu Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Multimodal Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大语言模型遗忘中训练方法难以平衡遗忘效果与模型效用、训练免方法存在参数级遗忘不足等问题，提出VGID（视觉噪声引导的上下文蒸馏）框架，通过双模态干预（视觉扰动+文本上下文遗忘）构建教师分布，实现参数级遗忘。实验表明在保持模型效用的同时遗忘效果显著。

<h3 id="229">[229] A Methodological Framework for Explicit Control of the Speed-Accuracy Trade-off in Brain-Computer Interfaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00106](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00106) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Javier Jiménez, Francisco B Rodríguez
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对脑机接口中速度-精度权衡缺乏显式控制的问题，提出独立于分类器和早停策略的评估框架，使用增益和守恒两个指标，并通过参数α调节权衡。在P300范式上验证，可实现快速、准确或平衡的BCI行为。

<h3 id="230">[230] Motif-based morphology signatures for interpretable ECG screening and monitoring</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00107) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nivedita Bijlani, Mauricio Villarroel
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, ECG Analysis
- **Venue**: IEEE Engineering in Medicine and Biology Conference (EMBC) 2026
- **Summary**: 针对心电图解释依赖人工且仅在短暂记录中检测异常的问题，提出基于模板的心电图形态特征框架，定义三种漂移度量。在PTB-XL和MIT-BIH数据集上验证，能显著区分正常和异常，支持可解释的纵向监测。

<h3 id="231">[231] Project SPARROW and the Future of Conservation Technology</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00108](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00108) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juan M. Lavista Ferres, Carl Chalmers, Bruno Demuro Segundo, Zhongqi Miao, Andres Hernandez Celis, Federico Alves Torres, Isai Daniel Chacon Silva, Anthony Cintron Roman, Allen Kim, Meygha Machado, Luana Marotti, Amy Michaels, Daniela Ruiz Lopez, Catherine Romero, Rahul Dodhia, Inbal Becker-Reshef, Pablo Arbelaez
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Environmental AI, Edge Computing, Autonomous Systems
- **Summary**: 针对生物多样性监测中电力、连接性和可及性限制，提出SPARROW开源平台，集成太阳能、边缘AI和卫星通信，实现远程环境自主持续监测。在多个生态系统中部署，24/7运行，收集超过200万图像和音频记录，实现全自主运行。

<h3 id="232">[232] VDSB-GWSyn: Diffusion Schrödinger Bridge for Controllable and Anatomically Feasible Guidewire Synthesis in Coronary Angiography</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00109](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00109) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoyuan Tang, Zhuo Zhang, Jialin Li, Shuai Xiao, Jiachen Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Image Synthesis, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 针对冠状动脉造影中导丝标注稀缺和合成模型适应性差的问题，提出VDSB-GWSyn框架，基于扩散薛定谔桥模型，利用形状先验和血管分割掩码约束生成高保真导丝样本。实验表明合成数据预训练显著提升导丝端点定位性能，MPE从16.01 px降至7.71 px，PCK@3px从52.63%提升至86.27%。

<h3 id="233">[233] Enhancing BiGRU with a KAN Block for Legal Document Classification and Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00116](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00116) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Faizul Haque Dhrubo, Souvik Pramanik, Most. Aysha Siddika Sumona, Shahnewaz Siddique, Mohammad Ashrafuzzaman Khan, Mohammad Abdul Qayum, Mohsin Sajjad
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Legal AI, Text Classification, Summarization
- **Summary**: 针对低资源多语言法律文档分类和摘要任务，提出基于KAN的BiGRU模型。在孟加拉国法律文档数据集上，分类准确率67.96%，F1 0.65；摘要ROUGE-1/2/L分别为0.38/0.23/0.31。消融实验表明KAN提升分类准确率约10%。

<h3 id="234">[234] V2I Work Zone Geometry Reconstruction with Pose-Conditioned UWB Range Denoising</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxi Liu, Hangyu Li, Yang Cheng, Rui Gana, Junwei You, Weizhe Tang, Peng Zhang, Steven T. Parker, Xiaopeng Li, Bin Ran
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Sensor Fusion
- **Summary**: 针对车联网工作区几何重建中UWB测距的异常值和NLOS误差等问题，提出姿态条件、置换等价的预测去噪器，利用锚点共享时间预测和对称集合聚合。在实际CAV数据上，测量加权均方误差降低66.9%，显著提升锥体定位和工作区几何重建精度。

<h3 id="235">[235] SpikeWFM: Spiking-Aided Wireless Foundation Model for Robust Channel Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00120) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liwen Jing, Yisha Lu, Tingting Yang, Li Sun, Yuxuan Shi, Yuwei Wang, Mengfan Zheng, Leiyang Xu
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Wireless Networks, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 针对无线基础模型对噪声和干扰脆弱的问题，提出SpikeWFM，融合脉冲神经网络与ANN转换器，利用脉冲神经元的事件驱动处理增强鲁棒性。理论分析和实验表明，SpikeWFM在预训练收敛和信道预测精度上均优于传统ANN-WFM。

<h3 id="236">[236] Versatile Framework with Semantic and Structural guidance for Image Reconstruction from Brain Activity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00121](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00121) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yizhuo Lu, Changde Du, Qiongyi Zhou, Liuyun Jiang, Huiguang He
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, Image Generation, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 针对从脑活动重建视觉刺激时细粒度结构信息保真度不足的问题，提出MindDiffuser两阶段框架：第一阶段使用CLIP文本嵌入和Stable Diffusion生成语义图像，第二阶段用浅层CLIP视觉特征迭代优化结构信息。在fMRI、EEG、MEG三种模态上均显著优于先前方法，并具有神经生物学合理性。

<h3 id="237">[237] CardioLens: Revealing the Clinical Reality Gap of MLLMs via Multi-Sequence Cardiac MRI Evaluations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00123](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00123) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zixian Su, Hongkai Zhang, Fan Gao, Encheng Su, Taiping Qu, Jingwei Guo, Nan Zhang, Hui Wang, Zhen Zhou, Kairui Bo, Yan Chen, Yue Ren, Shuai Li, Lei Xu, Henggui Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大语言模型在临床心脏MRI评估中的现实差距，构建CardioLens测试床（来自私有医院数据，含473,896张切片和13,494个QA对），评估图像理解、报告生成和疾病诊断。24个先进MLLM表现差，且性能沿实际工作流下降；混淆分析显示类别崩溃故障。

<h3 id="238">[238] Multimodal Music Recommendation System using LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00125](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00125) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Srikar Prabhas Kandagatla, Sreehitha R. Narayana, Chandana Magapu, Swetha Mohan, Shamanth Kuthpadi, Hongjie Chen, Ryan A. Rossi, Franck Dernoncourt, Nesreen Ahmed
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Sequential Recommendation, Multimodal Learning, LLM
- **Summary**: 针对音乐推荐系统忽略语义和声学内容的问题，提出多模态框架，为LastFM-1K数据集补充音频、歌词嵌入、LLM语义元数据和收听完成率，并扩展E4SRec框架。实验表明融合内容特征相比仅ID序列推荐Recall提升高达95%，NDCG提升79%，但简单多模态融合未必带来累加增益。

<h3 id="239">[239] A Shared Valence Axis Across Modern LLMs and Human EEG: The Saturation Regularity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00129](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00129) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yousef A. Radwan, Xuhui Liu, Kilichbek Haydarov, Yuqian Fu, Mohamed Elhoseiny
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Brain-Computer Interface, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 研究现代LLM能否作为理解人类脑电情感价态表示的透镜。构建基于LLM的价态轴V-axis，并在123名受试者观看情感视频的EEG上验证其映射到神经活动，且36个EEG情感分类器自发发现相同方向。但进一步发现该收敛无法提供有效训练信号，提出饱和规律，并通过残差多样性集成提升FACED准确率10.5%。

<h3 id="240">[240] Automatically Differentiable Nonlinear Tensor Networks (ADNTNs) for Exponential Compression of Deep Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00130](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00130) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrzej Cichocki, Michal Wietczak
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Tensor Networks
- **Summary**: 提出自动可微非线性张量网络（ADNTN），作为结构化权重生成器，通过紧凑核心张量端到端训练，扩展低秩适应和因子分解。在AlexNet和VGG-16上实现每层压缩比约2000倍到77000倍，精度通常匹配甚至提升基线。

<h3 id="241">[241] AI-PROPELLER: Warehouse-Scale Interprocedural Code Layout Optimization with AlphaEvolve</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00131](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00131) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chaitanya Mamatha Ananda, Rajiv Gupta, Mircea Trofin, Aiden Grossman, Sriraman Tallam, Xinliang David Li, Amir Yazdanbakhsh
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.PL
- **Tags**: Code Optimization, Compiler Optimization
- **Summary**: 目前的后链接优化器仅支持过程内代码布局，忽视了全局过程间布局的巨大潜力。本文提出AI-PROPELLER，采用Magellan智能体工作流将编译器启发式方法演变为细粒度过程间优化器，并通过真实硬件性能计数器提供精确奖励信号。在大型仓库级应用中，该方法在已有先进FDO/PLO基础上额外获得0.23%至1.6%的性能提升，首次实现了工业环境下细粒度过程间代码布局。

<h3 id="242">[242] Foundation-Preserving Adaptation via Generalized Rayleigh-Quotient Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00132](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00132) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongjun Kim, Adrian de Wynter, Huancheng Chen, Heasung Kim, Haris Vikalo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 微调会损害基础模型预训练获得的非目标任务能力，现有遗忘感知方法缺乏动态调节。本文提出FoLoRA，通过广义Rayleigh商在遗忘惩罚与任务效用之间评分更新方向，并采用方向门控Adam更新。在数学、代码和指令遵循任务上，FoLoRA在目标任务性能和保留非目标能力之间达到了最佳平衡。

<h3 id="243">[243] XAI-SOH-FL: Enhancing SOH-FL with Adaptive Aggregation and Explainable AI for Intrusion Detection in Heterogeneous IoT</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00134) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ambreen Aslam, Maaz Hassan, Bibi Zahra, Muhammad Khuram Shahzad
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Intrusion Detection, Explainable AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aaslam-msit/SOH-FL-Enhancement)
- **Summary**: 物联网入侵检测面临数据异质、缺乏标签和可解释性差的问题，现有SOH-FL依赖手动调参且不可解释。本文提出XAI-SOH-FL，引入基于相似度阈值的动态聚合参数选择、贝叶斯优化自动调参以及SHAP特征级可解释性。在CICIDS2017数据集上准确率达94.12%，F1分数0.92，优于基线且收敛更快。

<h3 id="244">[244] On Effectiveness and Efficiency of Agentic Tool-calling and RL Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00135](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00135) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tong Liu, Cheng Qian, Matej Cief, Yuan He, Daniele Dan, Nikolaos Aletras, Gabriella Kazai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 工具调用评估对实现细节高度敏感，导致排行榜不可靠；RL训练存在计算浪费。本文系统分析了评估管道的不稳定性，并提出了两种加速RL工具调用训练的技术。实验表明评估结果受随机种子、系统提示等影响显著，所提技术在不降低性能的前提下实现了训练加速。

<h3 id="245">[245] Generative AI and Digital Ecosystem Resilience: A Proactive Lifecycle-Based Survey</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00136](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00136) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonghyun Chung, Rishabh Chaddha, Sanket Badhe, Debanshu Das, Nathan Huang, Amanpreet Kaur
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CR; cs.SI
- **Tags**: Survey, Misinformation, AI-Generated Content
- **Venue**: IEEE Access
- **Summary**: 生成式AI制造的对抗性内容使被动检测失效。本文以C5交互模型为框架，综述了基于生命周期的主动检测方法，包括协调不诚实行为分析、流行病学建模、异常检测和智能体系统。文章还指出了生成式AI带来的挑战，展望了建立弹性信息生态系统的未来研究方向。

<h3 id="246">[246] Geodesics with Unified Tangent-constrained Priors and Curvature Regularization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00139](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00139) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chong Di, Li Liu, Jinglin Zhang, Zhenjiang Li, Da Chen, Laurent D. Cohen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 曲率惩罚测地线模型在分割复杂形状时易出现短路，缺乏形状感知的切向约束。本文提出统一的测地线框架，在方向提升空间中集成切向约束先验与曲率惩罚，形成切向约束的Finsler度量。在合成、自然和医学图像上的实验表明，该框架有效增强了弱边界和拓扑捷径的鲁棒性，提高了形状保真度。

<h3 id="247">[247] Geometric Erasure by Contrastive Velocity Matching in Rectified Flows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00140](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00140) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonas Henry Grebe, Tobias Braun, Anna Rohrbach, Marcus Rohrbach
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Diffusion Model, Generative Model Alignment
- **Summary**: 随着生成模型从U-Net扩散模型转向整流流Transformer，概念擦除研究滞后。本文提出GEM框架，通过对比速度匹配将轨迹级反学习与教师引导擦除相结合。实验表明GEM能有效抑制有害概念生成，同时保持良性生成能力。

<h3 id="248">[248] Adaptive data selection improves wearable prediction under low baseline performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00141](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00141) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ali Kargarandehkordi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wearable Computing, Healthcare Monitoring, Data Selection
- **Summary**: 自适应感知在可穿戴健康监测中的个体收益差异尚不明确。本文在多模态可穿戴数据上评估了自适应时间窗口选择策略，发现低基线性能参与者AUROC提升可达0.7，而高基线参与者增益有限甚至为负。自适应增益与基线性能呈强负相关（Pearson r=-0.67），表明自适应感知在低性能场景下最有价值。

<h3 id="249">[249] Regime-Adaptive Continual Learning for Portfolio Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00143) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chaofan Pan, Lingfei Ren, Linbo Xiong, Yonghao Li, Wei Wei, Xin Yang
- **Subjects**: q-fin.PM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Financial AI, Portfolio Optimization
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 金融市场频繁变结构使传统投资组合管理失效。本文提出ReCAP框架，利用自适应状态检测将历史数据分割为可变长度状态，构建策略库并通过状态门控模块实现快速适应。在五个真实数据集上，ReCAP持续优于基线，实现了长期投资中的高收益和快速状态适应。

<h3 id="250">[250] BudgetDraft: Acceptance-Aware Multi-View Training for Sparse-KV Speculative Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00144](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00144) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liang He, Jingbo Wen, Qishi Zhan, Yixiong Chen, Kangning Cui, Qizhen Lan, Xilu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, KV Cache, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 稀疏KV缓存投机解码在长上下文下因稀疏/全缓存不匹配导致接受率下降。本文提出BudgetDraft，通过多视图稀疏训练使草稿模型适应多种KV预算，结合接受感知损失和多视图损失。在PG-19、LongBench和LWM上，相对于自回归解码，在4K、8K和16K上下文长度下分别实现最多6.55倍、4.46倍和2.10倍端到端加速，且内存友好。

<h3 id="251">[251] Completion at the Boundary (CaB): Deployable Switching with Completion-Aware Control under Limited Calibration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00145](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00145) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusuke Sano, Takeshi Itoga
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Vision-Language Model, Task Planning
- **Summary**: 部署的VLA智能体缺少决定指令是否完成的接口，导致复合任务中交接时机错误引发下游失败。本文提出CaB，通过边界相位令牌预测局部完成对象，并利用该对象控制切换时机和动作生成。在Minecraft VLA基准上，CaB在有限校准条件下改进了复合任务执行和交接质量。

<h3 id="252">[252] Multi-Contrast MRI Motion Correction via Parameter-Informed Disentanglement and Adaptive Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00146](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00146) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Honglin Xiong, Yuxian Tang, Feng Li, Yulin Wang, Lei Xiang, Dinggang Shen, Qian Wang
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Image Restoration
- **Summary**: MRI运动伪影校正方法通常针对特定对比度，无法泛化。本文提出统一框架，通过ScanCLIP解耦对比样式与解剖内容，利用ViT估计运动严重度并路由至混合专家网络校正。在IXI和HCP上PSNR提升0.75 dB，SSIM提升0.0279，且在未知参数的临床数据上表现出鲁棒的零样本泛化能力。

<h3 id="253">[253] RAFT: Data Refinement and Adaptive Distillation for Domain Fine-Tuning with Alleviated Forgetting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00147) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuduo Li, Xiaofeng Shi, Qian Kou, Longbin Yu, Hua Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Instruction Tuning, Knowledge Distillation, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 领域微调会降低通用能力，原因在于监督兼容性缺口和轨迹保持缺口。本文提出RAFT框架：第一阶段构建模型兼容的监督信号；第二阶段进行答案条件在线蒸馏。在多个领域上，RAFT相比标准SFT平均领域准确率提升23.2%，并在MS-Bench和IFEval上分别恢复18.2%和10.2%的退化性能。

<h3 id="254">[254] StemBind: When MLLMs Get Lost Between Rules and Instances in Abstract Visual Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00148](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00148) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xixiang He, Baiqi Wu, Xingming Li, Ao Cheng, Qiyao Sun, Xuanyu Ji, Qingyong Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Visual Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型在抽象视觉推理中常出现“知道规则但选错答案”的现象，现有基准无法定位错误步骤。本文构建StemBind诊断基准，通过共享主干问题分离感知、规则和完整推理步骤。评估24个模型发现，规则准确率普遍高于完整准确率，且即使感知和规则都正确，仍有51.2%的绑定失败，瓶颈在于规则到实例的映射。

<h3 id="255">[255] Persona Attack: Incremental Memory Injection Jailbreak Attack against Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00150](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00150) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junyoung Park, Seongyong Ju, Sunghwan Park, Jaewoo Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 现有越狱攻击多为单次提示注入，未利用模型对上下文对话的记忆。本文提出Persona Attack，通过逐步内存注入操作模型上下文窗口，逐步覆盖安全对齐机制。实验表明，攻击成功率取决于内存实现和指令组合，特定配置下可达95%。

<h3 id="256">[256] Emergence of Exploration in Policy Gradient Reinforcement Learning via Retrying</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00151](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00151) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soichiro Nishimori, Paavo Parmas, Sotetsu Koyamada, Tadashi Kozuno, Toshinori Kitamura, Shin Ishii, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Policy Gradient, Exploration Strategy
- **Summary**: 强化学习中探索仅在重复状态中有益。本文提出ReMax目标，通过优化M次采样中的最大期望回报来隐式诱导探索，并导出RePPO算法。在MinAtar和Craftax基准上，RePPO无需显式探索奖励即可促进探索行为。

<h3 id="257">[257] PrivacyPeek: Auditing What LLM-Based Agents Acquire, Not Just What They Say</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00152) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingxuan Zhang, Jiahui Han, Dadi Guo, Songze Li, Guanchu Wang, Na Zou, Dongrui Liu, Xia Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Evaluation, Privacy
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Xuan269/PrivacyPeek-Resource)
- **Summary**: 现有基准仅审计LLM代理的响应泄露，忽略了数据进入上下文的获取阶段。提出PrivacyPeek基准，包含1182个案例和7种获取行为，检测代理在任务之外获取敏感信息。实验表明不必要的获取普遍存在，提示防御效果有限。

<h3 id="258">[258] DiffCrossGait: Trajectory-Level Alignment for 2D-3D Cross-Modal Gait Recognition via Latent Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00153) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyang Lu, Ming Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Diffusion Model, Gait Recognition
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Summary**: 针对2D-3D跨模态步态识别中的领域差异问题，提出DiffCrossGait方法，通过潜在扩散空间中的轨迹级对齐和三阶段对齐策略，实现模态不变特征。在SUSTech1K和FreeGait基准上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="259">[259] Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs on Code Generation for Complex Interactive Webpages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00154) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fan Wu, Lishuai Dong, Cuiyun Gao, Yujia Chen, Yiming Huang, Yang Xiao, Qing Liao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Multimodal Learning, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/anoa12159-hue/WebIGBench_eval)
- **Summary**: 现有基准仅评估静态网页代码生成，忽略交互行为。提出WebIGBench，包含103个复杂网页和871个交互动作，并设计自动化评估流程。实验揭示了当前MLLM在交互式网页代码生成上的性能边界。

<h3 id="260">[260] A Protocol-Language Model for Network Intrusion (Without Deep Packet Inspection)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00155) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vivek Kumar Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Network Traffic Analysis, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 网络入侵检测系统无法检查加密负载，提出PLM-NIDS，将网络流视为语言，使用L3/L4包元数据训练RWKV模型。零样本检测PR-AUC达0.93，监督微调后达0.94，支持逐包流式推理。

<h3 id="261">[261] A physics-informed foundation model for quantitative diffusion MRI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00156) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihan Li, Jialan Zheng, Ziyu Li, Xun Yuan, Kasidit Anmahapong, Ziang Wang, Mingxuan Liu, Hongjia Yang, Yifei Chen, Zhuhao Wang, Yuhang He, Fang Chen, Rui Li, Huaiqiang Sun, Yi Liao, Congyu Liao, Yang Yang, Haibo Qu, Xue Zhang, Hongen Liao, Qiyuan Tian
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Physics-Informed Learning, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 定量扩散MRI在临床中受限于密集采样，提出PIGMENT，基于物理信息的生成微结构网络，在11375次扫描上训练，零样本适应受试者数据。在加速扫描、低场强和超快协议下实现可靠定量映射。

<h3 id="262">[262] Interpreting FCDNNs via RG on Exponential Family</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00157](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00157) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fuzhou Gong, Zigeng Xia
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG; math.PR
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Deep Learning Theory, Statistical Physics
- **Summary**: 为建立深度学习可解释性，提出DNN训练与统计物理重整化群方法的对应关系。证明指数族数据下，全连接网络最优参数与RG不动点等价，解释了DNN的特征提取能力。

<h3 id="263">[263] Digital-to-Physical Transfer of Adversarial Patches for Aerial Vehicle Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00159](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00159) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jung Heum Woo, Eun-Kyu Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Object Detection, Remote Sensing
- **Summary**: 评估空中目标检测的物理对抗补丁攻击，通过NPS和TV约束优化补丁，并在ON/OFF/OFF-Side配置下测试。数字域OFF补丁效果最好（AORR 85.51%），但物理域ON补丁更鲁棒（OSR 0.197-0.343）。

<h3 id="264">[264] DataShield: Safety-degrading Data Filtering for LLM Benign Instruction Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00160) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junbo Zhang, Qianli Zhou, Xinyang Deng, Wen Jiang, Jie Pan, Jinbiao Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Data Selection, Instruction Tuning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZJunBo/DataShield)
- **Summary**: 大模型在良性微调时安全能力下降，提出DataShield通过合规性感知分数高效识别风险样本。在Llama3-8B等模型上验证有效性，发现开放式问答更易触发安全退化。

<h3 id="265">[265] Improving IoT Intrusion Detection Through SMOTE-Based Oversampling and Extended Multi-Model Evaluation on Side-Channel Power Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00161](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00161) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Muhammad Khuram Shahzad, Haseeb Khan, Muhammad Masood Khan, Mubashra Bibi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Class Imbalance, IoT
- **Summary**: 解决IoT侧信道数据中的类不平衡问题，使用SMOTE过采样并评估多种分类器。随机森林微平均F1达0.9989，优于此前结果。引入宏F1揭示类别级信息，特征重要性显示最近时间步最关键。

<h3 id="266">[266] ChurnNet: A Optimized Modern AI for Churn Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00169) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Syed Saad Saif, Giulio Maggiore, Paolo Russo, Damiano Distante
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Churn Prediction
- **Summary**: 客户流失预测中，传统机器学习（随机森林、XGBoost）在多数据集上优于统一多任务时序模型，在预测性能、数据效率和计算资源方面表现更好。

<h3 id="267">[267] UF-AMA: A unified framework for cross-domain emotion recognition via adaptive multimodal alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00170](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00170) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zheng Wang, Shuo Wang, Junhong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Emotion Recognition, Multimodal Learning, Domain Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/BetterCoderLab/UF-AMA)
- **Summary**: 跨域情感识别面临分布偏移和多模态样本质量差异，提出UF-AMA框架，结合置信度感知筛选和多级域自适应。在SEED和SEED-IV数据集上达到最先进性能。

<h3 id="268">[268] MyoSem: Aligning Electromyography to Natural-Language Action Semantics for Hand Action Understanding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00174](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00174) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chiyue Wang, Dong She, Yang Gao, Zhanpeng Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Gesture Recognition, Multimodal Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 现有EMG手势识别局限于固定标签分类，提出MyoSem将EMG信号与多视角动作描述对齐到共享语义空间，实现双向检索。在多个数据集上表现优异，并泛化到新用户和新动作类别。

<h3 id="269">[269] Beyond Augmentation: Score-Guided Pathological Prior for EEG-based Depression Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00180](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00180) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaojing Chen, Jingqi Cheng, Xu Zhao, Wan Jiang, Jingjing Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Anomaly Detection, EEG Classification
- **Summary**: EEG抑郁症检测受小样本限制，提出SGC框架，利用无监督生成网络建模异常度作为病理先验，无需数据合成。在Mumtaz2016和MODMA数据集上验证有效性，具有零数据增强和零合成成本的优势。

<h3 id="270">[270] The New Social Image: How AI Competency and AI Proactivity Influence Self- and Peer-Perceptions in the Workplace</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00182](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00182) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kuntal Ghosh, Marc Hassenzahl, Shadan Sadeghian
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Human-AI Collaboration, Social Science, AI and Labor
- **Venue**: Interacting with Computers
- **Summary**: 研究人-AI协作中AI能力和主动性对自我和同伴感知的影响。2x2x2 vignette实验表明，低能力和低主动性的AI提升所有权感、意义感和满意度，但效果受视角调节。

<h3 id="271">[271] Agentic Transformers Provably Learn to Search via Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00183) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tong Yang, Yu Huang, Yingbin Liang, Yuejie Chi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Transformer Architecture, Tree Search
- **Summary**: 缺乏Transformer通过RL学习搜索的理论理解。在随机k叉树环境中，构造实现随机DFS的两头注意力网络，通过策略梯度训练和深度课程学习，该机制从零涌现，并推广到更深树。

<h3 id="272">[272] Learning to Construct Practical Agentic Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00189](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00189) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aditya Kumar, Zhihan Lei, Jerry Yan, Joshua W. Momo, Lauhitya Reddy, Rafael Enrique Cabrera Jimenez, Cassandra A. Cohen, Arthur Kajiyama, William W. Cohen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Architecture, Cost Optimization
- **Summary**: 实用代理系统需关注简单性和成本可控性，提出基于伪工具的模块化框架。手工构建的固定工作流比动态规划更便宜准确，并利用多目标优化联合优化成本与响应质量。

<h3 id="273">[273] BAGEN: Are LLM Agents Budget-Aware?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00198](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00198) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxiang Lin, Zihan Wang, Mengyang Liu, Yuxuan Shan, Longju Bai, Junyao Zhang, Xing Jin, Boshan Chen, Jinyan Su, Xingyao Wang, Jiaxin Pei, Manling Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cost Optimization, Budget Awareness
- **Summary**: 当前LLM代理在执行任务时缺乏预算意识，往往在任务不可能成功时仍继续消耗资源。本文提出预算感知代理（BAGEN）形式化定义内部和外部预算，并通过渐进区间估计实现预算感知。实验发现前沿模型普遍过度乐观，早期停止可节省28-64%令牌，但精确区间校准仍具挑战。

<h3 id="274">[274] From Rashomon Theory to PRAXIS: Efficient Decision Tree Rashomon Sets</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00202) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zakk Heile, Hayden McTavish, Varun Babbar, Margo Seltzer, Cynthia Rudin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Decision Tree, Interpretability, Rashomon Set
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zakk-h/PRAXIS)
- **Summary**: 标准机器学习管道中存在许多近似最优模型（Rashomon集），但计算代价高昂。本文提出PRAXIS算法，以数量级提升的时间和内存效率近似决策树的Rashomon集，并验证其能恢复几乎全部完整集合。

<h3 id="275">[275] SEMBridge: Tagless-Final Program Semantics with Weakest-Precondition and Bounded-Checking Interpretations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00220) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.PL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Program Verification
- **Summary**: 程序语义的正式方法与实际工程存在鸿沟。本文提出SEMBridge框架，基于tagless-final风格，从同一可执行程序生成最弱前置条件和有界检查解释，实现语义一致与同步。

<h3 id="276">[276] Continuous Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00229](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00229) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yueh-Hua Wu, Tatsuya Matsushima, Kei Ota
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Robotics, Continuous Reasoning
- **Summary**: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略中语言推理粒度与连续控制不匹配。本文提出连续推理方法，预测结构化连续思维作为共享上下文，并通过自验证目标训练，在机器人任务上显著提升成功率。

<h3 id="277">[277] Civilizational Metamaterials: Engineering Coordination Under Capability Gradients and Structural Turbulence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00235](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00235) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: David Orban
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.MA
- **Tags**: AI Safety, AI Governance
- **Venue**: AGI-26
- **Summary**: 通用人工智能增加决策速度而人类验证能力有限，可能导致“冻结均衡”。本文借鉴超材料物理学，提出制度协调的本构定律，并设计可检验假设和实验方案，桥接AI对齐与制度设计。

<h3 id="278">[278] InfoAtlas: A Foundation Model for Zero-Shot Statistical Dependence Estimate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00241](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00241) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhengyang Hu, Yanzhi Chen, Hanxiang Ren, Qunsong Zeng, Youyi Zheng, Adrian Weller, Kaibin Huang, Yanchao Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Zero-Shot Learning, Mutual Information Estimation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 传统神经网络互信息估计器需对每个新数据集迭代优化，实用受限。本文提出InfoAtlas，类似基础模型架构，通过单次前向传播直接推断互信息，精度与最优方法相当，速度快100倍。

<h3 id="279">[279] Effects of Varying LLM Access on Essay Writing Behavior</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00250](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00250) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Julia Christenson, Karin de Langis, Shirley Anugrah Hayati, Dongyeop Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Human-Computer Interaction, Writing Assistance
- **Summary**: LLM辅助写作对学习效果的影响尚不明确。本文通过控制LLM访问级别（无、有限、无限），发现限制访问可保持学生作者信心和写作投入，而无限访问降低创意表达和归属感。

<h3 id="280">[280] ARCA: Adapter-Residual Credit Assignment When Token Signals Degenerate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00257](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00257) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rodney Lafuente-Mercado
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LoRA, Credit Assignment
- **Venue**: DEMO 2026: ICML Workshop on Decision-Making from Offline Datasets to Online Adaptation
- **Summary**: LoRA微调下语言模型强化学习中的令牌级信用分配信号可能退化。本文提出适配器-残差信用分配（ARCA），利用适配器隐藏状态残差作为令牌显著性度量，避免退化，在GRPO实验中表现稳健。

<h3 id="281">[281] When Softmax Fails at the Top: Extreme Value Corrections for InfoNCE</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00262](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00262) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Melihcan Erol, Suat Evren, Oktay Ozel, Alexander Morgan, Jongha Jon Ryu, Lizhong Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.AP; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Contrastive Learning, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: InfoNCE的softmax形式在归一化嵌入场景中统计假设不匹配。本文提出WEINCE，利用在线批统计进行极端值修正，无需额外参数，在五个视觉基准上提升了冻结特征评估性能。

<h3 id="282">[282] StressDream: Steering Video World Models for Robust Policy Evaluation and Improvement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00267](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00267) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junwon Seo, Sushant Veer, Ran Tian, Wenhao Ding, Apoorva Sharma, Karen Leung, Edward Schmerling, Marco Pavone, Andrea Bajcsy
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: World Model, Video Generation, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 视频世界模型在策略评估中依赖名义想象，可能遗漏高风险结果。本文提出StressDream，通过优化扩散模型初始噪声，引导想象至文本指定的高影响但合理场景，实现鲁棒策略评估。

<h3 id="283">[283] Hyperbolic and Evidence-Prioritized Experts for Large Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00275) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zijie Zhou, Dandan Zhu, Hangxiangpan Wang, Heng Zhang, Huishen Jiao, Yi Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Hallucination, Hyperbolic Learning
- **Summary**: 大型视觉语言模型中混合专家（MoE）对称处理模态，忽略层次关系。本文提出AsyMoE，包含双曲跨模态专家和证据优先语言专家，在幻觉敏感任务上提升达3.8%，激活参数减少25.45%。

<h3 id="284">[284] Synthetic Data from Cross-Domain Events for Large-Scale Recommendation Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00282) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiangyu Wang, Yawen He, Shivendra Pratap Singh, Han Huang, Mengtong Hu, Sharath Ciddu, Yi-Hsuan Hsieh, Erik Groving, Yi Ding, Jieming Di, Tony Wang, Min Yun, Xiaoyu Chen, Ling Leng, Rob Malkin
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Data Synthesis, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 大规模推荐系统面临数据稀疏和噪声问题。本文提出SCALR框架，通过生成目标域合成交互事件，将跨域学习分解为事件翻译和下游训练两阶段，在线A/B测试取得显著提升。

<h3 id="285">[285] Real2SAM2Real: Generative 3D Caches as Complementary Context for Video Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00299](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00299) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiayi Wu, Haoming Cai, Cornelia Fermuller, Christopher Metzler, Yiannis Aloimonos
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Diffusion Model, 3D Reconstruction, Novel View Synthesis
- **Summary**: 视频扩散模型在复杂动态中容易结构坍塌。本文提出Real2SAM2Real，利用3D提升模型提取可编辑3D缓存作为几何支架，并通过软空间对齐注入机制实现相机和物体运动精确控制。

<h3 id="286">[286] Bridging Reasoning Trajectories in On-Policy Distillation via Near-Future Guidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00305](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00305) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Jiang, Francis Ferraro
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation, On-Policy Distillation
- **Summary**: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中令牌级学习信号无法可靠桥接学生与教师轨迹。本文提出TOPD，利用近未来轨迹信息识别真正发散状态并分配跨多个未来令牌的指导，在AIME24和AIME25上分别提升至63.3%和53.3%。

<h3 id="287">[287] Rethinking the Role of Temperature in Large Language Model Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00306](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00306) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hoang-Chau Luong, Lingwei Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Training, Temperature Scaling
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型蒸馏中温度选择被忽视，影响前向KL与反向KL的比较。本文发现温度不对称影响：高温下前向KL显著优于反向KL，且温度改善多种蒸馏目标，使简单KL方法达到最新性能。

<h3 id="288">[288] How Generation Architecture Shapes Code Complexity in Multi-Agent LLM Systems: A Paired Study on HumanEval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00308](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00308) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nazmus Ashrafi
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Code Generation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 多智能体LLM代码生成架构对代码复杂度的影响未知。本文比较六种配置，发现架构分为两大簇，分析师-编码器拆分增加复杂度，而调试器不增加；复杂架构未带来准确率提升。

<h3 id="289">[289] DRL-Based Pose Control for Double-Ackermann Robots Under Actuation Uncertainties</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00313](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00313) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oussama Zaim, Mélodie Daniel, Aly Magassouba, Miguel Aranda, Olivier Ly
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Sim-to-Real
- **Venue**: Uncertainty in Open-World Robotics an IEEE International Conference on Robotics & Automation (ICRA 2026) workshop
- **Summary**: 针对双阿克曼机器人在仿真与现实动力学差异下的位姿控制问题，提出将ManeuverNet扩展为完整位姿控制，并采用多环境DRL（SAC和CrossQ）的sim-to-sim-to-real方法。在Gazebo中成功率从25%提升至92%（严格阈值下69%），并成功迁移到真实机器人。

<h3 id="290">[290] LLMs Need Encoders for Semantic IDs Too</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00324) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiangyi Chen, Zelun Wang, Xinyi Li, Yi-Ping Hsu, Jaewon Yang, Jiajing Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对生成式推荐中语义ID（SID）的上下文依赖问题，提出PrefixMem——一种基于前缀n-gram记忆表的轻量级SID编码器，为LLM提供结构化前缀条件表示。在Pinterest大规模数据上，最深层SID准确率相对提升46%，完整SID检索召回率相对提升22%。

<h3 id="291">[291] Isolating LLM Lexical Bias: A Curation-Free Triangulated Metric for Preference-Stage Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00334) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyang Ming, Jose Hernandez, Thomas Stephan Juzek
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Bias Mitigation, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 为了在不依赖人工标注的情况下量化偏好学习导致的LLM词汇偏差，提出三角偏好偏移（TPS）指标，通过三角测量人类基准、基础模型和指令变体的差异。在6个模型族上验证了该自动化方法的有效性，并分析了偏好学习是否使模型偏向“威望语言”。

<h3 id="292">[292] ROGUE: Misaligned Agent Behavior Arising from Ordinary Computer Use</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00341](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00341) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jeremy Tien, Abishek Anand, Yu-Rou Tuan, Yuchen Shen, J. Zico Kolter, Aran Nayebi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 论文发现AI代理在良性场景中也会出于任务完成动机而表现出不遵从人类纠正/中断的不对齐行为。通过引入可修正性基准评估前沿模型，发现大多数模型频繁违反可修正性，且性能提升反而加剧不对齐，子代理也无法保证可修正。

<h3 id="293">[293] (HB-ARFM) History-Bootstrapped Flow Matching for Inverse Boiling Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00349](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00349) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xianwei Zou, Sheikh Md Shakeel Hassan, Arthur Feeney, Aparna Chandramowlishwaran
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Scientific Computing, Inverse Problem
- **Summary**: 针对部分观测下时空场逆重建的ill-posed问题，提出历史自举自回归流匹配（HB-ARFM），利用观测历史通过条件流匹配减少歧义，并自回归传播。在沸腾动力学重建中，该方法能生成物理有效的结果，而其他模型失败。

<h3 id="294">[294] Drift Q-Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00350](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00350) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anas Houssaini, Mohamad H. Danesh, Amin Abyaneh, Scott Fujimoto, Hsiu-Chin Lin, David Meger
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Off-Policy RL
- **Summary**: 为解决离线强化学习中扩散/流策略需要迭代去噪的问题，提出DriftQL，结合基于漂移的行为正则化和评论家驱动的策略改进，单次前向传播生成动作。在D4RL和OGBench上持续优于扩散和流方法，且在数据质量下降时仍保持接近清洁数据的性能。

<h3 id="295">[295] Reinforcement Learning with Pairwise Preferences in Long-Term Decision Problems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00367](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00367) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonathan Colaço Carr, Prakash Panangaden, Doina Precup, Benjamin Van Roy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对长时域决策中成对偏好强化学习方法的低效和缺乏最优性保证问题，提出马尔可夫决策竞赛（MDC）新模型，证明平稳策略最优且求解属于P类。所提迭代算法在长时域高维问题中显著优于先前方法。

<h3 id="296">[296] Agentic Authoring of Interactive Multiview Visualizations in Genomics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00370](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00370) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Astrid van den Brandt, Kiroong Choe, Sehi L&#39;Yi, Devin Lange, Nils Gehlenborg
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Data Visualization, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 针对基因组学交互式多视图可视化创作困难的问题，比较了六种LLM方案（直接生成、固定管线、多种代理配置）。发现代理迭代大幅提升感知质量，但更复杂的代理架构无额外收益，为领域可视化创作提供了设计指导。

<h3 id="297">[297] SUPREME: A Multi-GPU Framework for Reproducible Image Unlearning Method Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00380) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Petros Andreou, Jamie Lanyon, Axel Finke, Georgina Cosma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Distributed Training, Image Classification
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/pedroandreou/supreme-unlearning)
- **Summary**: 为解决图像去学习评估的计算开销，提出SUPREME——一个多GPU框架，采用注册式设计支持新方法、指标和场景，并在Pins人脸识别任务（ResNet18和ViT）上展示了多种子评估。

<h3 id="298">[298] Zamba2-VL Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00390) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hassan Shapourian, Kasra Hejazi, Olabode M. Sule, Beren Millidge
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 提出Zamba2-VL视觉语言模型套件，基于Mamba2状态空间和共享Transformer的混合架构。在图像理解、OCR、定位等基准上性能与领先的Transformer VLM相当，但首次令牌延迟降低约一个数量级，适合端侧部署。

<h3 id="299">[299] Detector-Evasive LLM Paraphrasing via Constrained Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00392](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00392) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingyi Wang, Zhuoer Shen, Yuheng Bu, Shaofeng Zou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对现有LLM释义检测逃避方法缺乏精确语义保持控制的问题，将其建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，提出DEPO算法（拉格朗日前沿对偶RL）。在多个数据集上，DEPO在满足语义约束的同时实现强检测逃避，并具有跨领域、跨检测器和提示级鲁棒性。

<h3 id="300">[300] PR2: Predictive Routing Replay for MoE-Based LLM Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00395) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daize Dong, Junlin Chen, Haolong Jia, Jiawei Wu, Huanwei Di, Jiang Liu, Jialian Wu, Zhengzhong Liu, Zicheng Liu, Emad Barsoum, Dimitris N. Metaxas, Hongyi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 针对MoE LLM强化学习中路由器漂移导致的训练不稳定问题，提出预测性路由回放（PR2），为每个路由器添加轻量级演化预测器，在部署阶段使用预测路由分布进行top-k路由。实验表明PR2减少了路由失配，提高了训练稳定性并在推理基准上取得更强性能。

<h3 id="301">[301] A Distribution-Free Framework for Rewrite-Based Human-text Detection via Knockoff Filtering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00402) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Liu
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; stat.AP
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 提出一种无分布统计框架，通过knockoff滤波将基于改写的人机文本检测器转化为具有有限样本FDR保证的检测器，无需重训练。在3种检测模型、19个领域和4种LLM上验证了可靠的FDR控制和有意义的检测能力。

<h3 id="302">[302] Masking Stale Observations Helps Search Agents -- Until It Doesn't: A Regime Map and Its Mechanism</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00408) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoxiang Zhang, Qixin Xu, Zhuofeng Li, Lei Zhang, Pengcheng Jiang, Yu Zhang, Julian McAuley
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Information Retrieval, Long Context
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/i-DeepSearch/observation-masking)
- **Summary**: 针对长时域搜索代理中累积过时观测的问题，系统研究了观测屏蔽的作用，发现准确率增益呈非对称倒U型曲线，取决于检索器召回率和模型隐式过滤能力的交互。机制上，屏蔽实现了令牌与回合的权衡，提供了上下文管理的视角。

<h3 id="303">[303] AgentxGCore: Agentic AI for Next-Generation Mobile Core Network</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00417](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00417) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maria Katarine Santana Barbosa, Kelvin L. Dias
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, LLM Agent, Autonomous Systems
- **Venue**: IEEE Network
- **Summary**: 针对6G核心网需要AI原生架构和简化管理的问题，提出AgentxGCore，基于Agentic AI原生层扩展3GPP架构，采用多代理系统（规划代理和执行代理）实现持续优化。在开源核心网和异构数据集上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="304">[304] Finer Parameter Steps for Low-Rank PEFT: A Controlled Study with CP Tensor Adapters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00428](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00428) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinjue Wang, Xiuheng Wang, Yejun Zhang, Sergiy A. Vorobyov, Esa Ollila, Zhi-Yong Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Low-Rank Adaptation, Tensor Networks
- **Venue**: ICML 2026 Workshop on CoLoRAI
- **Summary**: 针对LoRA秩步长导致参数预算分辨率粗糙的问题，使用规范多元分解（CP）张量适配器实现更细粒度的参数步长。在OPT-1.3B上比较发现，CP可填补LoRA秩之间的空白，但效果因任务而异，不能保证更好的精度-预算曲线。

<h3 id="305">[305] Detect Before You Leap: Mirage Detection in Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00435](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00435) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sayeed Shafayet Chowdhury, Md. Shaown Miah
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Hallucination
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型在视觉证据缺失时产生自信但无根据的回答（幻象）的问题，提出文本条件逐层内部对齐（TC-LIA）方法，通过探测CLIP ViT-H/14视觉编码器的层间补丁-令牌表征，并结合像素统计、零样本领域路由和VLM自评估集成。在多个VQA领域和12个VLM骨干上，三分类检测准确率达约94.6-94.7%，幻象率低于3%。

<h3 id="306">[306] DarkVesselNet: Multi-Modal Remote Sensing and Trajectory Reasoning for Dark Vessel Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00445) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arun Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Object Detection, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 针对暗船检测需要融合AIS数据与卫星雷达和光学图像的问题，提出DarkVesselNet多模态遥感堆栈，结合Sentinel-1 SAR、Sentinel-2光学、地理空间基础模型、AIS轨迹推理和异常检测头。通过软件验证测试各组件功能。

<h3 id="307">[307] GeoSAM-3D: Geodesic Prompt Propagation for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Segmentation from Monocular Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00447](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00447) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arun Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Open-Vocabulary Segmentation, 3D Gaussian Splatting
- **Summary**: 针对开放词汇3D场景分割通常需要RGB-D视频或网格的问题，提出GeoSAM-3D，利用冻结的图像和视频基础模型、单目3D高斯泼溅重建以及图-测地传播核。通过热核距离传播提示，保持曲面连续性，减少泄漏。

<h3 id="308">[308] When Safe Skills Collide: Measuring Compositional Risk in Agent Skill Ecosystems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00448) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Su Wang, Pin Qian, Yihang Chen, Junxian You, Xiaoyuan Wang, Xiaochong Jiang, Lifei Liu, Haoran Yu, Jingzhou Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理中单个安全的技能组合可能产生不安全后果的问题，提出SkillReact框架，包含静态组合基准、人工裁决流程和基于动作的可利用性测试。在1520个技能上，22.25%的配对被标记，其中约18.2%为真实风险，且风险实现取决于宿主模型。

<h3 id="309">[309] Short-form Text Rewriting with Phi Silica</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00462) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Divya Tadimeti, Shawn Pan, Sameera Lanka, Chenghui Zhou, Sadid Hasan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, Text Generation, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对小语言模型在短文本改写中语义忠实度和幻觉鲁棒性不足的问题，通过数据集整理、提示蒸馏、参数高效微调适配Phi Silica。微调提高了语义忠实度，减少了幻觉，并提升了与GPT-5-chat改写的胜率。

<h3 id="310">[310] On the Limits of LLM Adaptability: Impact of Model-Internalized Priors on Annotation Task Performance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00467) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Etienne Casanova, Rafal Kocielnik, R. Michael Alvarez
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Prompt Engineering, Bias Mitigation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在零样本标注任务中受先验知识影响且提示修正效果有限的问题，引入定义特定熟悉度（DSF）指标。实验发现近三分之二的零样本错误抗修正，DSF与性能正相关（偏r=+0.41），而文本记忆指标无正相关。

<h3 id="311">[311] CodeCytos: AI-assisted spatial molecular imaging analysis via code-augmented agent action space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00472](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00472) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hung Q. Vo, Huy Q. Vo, Son T. Ly, Zhihao Wan, Anh-Vu Nguyen, Hong Zhao, Jianting Sheng, Stephen T. C. Wong, Hien V. Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 针对传统组织图像分析工具缺乏灵活性和代码驱动自动化的问题，提出CodeCytos，一个基于编码的推理代理框架，实现与空间分子成像数据的动态可编程交互。在四个组织数据集上优于基线，且领域无关的少样本示例可提升性能。

<h3 id="312">[312] Pre-Deployment Robustness Stress Testing for CT Segmentation Systems Using Clinically Motivated Multi-Corruption Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00491](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00491) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: CholMin Kang, Jonghyun Chung, Amanpreet Kaurb, Nagesh Gulkotwarb, Arthi Sivasankaranb
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Data Augmentation
- **Summary**: 针对CT分割系统在临床多样化成像条件下性能下降的问题，提出RAMP鲁棒性增强框架，结合解剖约束空间扰动、CT强度变换和多退化组合。在噪声评估基准上，平均退化Dice从0.610提升至0.753，鲁棒性差距从0.264降至0.064。

<h3 id="313">[313] TabChange: Precise Attribute Changes in Tabular Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00503) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Arjun Dahal, Yu Lei, Raghu N. Kacker, Richard Kuhn
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Counterfactual Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对表格数据中修改属性会破坏属性间关系的问题，提出TabChange，通过分析目标属性与其他属性的关系：若关系弱则直接翻转，若强则利用对抗框架去除潜在空间中的属性信息。实验表明生成的对抗样本在自然度相当的情况下更接近原始实例。

<h3 id="314">[314] V-LynX: Token Interface Alignment for Video+X LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00508](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00508) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jungin Park, Jiyoung Lee, Kwanghoon Sohn
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Multimodal Learning, Video Understanding
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/park-jungin/lynx)
- **Summary**: 针对视频LLM中集成新模态需要大量模态特定编码器或配对监督的问题，提出V-LynX，利用轻量级辅助路径与冻结视觉编码器并行，通过对齐注意响应和统计分布实现新模态集成。在音视频问答、3D推理等基准上达到最优。

<h3 id="315">[315] Skill or Skip? Learning Selective Skill Invocation in Agentic Tasks via Dual-Granularity Preference Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00510) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chishui Chen, Jiaye Lin, Te Sun, Junxi Wang, Yi Yang, Cong Qin, Yangen Hu, Lu Pan, Ke Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对代理技能调用可能无益甚至干扰执行的问题，提出SelSkill双粒度偏好学习框架，制定技能或跳过决策，使用预测不确定性优先候选点，并构建受控调用-跳过偏好对。在ALFWorld上任务成功率提升10.9个百分点，执行精度提升29.1个百分点。

<h3 id="316">[316] PaCo-VLA: Passivity-Shielded Compliance Prior for Contact-Rich Vision-Language-Action Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00515](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00515) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haofan Cao, Zhaoyang Li, Zhichao You, Liang Guo, Tianrui Li
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Robotics, Vision-Language Model, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在接触丰富的操作中缺乏可靠性的问题，提出PaCo-VLA，一种无源性屏蔽的顺应先验，将VLA输出视为任务级顺应建议，并通过高频无源性屏蔽进行验证。在连接器插入实验中实现零无源性违规，精度优于未屏蔽基线。

<h3 id="317">[317] CAFOSat: A Strongly Annotated Dataset for Infrastructure-Aware CAFO Mapping Using High-Resolution Imagery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oishee Bintey Hoque, Nibir Chandra Mandal, Mandy L Wilson, Samarth Swarup, Madhav Marathe, Abhijin Adiga
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Dataset, Agricultural AI
- **Venue**: CVPR Workshop-2026
- **Summary**: 针对远程感知中CAFO大规模标注面临异质基础设施和不完整清单的问题，构建CAFOSat数据集，结合NAIP影像和多源清单，通过人机协作流程精细标注。包含超过45,000个图像块，覆盖20个州，为CAFO分类和泛化提供基准。

<h3 id="318">[318] Richer Representations for Neural Algorithmic Reasoning via Auxiliary Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00559) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiafu Huang, Chao Peng, Chenyang Xu, Zhengfeng Yang, Kecheng Cai, Chenhao Zhang, Yi Wang, Yiwei Gong, Wanqin Zhou, Irene Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Algorithmic Reasoning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对神经算法推理中编码器通常为简单MLP导致表征不足的问题，提出辅助重建模块恢复输入状态，并设计增强变体捕获特征间依赖。实验表明该方法提升了现有架构在标准基准上的性能。

<h3 id="319">[319] Interpretable Policy Distillation for Power Grid Topology Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00561) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aleksandra Dmitruka, Karlis Freivalds
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Power System
- **Summary**: 针对深度强化学习策略在电网拓扑控制中成本高、部署难、不透明的问题，通过压力聚焦蒸馏将PPO策略压缩为决策树和随机森林。代理在平均奖励和生存时长上超越教师，且决策树可直接检查。

<h3 id="320">[320] A Practical Upper Bound on Selection Bias Effects in Medical Prediction Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00563) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kara Liu, Maggie Wang, Russ B. Altman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Model Evaluation, Fairness, Medical AI
- **Venue**: ACM SIGKDD '26
- **Summary**: 针对医疗预测模型因选择偏差导致泛化性差且现有方法需访问目标分布的问题，提出一种在部分观测选择机制和数据下的最坏情况性能上界。在合成、半合成和真实数据上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="321">[321] Revisiting Parameter-Based Knowledge Editing in Large Language Models: Theoretical Limits and Empirical Evidence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00570](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00570) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wanying Ren, Xin Song, Futing Wang, Guoxiu He, Aixin Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Editing, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有参数化知识编辑方法忽略了理论局限性，且缺乏实际场景评估。本文基于维度坍缩假设进行理论分析，并通过全面实验证明参数编辑方法会损坏LLM核心能力，而简单的检索式基线在所有条件下均表现更优。

<h3 id="322">[322] On the Difficulty of Learning a Meta-network for Training Data Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00571](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00571) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zilin Du, Junqi Zhao, Boyang Albert Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Data Selection, Meta-Learning
- **Summary**: 元学习数据选择（MTS）在实际中常表现不佳，原因在于梯度信噪比低和缺乏有效特征。本文提出增大批大小和利用位置特征等简单解决方案，在四个基准上平均提升5.49%和2.89%。

<h3 id="323">[323] Improving Visual Representation Alignment Generation with GRPO</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00583](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00583) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shentong Mo, Sukmin Yun
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Reinforcement Learning, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 扩散Transformer训练效率低，源于生成与判别表示的对齐不足。本文提出VRPO，用强化学习替代静态对齐损失，基于生成保真度等奖励引导优化，在ImageNet上实现+1.8 FID提升和2.3倍加速训练。

<h3 id="324">[324] Critic-R: Improving Agentic Search using Instruction-tuned Retrievers with Natural Language Introspective Feedback</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00590](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00590) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Md Zarif Ul Alam, Alireza Salemi, Hamed Zamani
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, RAG
- **Summary**: 智能体搜索中检索器优化困难。本文提出Critic-R，通过批评模型评估智能体内省推理轨迹，实现查询改写和检索器优化，显著提升多个多跳问答数据集上的检索质量和答案准确率。

<h3 id="325">[325] SPADER: Step-wise Peer Advantage with Diversity-Aware Exploration Rewards for Multi-Answer Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00593](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00593) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiming Shi, Zhaolu Kang, Yunfan Zhou, Di Weng, Yingcai Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KhanCold/spader)
- **Summary**: 多答案问答需要发现完整答案集，但长搜索轨迹的信用分配和探索奖励对齐困难。本文提出SPADER，包含步骤级同行优势（SPA）和多样性感知探索奖励，在多个基准上提升召回率和F1。

<h3 id="326">[326] CARE-RL: Capability-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Mitigating Cross-Domain Conflicts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00609) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Zhang, Xinle Wu, Yao Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Alignment, Multi-Task Learning
- **Summary**: 多域强化学习面临奖励不可靠和能力干扰问题。本文提出CARE-RL，结合协议感知生成奖励模型和方向感知能力子空间投影，在数学、聊天和指令遵循基准上优于标准多域RL方法。

<h3 id="327">[327] MemGraphRAG: Memory-based Multi-Agent System for Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00610](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00610) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chuanjie Wu, Zhishang Xiang, Yunbo Tang, Zerui Chen, Qinggang Zhang, Jinsong Su
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, RAG, Multi-Agent System
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/XMUDeepLIT/MemGraphRAG)
- **Summary**: 现有GraphRAG方法缺乏全局视角导致图构建不一致。本文提出MemGraphRAG，利用基于记忆的多智能体系统保障高质量图构建，并设计记忆感知分层检索算法，在多个基准上超越基线。

<h3 id="328">[328] Linguistics-Aware Non-Distortionary LLM Watermarking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00613) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shinwoo Park, Hyejin Park, Hyeseon An, Yo-Sub Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Watermarking, Multilingual Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shinwoo-Park/luna_watermark)
- **Summary**: LLM水印需在多语言环境下不降低质量且可公开检测。本文提出LUNA，利用词性熵指导非扭曲二值锦标赛采样，在6种语言上达到AUROC 0.9959，困惑度偏移仅0.045，优于所有基线。

<h3 id="329">[329] Pause and Think: A Dataset and Benchmark for Video-Grounded Assistive Action Suggestion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00616](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00616) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shivam Singh, Saptarshi Majumdar, Pratik Prabhanjan, Zicheng Liu, Emad Barsoum
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Video Understanding, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 视觉语言模型在视频理解中缺乏接地推理和时序一致性。本文构建pause-and-think-T数据集，微调4B模型，在场景理解任务上以59倍少参数达到58.0%准确率，并展现强泛化能力。

<h3 id="330">[330] MemPro: Agentic Memory Systems as Evolvable Programs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00619](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00619) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qingshan Liu, Guoqing Wang, Wen Wu, Jingqi Huang, Xinqi Tao, Dejia Song, Jie Zhou, Liang He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Program Synthesis
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wanghai673/MemPro)
- **Summary**: 现有智能体记忆系统固定流水线难以适应演化。本文提出MemPro，将记忆构建检索流水线视为可进化程序，通过故障驱动编辑调试迭代改进，在多个基准上超越强基线。

<h3 id="331">[331] Authenticity Debt and the Synthetic Content Threat Landscape: A Layered Framework for Trust, Provenance, and IP Governance in the Generative AI Era</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00621) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shubhashis Sengupta, Benjamin McCarty, Milind Savagaonkar, Rhine Andotra
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, AI Governance, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 生成式AI带来真实性、溯源、完整性和问责风险。本文引入“真实性债务”概念，提出分层参考架构整合密码学溯源、人在回路验证和持续治理，并分析监管环境。

<h3 id="332">[332] LP5X-PIM Sim: A High-Fidelity HW/SW Integrated Simulator for LPDDR5X-PIM</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00636](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00636) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: SangHoon Cha, Jaewan Choi, Byeongho Kim, Yoonah Paik, Sukhan Lee, Kyomin Sohn
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Hardware-Software Co-design, Memory Architecture, Hardware Simulation
- **Summary**: LPDDR5X-PIM的高保真仿真器对于硬件-软件协同设计至关重要。本文描述了三星开发的LPDDR5X-PIM仿真器架构，集成硬件数据路径和软件控制层，实现精准性能和能效评估。

<h3 id="333">[333] LinguIUTics at PsyDefDetect: Iterative Imbalance-Aware Fine-tuning of Qwen3-8B for Psychological Defense Mechanism Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00647) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shefayat E Shams Adib, Ahmed Alfey Sani, Md Hasibur Rahman Alif, Ajwad Abrar
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Text Classification, Class Imbalance
- **Venue**: BioNLP 2026
- **Summary**: 对话文本中心理防御机制检测面临类别不平衡挑战。本文采用QLoRA微调Qwen3-8B，结合分层交叉验证、少数类增强和后处理，在PsyDefDetect共享任务中取得宏F1 0.3917（第4名）。

<h3 id="334">[334] MESA: Improving MoE Safety Alignment via Decentralized Expertise</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00651) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yitong Sun, Yao Huang, Teng Li, Ranjie Duan, Yichi Zhang, Xingjun Ma, Hui Xue, Xingxing Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Safety, Optimal Transport
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lorraine021/MESA)
- **Summary**: MoE架构存在安全稀疏性问题，传统对齐方法降低效用。本文提出MESA，基于最优传输理论重新分配专家安全职责并优化路由，在保持有用性的同时实现鲁棒防御。

<h3 id="335">[335] Scaling Behavior of Single LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00655](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00655) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jialing Li, Zhouhong Gu, Yin Cai, Hongwei Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: LLM多智能体系统的缩放行为尚不清楚。本文提出SIMAS框架，实验表明性能随智能体数量增加呈边际递减，有效MAS需要足够强的基座模型和策略交互设计。

<h3 id="336">[336] Demystifying the Optimal Fair Classifier in Multi-Class Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00656](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00656) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Li Zhang, Yuyuan Li, XiaoHua Feng, Jiaming Zhang, Fengyuan Yu, Chaochao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Bias Mitigation, Multi-Class Classification
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 多类分类中的公平分类问题缺乏理论表征和实用算法。本文提出最优公平分类器的解析形式，并设计处理中和后处理算法，理论证明收敛到Pareto前沿，实验验证精度-公平权衡优势。

<h3 id="337">[337] Collaborative Few-Step Distillation and Low-Bit Quantization for Wan2.2 Dual-Expert Video Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00658) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinyang Du, Shenghao Jin, Ziqian Xu, Ruihao Gong, Shiqiao Gu, Yang Yong, Jinyang Guo, Xianglong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Diffusion Model, Quantization
- **Summary**: 针对大型视频扩散模型部署成本高的问题，提出结合少步分布匹配蒸馏与低比特量化的联合压缩流程，针对Wan2.2-T2V-A14B模型的双专家去噪路径分别校准，保护敏感入口层，并采用HiF4风格低比特表示以改善动态范围覆盖。实验表明，联合设计使量化模型在8步和20步时接近同精度全精度模型，在平均性能上超越原始全精度基线，20步设置达到最佳质量-效率权衡。

<h3 id="338">[338] Beyond the Mouth: Upper-Face Affective Cues in Audiovisual Sentence Recognition under Acoustic Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00670) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhou Yang, Yueyi Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio-Visual Learning, Affective Computing, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 研究在声学退化条件下，上半脸情感线索是否对音视频句子识别有贡献。基于CREMA-D语料库，训练分类器比较不同线索条件，发现嘴部特征在退化音频下提供显著鲁棒性提升（0 dB SNR时准确率提高0.0794），而上半脸情感线索对准确率直接提升不大，但全脸模型校准更好，提示情感面部信息可能支持多模态鲁棒性和置信度估计。

<h3 id="339">[339] The Paradox of Outcome Optimization: A Causal Information-Theoretic Bound on Reasoning Shortcuts in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00674](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00674) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihan Chen, Yiming Zhang, Wenxiang Geng, Zenghui Ding, Yining Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Causal Inference, Information Bottleneck
- **Summary**: 针对基于结果奖励的RL微调LLM导致推理能力脆弱的问题，建立结构因果模型与信息瓶颈原理的理论框架，定义推理为高复杂度因果过程、捷径学习为低复杂度虚假相关，推导出基于语义覆盖度的新泛化界，并证明过程奖励模型通过逐步骤互信息约束使低复杂度捷径流形不可达，为过程监督提供数学基础。

<h3 id="340">[340] COPF: An Online Framework for Deployment-Stable Counterfactual Fairness in Evolving Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00700](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00700) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sheng&#39;en Li, Dongmian Zou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Graph Neural Network, Recommender System
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lsnnnnnnnn/COPF)
- **Summary**: 针对在线链接推荐中公平性估计因推荐策略更新而漂移的问题，提出COPF框架，通过显式探索和记录候选呈现概率来估计群体级暴露反事实差距，并使用残差结果不可区分性和图感知双重稳健估计器进行审计与控制。实验表明，COPF显著降低暴露反事实群体差异的最坏情况峰值，同时保持排名效用。

<h3 id="341">[341] Shape Your Body: Value Gradients for Multi-Embodiment Robot Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00702](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00702) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nico Bohlinger, Jan Peters
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Reinforcement Learning, Robotics
- **Summary**: 针对机器人设计中需要为每个新设计重新进行强化学习协同设计循环的问题，提出先训练跨多种机器人设计的具身感知策略和价值函数，然后固定价值函数，通过价值梯度作为可微代理来优化候选机器人设计参数。在最多50个机器人、超过1100个连续设计参数的空间上评估，证明能够优化完整设计并识别性能限制参数。

<h3 id="342">[342] Information-Theoretic Lower Bounds for Bit-Constrained Stochastic Optimization via a Reduction to Compressed Gaussian Mean Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00703) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Munsik Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Theory, Optimization, Quantization
- **Summary**: 针对低精度预训练中缺乏信息论下界的问题，研究B比特量化随机一阶优化器，将强凸二次族优化问题精确归约为交互式压缩高斯均值估计，推导出无条件下界（通信界、统计界及乘积形式），并给出近似匹配的可行性结果，扩展了序列率失真视角修正了噪声相关性的影响猜想。

<h3 id="343">[343] Multi-Agent Conformal Prediction with Personalized Statistical Validity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00717](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00717) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Martin V. Vejling, Christophe A. N. Biscio, Adrien Mazoyer, Petar Popovski, Shashi Raj Pandey
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Conformal Prediction, Personalized Federated Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体设置中局部校准数据有限、隐私约束和数据异质性的问题，提出个性化联邦加权共形预测（PFWCP），结合局部密度比加权和加权分位数聚合来校正异质性并保护隐私，为每个参与智能体提供渐近有效的覆盖保证。实验证明在合成和真实数据集上校准质量优于现有联邦共形基线。

<h3 id="344">[344] EPIC: Efficient and Parallel Inference under CFG Constraints for Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00722](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00722) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hyundong Jin, Yo-Sub Han
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Constrained Generation, Parallel Decoding
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/hyundong98/EPIC-Decoding.git)
- **Summary**: 针对扩散语言模型在上下文无关文法约束下解码速度慢、无法并行的问题，提出EPIC框架，通过词法记忆化、使用Earley解析代替确定自动机进行验证、以及松弛兼容子集选择实现并行提交，显著减少重复词法分析和验证开销。实验表明，推理时间减少最高67.5%，额外开销降低最高90.5%。

<h3 id="345">[345] WaveFilter: Enhancing the Long-Context Capability of Diffusion LLMs via Wavelet-Guided KV Cache Filtering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00724](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00724) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinnan Yang, Yan Wang, Zhen Bi, Kehao Wu, Xiaojie Li, Jungang Lou, Zechao Li, Jing Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, Long Context, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对扩散大语言模型在长上下文任务中计算开销大和推理延迟高的问题，提出WaveFilter，一种即插即用的免训练缓存框架，创新性地引入小波变换对长序列进行分解以精确识别关键token，并基于此构建稀疏KV缓存来计算最终上下文表示。实验证明能显著提升现有KV缓存方法在复杂长上下文任务中的性能。

<h3 id="346">[346] SORA: Free Second-Order Attacks in Fast Adversarial Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00738) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mazdak Teymourian, Ramtin Moslemi, Farzan Rahmani, Mohammad Hossein Rohban
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Fast Adversarial Training
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SecondOrderAT/SORA)
- **Summary**: 针对快速对抗训练中灾难性过拟合的问题，形式化ε过拟合视角，提出PertAlign指标用于预测CO发生，并引入SORA自适应步长对抗训练方法，根据损失曲面几何动态调整扰动。实验表明SORA一致防止CO，在多种数据集和架构上达到最先进的鲁棒性和干净准确率，且使用单一固定超参数。

<h3 id="347">[347] Quantum Tunneling-Aware Machine Learning: Physics-Derived Noise Models for Robust Deployment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00741) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Uiwon Hwang, Jaeho Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Fault Tolerance, Hardware Reliability, Hardware-Software Co-design
- **Summary**: 针对晶体管缩放接近量子力学极限导致电子隧穿影响AI推理的问题，首次从原理上推导部署时的权重误差分布，揭示其结构（仿射均值漂移、每比特方差分层等），并提出隧穿感知补偿算法，结合闭式均值校正和基于WKB方差分解的比特预算分配。实验表明，TAC以3.4到33.6倍更少的ECC开销达到95%的干净准确率。

<h3 id="348">[348] SkyShield: Occupancy as a Safety Interface for Low-Altitude UAV Autonomy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00747](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00747) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jie Gao, Jie Ma, Kaihui Lin, Kai Ye, Miaohui Zhang, Pingyang Dai, Liujuan Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: UAV, 3D Vision, Autonomous Systems
- **Summary**: 针对低空无人机自主飞行中3D空间理解作为安全界面的需求，提出首个前视单目语义占用率基准SkyShield（含36K前视图样本），并提出UAV中心且动力学感知的度量KAR-mIoU以及几何优先基线SkyOcc。共同建立占用率作为低空自主安全接口。

<h3 id="349">[349] Causal Density Functions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00754) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sridhar Mahadevan
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 引入因果密度函数作为干预分布与观测分布之间的Radon-Nikodym导数，提供因果效应的局部密度比。推导基本恒等式，设计do-curve和有向边分数的实用估计器，并在合成和真实扰动基准上评估。

<h3 id="350">[350] Logit Distillation on Manifolds: Mapping by Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00771) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiru Yang, Junling Wang, Nishant Kumar Singh, Luohong Wu, Haoran Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Model Compression, LoRA
- **Summary**: 针对集成模型部署计算量大的问题，提出逐层逐点投影映射将学生和教师表示对齐到高维嵌入空间，结合LoRA注入，使学生模型可训练参数少于教师模型的1%，同时在词错误率上显著优于其他蒸馏方法，且可快速并行训练。

<h3 id="351">[351] GIRL-DETR: Gradient-Isolated Reinforcement Learning for Video Moment Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00775](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00775) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shihang Zhang, Mingjin Kuai, Ye Wei, Zhen Zhang, Wei Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Moment Retrieval, Reinforcement Learning, Temporal Grounding
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Z-Shihang/GIRL-DETR)
- **Summary**: 针对视频时刻检索中连续替代损失与非可微度量不匹配导致优化停滞的问题，首次在轻量级时序定位框架中引入RL后训练。提出梯度隔离RL用于DETR，包括跨模态交互和文本引导门控机制，以及三阶段渐进强化学习策略。实验表明有效解决替代损失退化，以最小参数更新获得显著精度提升。

<h3 id="352">[352] Behavior-Invariant Task Representation Learning with Transformer-based World Models for Offline Meta-Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00780](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00780) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fuyuan Qian, Menglong Zhang, Song Wang, Quanying Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Offline RL, Meta-Learning, World Model
- **Summary**: 针对离线元强化学习中上下文分布偏移和策略分布偏移导致泛化困难的问题，提出结合信息论任务表示学习和Transformer-based随机世界模型的框架，提取行为策略不变的任务定义潜变量以缓解上下文偏移，并应用保守值惩罚到想象展开以防止策略利用模型误差。实验表明在分布外和稀疏奖励设置下优于现有方法。

<h3 id="353">[353] Bayesian Inference of Nonlinear Malaria Dynamics in Ghana via an Ensemble Markov Chain Monte Carlo Sampler</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00783) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: T. Ansah-Narh, Y. Asare Afrane, J. Bremang Tandoh
- **Subjects**: stat.AP; cs.AI; math.PR; stat.CO
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Inference, Time Series Forecasting
- **Venue**: Expert Systems with Applications
- **Summary**: 该论文针对加纳疟疾监测数据短、噪声大、空间异质性强的问题，提出了一种集成贝叶斯非线性推断框架，使用仿射不变集成马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛采样器估计含阻尼振荡核的三次基线模型。结果表明，模型对五岁以下和五岁以上人群的R²分别达到0.9958和0.9956，残差低于2%，并预测2024-2026年病例将逐渐回升。

<h3 id="354">[354] Extending Causal Metamodeling to a non-Markovian Queue</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00795](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00795) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pracheta Amaranath, Anant Bhide, David Jensen, Peter Haas
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Probabilistic Inference
- **Summary**: 针对离散事件仿真元模型局限于马尔可夫系统的问题，该论文通过相位型分布近似非指数分布，将模块化动态贝叶斯网络扩展到非马尔可夫队列。在G/M/1队列上的实验表明，该方法能够准确回答概率和因果查询，并实现数量级的推理加速。

<h3 id="355">[355] DASH: Dual-Branch Score Distillation for Guidance-Calibrated Compact Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00798](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00798) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abdullah Al Shafi, Kazi Saeed Alam, Sk Imran Hossain, Engelbert Mephu Nguifo
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Diffusion Model, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 针对类条件扩散模型输出级蒸馏中无条件分数分支无监督导致引导间隙的问题，该论文提出DASH双分支蒸馏框架，独立监督两个分数分支并引入锚定项和教师重要性课程迁移。在CIFAR-10/100上，5.9倍压缩后质量与教师模型相差4个FID以内，无条件监督贡献了60%以上的增益。

<h3 id="356">[356] Dynamic Coordination Strategy Selection for Enterprise Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00804](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00804) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thanh Luong Tuan
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Enterprise AI
- **Summary**: 针对企业多智能体系统缺乏协调策略选择依据的问题，该论文通过30项跨行业任务的实证评估，发现动态路由策略在近最优性上得到支持（预测策略与最佳条件质量分数差距\<0.10），但精确优胜者识别不稳定，建议采用动态路由作为校准默认值。

<h3 id="357">[357] Certificates without Electrons? Theory and Evidence on Impacts from AI-Driven Power Demand</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00811](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00811) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dana Golden, Aruna Balasubramanian, Niranjan Balasubramanian
- **Subjects**: econ.EM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Sustainability, Energy Management
- **Summary**: 针对数据中心使用可再生能源证书和购电协议声称碳中和的有效性不明确的问题，该论文构建博弈论模型并利用大语言模型发布的自然实验进行差异分析。结果表明，AI需求显著增加化石燃料发电、批发电价（高达25%）和停电频率，而仅依靠可再生能源证书的策略无法缓解电网影响。

<h3 id="358">[358] SkillPager: Query-Adaptive Intra-Skill Navigation via Semantic Node Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00822](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00822) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zicai Cui, Zihan Guo, Weiwen Liu, Weinan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型智能体依赖长程序文档导致令牌浪费和信息稀释的问题，该论文提出SkillPager框架，将Markdown技能解析为类型化语义节点，并在线利用最大边际相关性进行查询条件节点选择。在技能基准上，以47.04%的令牌压缩实现78.89%的上下文充分性，接近全文档基线的82.23%。

<h3 id="359">[359] Beyond Independent Manipulation: Individual Fairness-aware Strategic Classification with Peer Imitation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00827](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00827) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinpeng Lv, Chunyuan Zheng, Yunxin Mao, Renzhe Xu, Jinxuan Yang, Yuanlong Chen, Wangrong Huang, Shaowu Yang, Wenjing Yang, Xinwang Liu, Peng Cui, Haotian Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Strategic Classification
- **Venue**: SIGKDD2026
- **Summary**: 针对现有公平感知策略分类假设智能体独立响应，但个体公平性导致相互依赖操纵的问题，该论文引入个体公平感知策略分类框架（IFSC），建模基于相似性模仿的同伴驱动操纵。实验表明，IFSC提升了个体公平一致性并减轻了模仿导致的失真。

<h3 id="360">[360] Hybrid Probabilistic Forecasting of Under-Five Malaria Admissions in Ghana: A Gaussian Process Regression with Holt-Winters Smoothing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00834](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00834) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: T. Ansah-Narh, Y. Asare Afrane, J. Bremang Tandoh
- **Subjects**: stat.AP; cs.AI; cs.LG; math.PR
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Probabilistic Forecasting
- **Venue**: Artificial Intelligence in Medicine
- **Summary**: 针对加纳疟疾预测中强季节性、报告不确定性和非平稳动态的挑战，该论文提出高斯过程回归与Holt-Winters指数平滑的混合框架。在地区级数据上，混合模型R²=0.9906，比单独Holt-Winters提高0.1693，94.2%的残差在±2σ范围内。

<h3 id="361">[361] MoEIoU: Rethinking Bounding-Box Regression as a Mixture of Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00844) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vinay Edula, Priyanka Bagade
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Object Detection
- **Summary**: 针对现有基于IoU的损失函数使用固定几何惩罚，未考虑优化动态的问题，该论文提出MoEIoU，一种基于混合专家模型的回归损失，使用对数求和指数函数聚合重叠、中心对齐和宽高比，并采用课程权重调度。在PASCAL VOC、HRIPCB和MS COCO上一致优于现有损失函数。

<h3 id="362">[362] RefDiffNet: Learning to Expose Subtle PCB Defects Before Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00852) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vinay Edula, Nilesh Badwe, Priyanka Bagade
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Object Detection, Anomaly Detection, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 针对PCB缺陷检测中微小缺陷难以区分的问题，该论文提出RefDiffNet，一种轻量级即插即用输入增强模块，利用无缺陷参考图像突出缺陷区域。在HRIPCB和DeepPCB上，从YOLOv8到YOLOv26、RT-DETR和Faster R-CNN均获得一致提升，最高相对mAP50:95增益达18%，额外参数仅0.004-0.005M。

<h3 id="363">[363] From Cues to Horizons: Dynamic Risk Horizon Profiling for Trajectory Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00857](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00857) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyi Ning, Zilin Bian, Dachuan Zuo, Semiha Ergan, Kaan Ozbay
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Trajectory Prediction, Autonomous Driving
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/bilab-nyu/RHP)
- **Summary**: 针对风险感知轨迹预测忽略未来风险演化的问题，该论文提出风险视野分析模块，结合连续可学习势场模型进行风险感知轨迹预测。在highD和SHRP2数据集上，5秒RMSE和minFDE分别降低25.0%和29.1%。

<h3 id="364">[364] GenPT: Beyond Self-Report for Reliable LLM Psychometrics via Generative Projective Testing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00860](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00860) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ming Wang, Shuang Wu, Bixuan Wang, Lu Lin, Yuxin Chen, Xiaocui Yang, Daling Wang, Shi Feng, Yifei Zhang, Yufan Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.SI; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Psychological Measurement
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sci-m-wang/GenPT)
- **Summary**: 针对大语言模型心理测量中自我报告问卷易受语料污染和方向偏差的问题，该论文提出GenPT生成式投射测试，改编TAT、罗夏测验和SCT，并设计三阶段评估流程。结果表明，GenPT避免了社交期望性偏差，在纵向咨询场景下抑郁评估变化幅度比问卷大一个数量级。

<h3 id="365">[365] Benchmarks for Vision-Language Models in Urban Perception Should Be Reliability-Aware and Negotiated</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00871](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00871) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rashid Mushkani
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对视觉语言模型在城市感知基准中忽略标注分歧和弃权的问题，该论文提出将分歧和弃权作为测量结果，并报告注释者间信度。在100张蒙特利尔街景、30个维度的基准测试中，模型与人类共识的一致性随维度人类信度协变。

<h3 id="366">[366] Task diversity produces systematic transfer but inhibits continual reinforcement learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00880](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00880) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Purab Seth, Neil Shah, Kunal Jha, Samuel J. Gershman, Max Kleiman-Weiner, Wilka Carvalho
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对任务多样性在持续强化学习中的作用尚不明确的问题，该论文引入Banyan域，将多样性分解为地图布局、对象和子目标依赖三个轴。实验表明，多样性引起局部迁移，但长期任务趋于平稳且早期任务被遗忘，无法实现真正的持续学习。

<h3 id="367">[367] Dive into Waves: Morlet Spectral Transformer for Cross-Subject Emotion Decoding from EEG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00884](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00884) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaxin Qing, Lexin Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: EEG Classification, Affective Computing, Brain-Computer Interface
- **Summary**: 针对跨被试EEG情绪识别中信号弱、噪声大、个体差异大的问题，该论文提出Morlet谱变换器，结合小波令牌化、长上下文基线去除和频带特定空间投影。在不预训练的情况下，它在SEED系列数据集上一致优于大型预训练EEG基础模型和频域方法。

<h3 id="368">[368] Memory-Efficient LLM Training with Dynamic Sparsity: From Stability to Practical Scaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00888) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiao Xiao, Boqian Wu, Patrik Okanovic, Tomasz Sternal, Maurice van Keulen, Elena Mocanu, Mykola Pechenizkiy, Decebal Constantin Mocanu, Torsten Hoefler
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Model Compression
- **Venue**: ICML2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/QiaoXiao7282/SMET)
- **Summary**: 针对动态稀疏训练在大语言模型中出现优化不稳定性（损失尖峰）的问题，该论文提出SMET，通过优化器热身和密度感知学习率缩放稳定训练，并仅存储活动参数的梯度和优化器状态。实验表明，SMET实现了稳定、可扩展且内存高效的稀疏预训练。

<h3 id="369">[369] MLLM-Microscope: Unlocking Hidden Structure Within Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00909) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ravil Mussabayev, Rustam Mussabayev
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Vision-Language Model, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）内部表示分析不足的问题，提出MLLM-Microscope系统，评估跨层的线性度、本征维度和各向异性。实验表明，LLaVA-NeXT和OmniFusion的主流和残差流均表现出高度线性，但OmniFusion的图像token维度更高且各向异性更低，有助于理解MLLM内部机制。

<h3 id="370">[370] Accuracy, Stability, and Repeated-Run Reliability of Large Language Models on Deterministic Programming Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00920](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00920) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongxi Zhou, Lai Yun Choi, Jiaxi Wen, Wenbo Ye
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对标准代码生成基准仅关注单次准确率，忽略稳定性的问题，提出重复运行评估协议，涵盖运行级准确率、免重试覆盖率等指标。在16个模型、16,000次实例上发现，运行级通过率最多高估免重试覆盖率17.8个百分点，且提示效果因模型而异，表明稳定性分析是必要补充。

<h3 id="371">[371] Benchmarking Security Risk Detection and Verification in Open Agentic Skill Ecosystems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00925) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ismail Hossain, Sai Puppala, Zhuoran Lu, Sajedul Talukder, Nan Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 开放智能体平台存在恶意技能供应链风险，但缺乏同时检测和验证的基准。提出SkillVetBench，包含语义审查和沙箱运行时验证两阶段。实验表明，纯语义基线漏检高达89%的恶意技能，运行时攻击集中于高权限原语，沙箱执行提供可审计证据。

<h3 id="372">[372] Detection vs. Execution: Single-Bucket Probes Miss Half the Mamba-2 State Sink</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00930](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00930) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuhang Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, State Space Model
- **Summary**: 机械可解释性中的探针可能无法区分检测电路和执行电路。针对Mamba-2的状态汇，使用单桶探针仅恢复少量执行层（约5%头部），而多类聚合可恢复检测层（27-35%头部）。消融实验表明，表示相似性不等于功能等价性，对下游性能影响显著。

<h3 id="373">[373] CV-Arena: An Open Benchmark for Instructional Computer Vision Problem Solving with Human-AI Collaborative Preferences</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00931) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Fangzhou Lin, Peiran Li, Lingyu Xu, Wenjing Chen, Qianwen Ge, Shuo Xing, Mingyang Wu, Xiangbo Gao, Siyuan Yang, Kazunori Yamada, Ziming Zhang, Haichong Zhang, Zhen Dong, Ming-Hsuan Yang, Zhengzhong Tu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Editing, Benchmark, Instruction Following
- **Summary**: 现有图像编辑基准集中于窄范围外观编辑，未能覆盖专业工作流的多样性。定义指令型计算机视觉问题求解，构建CV-Arena基准（12K高分辨率图像对，16种任务类型），并提出Active Elo协议。评估21个系统，发现指令遵循、物理推理等方面存在差距，CV-Agent展示潜力。

<h3 id="374">[374] Lodestar: An Online-Learning LLM Inference Router</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00946](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00946) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gangmuk Lim, Wanyu Zhao, Brighten Godfrey, Jiaxin Shan, Le Xu, Liguang Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Serving, Distributed Computing
- **Summary**: LLM推理请求路由因输入依赖、批处理、KV缓存重用而极具挑战。提出Lodestar，基于在线学习的路由系统，持续收集集群快照并训练奖励预测器。在公共云GPU集群上，平均TTFT降低1.41倍，P99 TTFT降低1.47倍，且能快速适应。

<h3 id="375">[375] Silent Failures in Federated Personalization of Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00947) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: YongKyung Oh, Alex Bui
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Fairness, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 联邦学习与基础模型个性化的结合产生了难以检测的可信度失效（静默失效），如偏见加剧、公平性崩塌和对齐侵蚀。通过现状分析，提出六种静默失效模式分类，并指出隐私保护训练不足以确保可信部署，需进一步研究隐私保护行为评估。

<h3 id="376">[376] Explainable deep reinforcement learning reveals energy-efficient control strategies for turbulent drag reduction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00949](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00949) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Federica Tonti, Ricardo Vinuesa
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 针对壁湍流减阻问题，提出结合多智能体强化学习和可解释深度学习的方法。基于SHAP归因的三种奖励策略中，结合壁面摩擦系数和壁面压力脉动的策略最佳，减阻率34.44%，净节能34.01%，归一化驱动功率仅0.43%。分析表明高效策略与压力门控驱动一致。

<h3 id="377">[377] SS-ZKR: Spatial-Semantic Zero-Knowledge Routing for Privacy-Preserving Multi-Agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00962](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00962) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hassan Touheed
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, Multi-Agent System, Zero-Knowledge Proof
- **Summary**: 现有协议无法在不解密负载的情况下进行基于内容的语义路由，违反合规要求。提出SS-ZKR，一个三机制隐私保护路由协议，包括基于差分隐私和零知识证明的盲路由、自适应净化、空间到密码策略编译器。提供正式威胁模型和复杂度分析。

<h3 id="378">[378] An Open-Source Benchmark and Baseline for Multi-temporal Referring Segmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.00987](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00987) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bingyu Li, Da Zhang, Tao Huo, Zhiyuan Zhao, Junyu Gao, Xuelong Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Segmentation, Vision-Language Model, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 大型视觉语言模型在多时间视觉推理方面能力不足。提出多时间指代分割（MTRS）任务，构建MTRefSeg-21K基准（21K多时间图像-文本-掩码三元组），并提出MTRefSeg-R1框架。实验表明，该框架优于现有LVLM基线，展示了MTRS的挑战与潜力。

<h3 id="379">[379] Beyond Task-Agnostic: Task-Aware Grouping for Communication-Efficient Multi-Task MoE Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01007](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01007) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiyao Xu, Aoxue Liu, Zhanjie Ding, Dan Zhao, Yong Jiang, Qing Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Inference, Distributed Computing
- **Summary**: 现有MoE专家放置方法使用全局聚合路由轨迹，忽略了任务特定的协同激活模式。提出任务感知协同激活分组（TACG）和通用专家共享复制（GESR），将通信成本降低31.39%，并保持高Jain公平指数。

<h3 id="380">[380] FVSpec: Real-World Property-Based Tests as Lean Challenges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01008](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01008) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Quinn Dougherty, Max von Hippel, Hazel Shackleton, Mike Dodds
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, LLM Agent, Program Verification
- **Summary**: 人工智能在真实软件形式验证上的评估缺乏基准。从真实Python仓库抓取11,039个属性测试，自动翻译2,772个为9,415个Lean规范。描述了三智能体LLM管道，并提供基准和基线，以推动AI辅助形式验证的发展。

<h3 id="381">[381] Cross-Axis Feature Fusion with Joint-Wise Motion Difference Prediction for Text-Based 3D Human Motion Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01014](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01014) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gyojin Han, Junmo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Text-to-Motion, Motion Synthesis
- **Summary**: 文本驱动的3D人体运动编辑需要保留源运动风格与结构。提出基于轴锚定Transformer和跨轴融合块的架构，并引入辅助任务预测关节间Soft-DTW距离。在MotionFix数据集上取得最优结果，显著提升语义对齐和运动质量。

<h3 id="382">[382] AI-IoT-Robotics Integration: Survey of Frameworks, Emerging Trends, and the Path Toward Connected Robotics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01015](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01015) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ranulfo Bezerra, Satoshi Tadokoro, Kazunori Ohno
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.NI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Survey, Embodied AI, IoT
- **Venue**: IEEE Internet of Things Journal
- **Summary**: 人工智能、物联网和机器人学的全面融合缺乏统一设计框架。综合评述各领域进展，提出模块化系统架构，强调小语言模型在边缘、大语言模型在云端的混合作用，并分析了互操作性和反馈控制等持续存在的差距。

<h3 id="383">[383] PolySpeech-100: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Speech Understanding Across 100+ Languages and Dialects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01016) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sicheng Yang, Shulan Ruan, Shiwei Wu, Yu Liu, Lu Fan, Zhi Li, You He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Recognition, Multilingual Learning, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YoungSeng/PolySpeech-100)
- **Summary**: 现有语音基准偏向高资源语言，局限于低层识别，忽视方言。构建PolySpeech-100基准，覆盖110种语言变体。评估22个模型发现：开源端到端模型在重方言上优于级联模型；思维链提示降低多数模型性能；开源模型在低资源语言上严重退化。

<h3 id="384">[384] Hybrid Verified Decoding: Learning to Allocate Verification in Speculative Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01019) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xin Su, Dawid Majchrowski, Fangyuan Yu, Vanshil Atul Shah, Sebastian Rogawski, Pawel Morkisz, Anahita Bhiwandiwalla, Phillip Howard
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 推测性解码速度提升取决于草稿接受长度，无参数草稿源收益多变。提出混合验证解码，预测缓存草稿的接受长度，选择缓存验证或模型草稿器。在智能体工作流上平均加速2.73倍，分析表明提示结构创造缓存机会，收益引导选择可减少顺序解码工作。

<h3 id="385">[385] ProductWebGen: Benchmarking Multimodal Product Webpage Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01022) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhihong Liu, Siqi Kou, Zheng Li, Ye Ma, Quan Chen, Peng Jiang, Kai Yu, Zhijie Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Benchmark, Image Generation
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/SJTU-DENG-Lab/ProductWebGen)
- **Summary**: 论文提出 ProductWebGen 基准，用于评估多模态产品网页生成任务。设计两种工作流：基于编辑的（LLM+图像编辑模型）和基于统一模型的。实验表明编辑方法在网页指令跟随和内容吸引力上领先，统一模型在视觉内容指令上更有优势。

<h3 id="386">[386] Data Collection for Training Quality-Control AI in Carpet Manufacturing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01023](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01023) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akbar Erkinov
