- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Industrial AI
- **Summary**: 论文提出一种用于地毯制造中质量控制的在线机器视觉系统设计，通过线扫描相机和特定照明采集缺陷图像，并采用无监督异常检测初步建模，再通过人工标注循环实现监督检测。该方法将数据收集作为工程目标，以提升过程质量。

<h3 id="387">[387] DSL-LLaDA: Scaling Continuous Denoising to 8B Masked Diffusion LMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01024) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Longxuan Yu, Yunshu Wu, Yu Fu, Siheng Xiong, Rob Brekelmans, Hui Liu, Yue Dong, Greg Ver Steeg
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Masked Language Modeling
- **Summary**: 论文通过连续去噪（DSL）改进离散掩码扩散语言模型，避免长短序列质量权衡。从LLaDA-8B-Instruct微调1000步，在低步数下零样本摘要的ROUGE-1指标最佳，并具备噪声鲁棒性。

<h3 id="388">[388] Temporally-Aligned Evaluation for Audio-Driven Talking Head Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01031) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.GR; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Audio-Visual Learning, Evaluation
- **Summary**: 论文质疑音频驱动说话头生成的逐帧评估指标，提出基于软动态时间规整的序列级对齐评估框架。在大规模基准测试中，时序对齐指标更稳定，能更好区分建模范式。

<h3 id="389">[389] OPD+: Rethinking the Advantage Design for On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01039](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01039) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hanyang Zhao, Haoxian Chen, Han Lin, Genta Indra Winata, David Yao, Wenpin Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 论文指出在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中停止梯度设计导致奖励估计偏差，提出OPD+修正版本。在数学推理和工具使用基准上验证了改进效果。

<h3 id="390">[390] Plausibility Is Not Prediction: Contrastive Evidence for LLM-Based Cellular Perturbation Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01042](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01042) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Yuan, Xixian Liu, Jianan Zhao, Yashi Zhang, Hongyu Guo, Jian Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Computational Biology, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 论文发现LLM在细胞扰动推理中虽有合理解释但预测能力差，提出CORE框架，通过对比证据组织提升扰动特异性预测。在药物扰动数据上Qwen3.5-9B聚合指标提升28.6%。

<h3 id="391">[391] 3DCodeBench: Benchmarking Agentic Procedural 3D Modeling Via Code</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01057](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01057) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yipeng Gao, Lei Shu, Genzhi Ye, Xi Xiong, Ameesh Makadia, Meiqi Guo, Laurent Itti, Jindong Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, 3D Generation, Code Generation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 论文提出3DCodeBench基准，评估VLM智能体通过代码进行程序化3D建模的能力。实验发现失败主要源于API不匹配，测试时扩展可提升性能。

<h3 id="392">[392] MENTIS: What Belief Changes Under Alignment? Measuring Multi-Scale Latent Torsion in Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01060](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01060) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Partha Pratim Saha, Samarth Raina, Mayur Parvatikar, Amit Dhanda, Vinija Jain, Aman Chadha, Amitava Das
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: 论文提出MENTIS框架，测量偏好对齐引起的模型内部几何结构变化。发现对齐效应具有选择性，规范概念变化更大，峰值位于中后层。

<h3 id="393">[393] Leyline: KV Cache Directives for Agentic Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01065) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bole Ma, Jan Eitzinger, Harald Koestler
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 论文提出Leyline，一种KV缓存指令原语，支持策略驱动的缓存编辑（如拼接、截断）。在agentic推理中减少延迟最高241ms，求解率提升14.3%。

<h3 id="394">[394] Test-Time Training for Zero-Resource Dense Retrieval Reranking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01070](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01070) (cross-listed)

- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Test-Time Adaptation, Zero-Shot Learning
- **Venue**: KnowFM @ ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 论文提出DART，在测试时通过伪正负例微调双线性评分矩阵，实现零资源稠密检索重排序。在BEIR上平均NDCG@10提升2.1%，延迟低于10ms。

<h3 id="395">[395] ThinkSwitch: Context Distillation with LoRA and Weight Interpolation for Specific-Purpose Reasoning Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dhruv Saini, Rohan Pandey
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, LoRA, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 论文提出ThinkSwitch，通过蒸馏和权重插值协同训练指令与思考检查点。在AIME和PubMedQA上分别提升10/30和5/30的准确率，成本仅2.86美元。

<h3 id="396">[396] MViewRouter: Internalizing Geometric Equivariance via Multi-view Alternating Attention for Combinatorial Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01084](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01084) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shiyan Liu, Bohan Tan, Yaoxin Wu, Yan Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Combinatorial Optimization, Geometric Deep Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 论文提出MViewRouter，通过多视图交替注意力内化几何等变性，解决组合路由问题。在TSP和CVRP上取得竞争力结果，并具备零样本泛化能力。

<h3 id="397">[397] Strong Stochastic Flow Maps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01086](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01086) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sam McCallum, Zander W. Blasingame, Timothy Herschell, Niklas Rindtorff, Alexander Tong, James Foster
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 论文提出强随机流映射（SSFMs），学习加性噪声SDE的强解映射，推广确定性流映射。在图像生成和分子系统采样中表现优异。

<h3 id="398">[398] A Fiber Criterion for Representation Identifiability in Supervised Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01092) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vasileios Sevetlidis
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Supervised Learning, Interpretability
- **Summary**: 论文形式化监督学习中表示的可识别性问题，提出纤维准则。证明预测器保持的增广会使表示属性无法识别，显示表示级声明需要额外假设。

<h3 id="399">[399] Beyond Task Success: Behavioral and Representational Diagnostics for WAM and VLA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01095](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01095) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hung Mai, Bin Zhu, Tuan Do
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 论文提出诊断框架，从行为和表示两个维度比较世界-动作模型（WAM）和视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）。发现WAM在目标级别行为上更好，但计算成本更高。

<h3 id="400">[400] Implicit Drifting Policy: One-Step Action Generation via Conditional Expert Geometry</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01098](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01098) (cross-listed)

- **Summary**: 论文提出隐式漂移策略（IDP），从条件专家几何中提取流形约束，实现单步动作生成。在2D、3D和真实机器人任务上有效保持动作流形，与强基线性能相当。

<h3 id="401">[401] MiCU: End-to-End Smart Home Command Understanding with Large Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01099](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01099) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haowei Han, Kexin Hu, Weiwei Cai, Debiao Zhang, Bin Qin, Yuxiang Wang, Jiawei Jiang, Xiao Yan, Bo Du
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Smart Home, Curriculum Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xiaomi-research/iot_spec_llm)
- **Summary**: 本文针对智能家居中命令理解系统对模糊或误对齐命令处理不佳的问题，提出MiCU，一个端到端的智能家居命令理解大语言模型。该方法通过自动化数据合成、课程学习注入领域知识、冷启动结合强化学习增强推理能力，并引入令牌压缩技术降低推理开销。实验结果显示MiCU在所有设备类别上平均准确率提升20.01%，在小米Home应用中部署后用户修正率降低1.57%，人工审核准确率提升32.05%。

<h3 id="402">[402] Soft-NBCE: Entropy-Weighted Chunk Fusion for Long-Context</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01101](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01101) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shihao Ji, Mingyu Li, Zihui Song
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long Context, LLM Inference, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 本文针对长上下文推理中Naive Bayes Cognitive Engine硬选择导致语义碎片化的问题，提出Soft-NBCE，一种软熵加权块融合方法。该方法用温度缩放的Softmax对预测熵分配连续权重，并通过一致性蒸馏（LoRA）约束块化logit分布接近全上下文教师。在LongBench多跳基准上，MuSiQue F1从0.275提升到0.310，HotpotQA F1从0.427提升到0.479，同时保持检索准确率。

<h3 id="403">[403] HASTE: Hardware-Aware Dynamic Sparse Training for Large Output Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01117](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01117) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nasib Ullah, Jinbin Zhang, Jean Lucien Randrianantenaina, Erik Schultheis, Rohit Babbar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Extreme Multi-Label Classification, Sparse Training, GPU Computing
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对极端多标签分类中输出层成为内存计算瓶颈的问题，提出HASTE，一种硬件感知的动态稀疏训练方法。该方法采用组共享固定扇入稀疏性，将语义相关标签共享稀疏输入模式，并利用长尾结构分解输出层。通过自定义CUDA内核，前向传播加速达4.4倍，反向传播加速达25倍，同时保持与密集瓶颈相近的精度。

<h3 id="404">[404] STARFISH: faST Accuracy Recovery in pruned networks From Internal State Healing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01126](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01126) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shir Maon, Odelia Melamed, Adi Shamir
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Pruning, Model Compression, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 本文针对剪枝网络准确率下降的问题，提出STARFISH，一种通过内部状态对齐的快速准确率恢复方法。该方法使用少量无标签校准集优化剪枝网络，使其与原始网络的内部表示对齐。在ViT网络上，去除50%权重时准确率恢复比SOTA提升高达22%；去除75%权重时，仅用0.4%训练图像恢复82%的原始密集准确率，而竞争方法仅恢复40%。

<h3 id="405">[405] AMP: A Vendor-Neutral Wire Format for Agent Memory Operations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01138](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01138) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thamilvendhan Munirathinam
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Architecture, Memory Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mthamil107/memorywire)
- **Summary**: 本文针对智能体记忆框架缺乏统一有线格式的问题，提出memorywire，一种基于JSON Schema的有线格式。该格式定义五种记忆操作和四种记忆类型，包含Human-in-the-Loop治理通道。参考实现表明，在100事实/50查询语料库上召回@5=1.000，摄入p50=37.8ms，召回p50=40.6ms，并通过16场景跨适配器一致性测试。

<h3 id="406">[406] ASE-26: a curriculum for agentic software engineering as a discipline</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01152](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01152) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mikael Gorsky
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Curriculum Learning, Education Technology
- **Summary**: 本文针对软件工程转向智能体驱动而缺乏结构化学科的问题，提出ASE-26，一个智能体软件工程本科学位课程。该课程包含21个模块，以进化螺旋为核心概念，并探讨了与智能体协作的评分方式。论文认为从业者技能缺口正是该学科所命名的技能，结构化课程是弥合缺口的主要机制。

<h3 id="407">[407] When Data Is Scarce: Scaling Sparse Language Models with Repeated Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01155](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01155) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boqian Wu, Qiao Xiao, Patrik Okanovic, Tomasz Sternal, Maurice van Keulen, Mykola Pechenizkiy, Elena Mocanu, Torsten Hoefler, Decebal Constantin Mocanu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Scaling Laws, Sparse Training, LLM Training
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/boqian333/sparse-dc-scaling)
- **Summary**: 本文针对数据受限条件下稀疏语言模型的缩放规律未探索的问题，通过实验研究了稀疏训练在多轮训练中的行为。提出了包含活跃参数、独特令牌、重复次数和稀疏度的缩放律。发现稀疏训练推迟了重复数据的收益递减，适度稀疏（约50%）在固定数据下损失最优，而计算最优稀疏度更高。

<h3 id="408">[408] AI From the Margins (AIM): Rethinking Participatory AI Design Through the Lived Experience of Minoritized Communities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01171) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tijs Portegies, Laureanne Willems, Maaike Harbers, Giovanni Sileno, Roland van Dierendonck, Mayesha Tasnim, Lotte Willemsen, Sennay Ghebreab
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Fairness, Participatory Design
- **Summary**: 本文针对参与式AI通常在问题定义后开始，限制边缘化社区重塑AI系统的问题，提出AIM方法论立场。该方法通过叙事启发、共建规则、参与者决定AI角色等方式，将边缘化社区的生活经验置于核心。在荷兰医疗健康语境中应用8次工作坊，参与者认为参与是实质性的，并呼吁继续。

<h3 id="409">[409] Physics-Informed Deep Learning for Entropy Prediction in Heterogeneous Systems: Thermodynamic and Information-Theoretic Case Studies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01179) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Biswajeet Sahoo, Debadutta Patra
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Scientific Computing, Information Theory
- **Summary**: 本文针对现有物理信息神经网络领域特定、难以提取领域无关熵表示的问题，提出一个统一物理信息深度学习框架。该框架同时强制执行微分方程残差和信息论约束，在热力学CSTR模型和金融市场模型中验证。Softplus约束确保热力学第二定律绝对满足，使用30%数据仍保持\>90%准确率，并通过后验几何分析识别相不稳定性。

<h3 id="410">[410] CA-BED: Conversation-Aware Bayesian Experimental Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01182](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01182) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel Arnould, Rashad Aziz, Zixuan Kang, Tanav Changal, Kevin Zhu, Sunishchal Dev, Gabriel Grand, Shreyas Sunil Kulkarni
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Active Learning, LLM Reasoning, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 本文针对大语言模型在交互式信息获取场景中性能下降的问题，提出对话感知贝叶斯实验设计（CA-BED）。该方法在推理时进行概率对话规划，通过贝叶斯实验设计优化问题选择。在结构化实体推断基准上，成功率平均提升21.8%，对话轮次仅增加1.8轮。

<h3 id="411">[411] Topological Ignorability for Structural Causal Effects Beyond Means</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01184](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01184) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Usef Faghihi
- **Subjects**: stat.ME; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Topological Data Analysis, Causal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 本文针对基于均值的因果估计量忽略分布结构变化的问题，引入拓扑几何因果度量。基于密度上水平集Betti摘要、欧拉签名和持续同调摘要，提出拓扑可忽略性假设。在弱可忽略性失败的半合成基准上，所选有限密度上水平集Betti和欧拉对比在已知、观察和加权分析中保持稳定。

<h3 id="412">[412] pcbGPT: Automatic PCB Schematic Synthesis from Natural Language Requirements</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01188) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tobias King, Steven Kehrberg, Michael Beigl, Tobias Röddiger
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: PCB Design, Hardware-Software Co-design, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 本文针对从自然语言硬件需求生成正确PCB原理图困难的问题，提出pcbGPT。该系统使用Python DSL表示电路，结合工具增强合成、组件库搜索、数据手册支撑的知识和执行检查。在20个嵌入式任务上，pass@1达0.90，pass@5达1.00，但尚不能完全替代专家审查。

<h3 id="413">[413] Low-Resource Safety Failures Are Action Failures, Not Representation Failures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01196](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01196) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rashad Aziz, Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif, Fajri Koto
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Cross-Lingual, Low-Resource NLP
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rashadaziz/low-resource-safety)
- **Summary**: 本文针对安全对齐跨语言迁移失败的问题，诊断发现表示存在但未转化为拒绝行为。通过重新校准低秩逻辑读出决策阈值，使用每类1-4个目标语言样本，将平均拒绝选择性从33.6提升到54.5，同时保持MMLU效用。表明部分低资源安全失败可通过重新校准现有表示而非学习新表示来修复。

<h3 id="414">[414] Implicit Geographic Inference in LLM Medical Triage: Language-Driven Disparities in Emergency Recommendations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01204](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01204) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Han Wong
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Medical AI, Cross-Lingual
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wongqihan/ai-behavioral-experiments)
- **Summary**: 本文研究了大语言模型是否仅根据患者提示语言产生不同的医疗分诊建议。使用Gemini 3.5 Flash评估六种语言，发现急诊推荐率从0%（日语、印地语）到30%（英语、阿拉伯语）不等，尽管严重度评分相似。添加美国位置语句使非英语提示的急诊推荐率增加高达76.7个百分点，而反向锚定减少推荐率。后向翻译控制证实差异来自语言隐含地理推理而非翻译质量。

<h3 id="415">[415] DiscourseFlip: An Oblique Discourse-Level Opinion Manipulation Attack against Black-box Retrieval-Augmented Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01212](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01212) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuyang Gong, Miaokun Chen, Jiawei Liu, Zhuo Chen, Guoxiu He, Wei Lu, XiaoFeng Wang, Xiaozhong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CR; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Security, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 本文针对现有RAG攻击局限于单查询的问题，提出DiscourseFlip，一种论述层意见操纵攻击。该攻击以智能体、图引导的方式在语义查询网络上协调影响，实现多主题意见偏移。实验表明DiscourseFlip consistently诱导目标意见偏移，覆盖率和效果显著优于基线，且用户难以察觉，现有防御无效。

<h3 id="416">[416] TECCI: Tricky Edits of Collected and Curated Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01213](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01213) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aishwarya Agrawal, Roy Hirsch, Yasumasa Onoe, Sherry Ben, Jason Baldridge
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Image Editing, Benchmark, Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文针对当前文本引导图像编辑方法在位置、运动、视角等挑战性编辑上仍存在困难的问题，提出TECCI基准。该基准包含7550对图像和编辑指令，涵盖7类图像和5种编辑类型。人工评估五个模型，无模型总体成功率超过22%。评估表明模型在指令跟随上表现较好，但在最小编辑和视觉质量上较弱，推理和创意编辑最难。

<h3 id="417">[417] Distilling Neuro-Symbolic Programs into 3D Multi-modal LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01215](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01215) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wentao Mo, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.MM
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, LLM Reasoning, Neurosymbolic AI
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/oceanflowlab/APEIRIA)
- **Summary**: 当前3D空间推理方法在可解释性和灵活性之间存在权衡。APEIRIA通过将神经符号程序的知识迁移到3D多模态大语言模型中，采用三阶段课程学习实现可解释推理。实验表明，APEIRIA在3D空间推理数据集上超越神经符号方法，并达到最先进3D MLLMs的性能。

<h3 id="418">[418] Fine-Tuning Diffusion Models for Molecular Generation via Reinforcement Learning and Fast Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01220) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guang Lin, Shikui Tu, Lei Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Molecular Generation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 结构药物设计中的分子生成面临多目标优化和采样成本高的问题。FTDiff提出基于强化学习的扩散模型微调框架，结合组相对策略优化和快速采样机制。在基准数据集上，FTDiff无需昂贵后处理即生成有效、多样、高质量的分子，优于现有方法。

<h3 id="419">[419] Hybrid Imbalanced Regression Through Unified Data-Level and Algorithm-Level Balancing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01221](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01221) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shermin Shahbazi, Hossein Mohammadi, Mohsen Afsharchi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imbalanced Regression
- **Venue**: Expert Systems with Applications
- **Summary**: 不平衡回归问题未得到充分研究，现有数据级和算法级方法各有局限。提出统一混合框架，结合自适应分箱、条件变分自编码器、多阶段重采样和潜在密度加权损失。实验表明该方法在基准数据集上稳定提升预测性能。

<h3 id="420">[420] Connecting the Dots: Benchmarking Reflective Memory in Long-Horizon Dialogue</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01223) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingjie Lin, Bingbing Wang, Zihan Wang, Zhengda Jin, Weiming Qiao, Jing Li, Ruifeng Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, LLM Evaluation, Memory Architecture
- **Summary**: 现有长对话基准仅测试事实记忆，缺乏对反思性记忆的评估。引入RefMem-Bench基准和REMIND层次框架，通过渐进式对齐提升反思推理。实验证明RefMem-Bench对当前模型具有挑战性，REMIND有效提高答案准确率和记忆召回。

<h3 id="421">[421] Understanding LLM Behavior in Multi-Target Cross-Lingual Summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01252](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01252) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sangwon Ryu, Yihong Liu, Mingyang Wang, Yunsu Kim, Jungseul Ok, Gary Geunbae Lee, Hinrich Schuetze
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Cross-Lingual, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 多目标跨语言摘要（MTXLS）研究不足。构建MEA基准，并通过层分析揭示翻译和摘要行为在后期层共同涌现。提出推理时激活引导方法，在各目标语言上一致提升MTXLS质量。

<h3 id="422">[422] IndoBias: A Dual Track Culturally Grounded Benchmark for LLMs Bias Evaluation in Indonesian Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01260](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01260) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ikhlasul Akmal Hanif, Muhammad Falensi Azmi, Filbert Aurelian Tjiaranata, Eryawan Presma Yulianrifat, Fajri Koto
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, LLM Evaluation, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 大语言模型在印尼语及当地语言中的偏见评估缺失。提出IndoBias基准，包含深度和广度评估轨道。发现现有模型偏向印尼语，当地语言在意识形态和宗教类别中偏见更高，且Common Crawl数据引入更多偏见。

<h3 id="423">[423] PALTO: Physics-Informed Active Learning for Tri-Gate FinFET Design Optimization for Vertical Power Delivery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01265](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01265) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ayoub Sadeghi, Leonid Popryho, Inna Partin-Vaisband
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Active Learning, Physics-Informed Learning, Circuit Design
- **Summary**: 氮化镓三栅极FinFET设计优化中，传统TCAD方法计算密集。采用物理信息主动学习框架，智能引导仿真。优化后器件D2在单栅中驱动电流更高，但300栅配置下D1功耗和开关效率更优，优于工业基准。

<h3 id="424">[424] DeepIPCv3: Event-Aware Multi-Modal Sensor Fusion for Sudden Pedestrian Crossing Avoidance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01277) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oskar Natan, Andi Dharmawan, Aufaclav Zatu Kusuma Frisky, Jazi Eko Istiyanto, Jun Miura
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; eess.IV; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Multi-Modal Fusion, Event Camera
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/oskarnatan/DeepIPCv3)
- **Summary**: 基于帧的传感器在突发行人穿越时存在延迟和运动模糊。提出DeepIPCv3框架，融合LiDAR与动态视觉传感器，采用交叉模态注意力。实验表明融合方法在轨迹和控制命令误差上达到最先进性能，实现快速避让。

<h3 id="425">[425] RLVR without Ineffective Samples: Group Prioritized Off-Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01281](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01281) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yixiu Mao, Yun Qu, Qi Wang, Heming Zou, Xiangyang Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Off-Policy RL
- **Summary**: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）受无效样本限制。提出组优先离策略优化（POPO），通过优先组重放和解耦重要性采样加速微调。在数学、规划、视觉几何等任务上，POPO显著加速RL微调并提升推理性能。

<h3 id="426">[426] Knowledge-Intensive Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01285](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01285) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chenxu Wang, Mingda Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Video, Benchmark, Knowledge-Intensive Video Generation
- **Summary**: 文本到视频生成在事实性评估上不足。提出知识密集型视频生成（KIVI）任务和KIVI-Bench基准。当前模型在视觉属性、操作过程和信息展示上落后于人类，凸显该方向的挑战。

<h3 id="427">[427] BenchEvolver: Frontier Task Synthesis via Solution-Centric Evolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01286](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01286) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yangzhen Wu, Aaron J. Li, Wenjie Ma, Li Cao, Ziheng Zhou, Mert Cemri, Shu Liu, Yuran Xiu, Chenxiao Yan, Haikun Zhao, Bin Yu, Ion Stoica, Dawn Song
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Benchmark, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 前沿大语言模型导致基准饱和，新数据集构建需大量人力。提出BenchEvolver，通过解决方案进化自动生成更难编程题。生成的LiveCodeBench-Plus使前沿模型Pass@1降至27.5%-62.6%，且进化任务可用于RL训练提升编码性能。

<h3 id="428">[428] Beyond Visual Memory: Mechanistic Diagnostics of Latent Visual Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01287) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Garvin Guo, Yu Chen, Xiang Wang, Shuai Li, Xinpei Zhao, Huaxing Liu, Shuai Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Visual Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 潜在视觉推理的增益来源存疑。将潜在标记分解为三个组件进行分析。发现增益来自边界标记、格式和注意力模式，而非潜在槽。训练监督方式影响机制使用，需评估模型实际依赖的内容。

<h3 id="429">[429] Quantum Algorithm for Distributed Reduction of Entanglements (QADR): A Trainable and Simulation-Efficient QML Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01291](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01291) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Syed Farhan Ahmad, Gregory T. Byrd
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Quantum Machine Learning
- **Summary**: 变分量子电路训练受指数级模拟内存和贫瘠高原限制。提出QADR将全局电路分解为局部子电路，降低内存需求。在MNIST和NASA轴承诊断任务上，QADR在2000特征下成功运行，性能匹配或超越经典模型。

<h3 id="430">[430] What Makes a Strong Model? A Unified Spectral Analysis of Knowledge Transfer over High-dimensional Linear Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01292](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01292) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wendao Wu, Fangqing Zhang, Haihan Zhang, Cong Fang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Deep Learning Theory, Knowledge Transfer
- **Summary**: 知识迁移缺乏统一理论框架。建立高维线性回归中SGD动力学的统一谱分析，揭示知识蒸馏的谱视野扩展和弱到强泛化的谱去噪机制。该框架统一了不同迁移现象。

<h3 id="431">[431] ResNet-34 with Lightweight Decoder for Accurate and Efficient Segmentation of Fetal Brain MRI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01293](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01293) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ashiqur Rahman, Muhammad E. H. Chowdhury, Md. Abu Sayed, Md. Sharjis Ibne Wadud, Abu Naser Md. Arafat, Mehedi Hasan Prince
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Segmentation, Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 胎儿脑MRI分割因运动、低对比度和解剖变异而困难。提出ResNet-34编码器与轻量MLP解码器。在FeTA 2021数据集上达到97.37%准确率、90.33% Dice系数，推理速度快，适合临床。

<h3 id="432">[432] ChronosAD: Leveraging Time Series Foundation Models for Accurate Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01300](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01300) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Uzair Khan, Luigi Capogrosso, Francesco Biondani, Michele Magno, Franco Fummi, Francesco Setti, Marco Cristani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Time Series Forecasting, Foundation Model
- **Venue**: 24th IEEE International Conference on Industrial Informatics (INDIN) 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/intelligolabs/ChronosAD)
- **Summary**: 时间序列异常检测方法难以跨数据集泛化。提出ChronosAD，利用时间序列基础模型提取特征，结合BiLSTM和多头注意力。在11个基准上平均AUC提升4.72%，AP提升6.60%。

<h3 id="433">[433] SkillAdaptor: Self-Adapting Skills for LLM Agents from Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01311](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01311) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhuoyun Yu, Xin Xie, Wuguannan Yao, Chenxi Wang, Lei Liang, Xiang Qi, Shumin Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zjunlp/SkillAdaptor)
- **Summary**: 现有的LLM agent技能适应方法依赖完整轨迹或会话级反馈，导致错误归因粗糙。本文提出SkillAdaptor，一种训练无关的步骤级技能适应框架，通过显式错误归因和责任链接实现针对性技能更新。在WebShop、PinchBench和Claw-Eval上，SkillAdaptor相比无技能和技能适应基线分别提升+1.7成功率、+1.5平均分和+1.8平均分。

<h3 id="434">[434] A Communication-Centric 6G-LLM Architecture for Scalable Tactical Autonomous Defense Vehicle Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01312](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01312) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kiran Khurshid, Shumaila Javaid, Nasir Saeed
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Digital Twin
- **Venue**: IEEE Network Magazine
- **Summary**: 针对战术自主防御车辆网络中协调效率和延迟问题，本文提出一种以通信为中心的6G-LLM分层架构，集成边缘LLM推理与语义通信。蒙特卡洛仿真表明，在30辆车规模下，相比5G传统AI基线，延迟降低75.2%（29.1 ms vs 117.5 ms），任务成功率提升68.7个百分点（82.9% vs 14.2%），通信开销降低88.6%。

<h3 id="435">[435] PSG-Nav: Probabilistic Scene Graph Navigation via Multiverse Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01313](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01313) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rufeng Chen, Yue Chang, Xiaqiang Tang, Hechang Chen, Sihong Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Scene Graph, Probabilistic Inference
- **Summary**: 开放词汇导航面临感知不确定性，现有方法采用局部确定性决策。本文提出概率场景图导航（PSG-Nav），构建3D概率场景图并利用多宇宙决策采样最优导航地标，同时引入证据经验校准器进行在线终身适应。在MP3D、HM3D和HSSD上分别达到66.1%、44.8%和67.9%的成功率，刷新SOTA。

<h3 id="436">[436] TukaBench: A Culturally Grounded Jailbreak Benchmark for African Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01322) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Victor Akinode, Senyu Li, Wassim Hamidouche, Waqas Zamir, Inbal Becker-Reshef, David Ifeoluwa Adelani
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Benchmark, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: LLM安全评估严重偏向英语，低资源非洲语言研究不足。本文提出TUKABENCH，涵盖七种非洲语言的越狱基准，包含四种设置：人工翻译、文化适配翻译、人机交互提示和代码切换。实验发现非洲语言提示比英语拒绝率更低，文化适配提示拒绝最少，并引入Deflection指标和人工标注验证。

<h3 id="437">[437] DiffuSent: Towards a Unified Diffusion Framework for Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01323](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01323) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shu Long, Yanglei Gan, Xuchuan Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 现有方面级情感分析生成模型采用自回归逐token生成，对多词方面词和观点词边界不敏感。本文提出DiffuSent，一个非自回归扩散框架，将七个ABSA子任务形式化为边界去噪扩散过程，并引入对比去噪训练。在28个设置上一致优于生成式和跨度式基线，多词三元组F1平均提升+2.48，推理速度提升181倍。

<h3 id="438">[438] Digital Twin-Assisted Adaptive Multi-Agent DRL for Intelligent Spectrum and Resource Management in Open-RAN UAV-Enabled 6G Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01324) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marwan Dhuheir, Thang X. Vu, Symeon Chatzinotas
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Digital Twin
- **Venue**: IEEE ICC-2026
- **Summary**: 无人机辅助6G网络中频谱和资源管理面临非线性交互和拓扑变化挑战。本文提出数字孪生辅助的深度强化学习框架，将问题分解为基于粒子群优化的无人机轨迹优化和多智能体DRL的动态频谱-功率-关联管理。仿真表明在频谱效率、数据速率和能量利用方面显著提升。

<h3 id="439">[439] FreqLite: A Lightweight Frequency-Decomposed Linear Model with Adaptive Reversible Normalization for Robust Long-Term Time-Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01339) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mirza Samad Ahmed Baiga, Syeda Anshrah Gillani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV; cs.ET
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 轻量级线性预测器在长期时间序列预测中受限于固定归一化和固定分解。本文提出FreqLite，一个超轻量级频域分解线性预测器，包含可学习的无损谱滤波器和自适应可逆归一化（A-RevIN）。在长回溯窗口L=336时，MSE 0.3244优于PatchTST的0.3587，参数量减少4倍，内存和时间减少2.2倍。

<h3 id="440">[440] Needles at Scale: LLM-Assisted Target Selection for Windows Vulnerability Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01364](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01364) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Michael J. Bommarito II
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Vulnerability Detection
- **Summary**: 大规模Windows漏洞研究中目标选择是主要瓶颈。本文提出Symbolicate-Enrich-Sample管道，通过自动获取符号文件、附加结构特征并由低成本LLM分配可达性等级和风险级别，将二进制文件转为优先研究队列。在720万函数中生成约2.2万候选函数短列表。

<h3 id="441">[441] BRo-JEPA: Learning Modular Arithmetic in Latent Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01372](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01372) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Divyansh Jha, Yuanfang Xie, Varan Mehra, Brennen Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: World Model, Compositional Generalization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/DL-World-Models/mnist-math)
- **Summary**: 神经网络能否学习抽象代数规则？本文利用MNIST数字和模算术操作在JEPA潜在世界模型中研究，提出块旋转预测器来施加循环结构。最佳ResNet-JEPA块旋转模型在模10算术上实现99.46%零样本准确率和99.46% rollout准确率，表明潜在世界模型在匹配结构时能学习符号变换规则。

<h3 id="442">[442] Beyond Access: Guided LLM Scaffolding for Independent Learning in Undergraduate Statistics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01375](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01375) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Amanlou, Yasaman Amou-Jafari, Mehrad Livian, Fatemeh Boloukazari, Fereshte Bagheri, Behnam Bahrak
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology
- **Summary**: LLM的教育价值取决于是否促进推理而非任务完成。本文在本科概率统计课程中进行准实验，比较无LLM、无限制LLM和引导LLM三种条件。结果显示引导组表现出更清晰的学习导向交互，干预期间无辅助测验表现更好，且自我评估校准更准确，表明仅提供LLM访问是不够的，需要脚手架设计。

<h3 id="443">[443] Efficient Exploration for Iterative Nash Preference Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01382) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianlong Nan, Xiaopeng Li, Christian Kroer, Tianyi Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 偏好对齐中标准奖励公式无法处理循环偏好。本文研究在线迭代Nash偏好优化，识别探索是关键障碍，发现标准方法可能对KL正则化参数呈指数依赖。提出显式探索的迭代NLHF算法，结合SFT正则化和对抗策略探索，实现O(√T)遗憾界，避免指数依赖。在Llama-3-8B-Instruct上得到一致改进。

<h3 id="444">[444] Bridging Requirements and Architecture: Multi-Agent Orchestration with External Knowledge and Hierarchical Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01385](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01385) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruiyin Li, Yiran Zhang, Xiyu Zhou, Yangxiao Cai, Peng Liang, Weisong Sun, Jifeng Xuan, Zhi Jin, Yang Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Software Engineering, RAG
- **Summary**: 软件架构设计复杂且耗时，LLM agent在此任务上探索不足。本文提出MAAD，一个知识驱动的多agent框架，集成RAG和分层记忆机制，自主将需求规格转化为多视图架构蓝图并评估质量属性。在10个案例和10个真实世界规格上，相比MetaGPT生成更完整、模块化和可追踪的架构，且评估agent显著减少手动验证工作。

<h3 id="445">[445] Dr. DocBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Expert-Level and Difficult Document Parsing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01393](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01393) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Minglai Yang, Xinyan Velocity Yu, Pengyuan Li, Xinyu Guo, Zhenting Qi, Konwoo Kim, Longtian Ye, Xiaolong Luo, Jinhe Bi, Henry Zhang, Haris Riaz, Xuan Zhang, Yunze Xiao, Bangya Liu, Tom Tang, Yunfei Zhao, Qunshu Lin, Zihan Wang, Minghao Liu, Michael Lingzhi Li, Yilun Du, Jesse Thomason, Rogerio Feris, Alex Pentland, Zexue He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, Benchmark, OCR
- **Summary**: 现有OCR和文档解析基准在覆盖度和难度上有限，缺乏专家领域结构标注。本文提出Dr. DocBench，一个难度感知的专家级文档解析基准，涵盖52个BISAC领域，通过解析器失败采样选择挑战性文档，包含4514页标注和6.5万高质量注释。评估显示现有强模型在新基准上表现不佳，揭示跨主题和内容类型的严重失败。

<h3 id="446">[446] Consistent and Distinctive: LLM Benchmark Efficiency via Maximum Independent Set Prompt Selection on Similarity Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01400](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01400) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Denica Kjorvezir, Marko Djukanović, Ana Gjorgjevikj, Gjorgjina Cenikj, Tome Eftimov
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Prompt Engineering
- **Summary**: 全面评估LLM成本高昂。本文提出基于图的提示选择框架，将每个基准建模为相似图，应用最大独立集算法选择多样化的非冗余子集。在四种基准和66个LLM上，随机配置中99.2%的Kendall W≥0.90，平均25-48%提示缩减，仅15.95%配置的排名与全基准显著偏离。

<h3 id="447">[447] Neural Network Compression by Approximate Differential Equivalence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01402) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ravi Dhiman, Andrea Passarella, Mirco Tribastone, Lorenzo Valerio
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression
- **Summary**: 神经网络压缩通常基于局部重要性剪枝。本文提出通过聚合功能相似的神经元来压缩模型，将训练好的网络编码为多项式ODE系统，应用近似前向微分等价识别具有匹配诱导动态的神经元。在合成和回归基准上，该方法在保持精度的同时大幅减少参数，在相似压缩水平上优于幅值剪枝和Wanda。

<h3 id="448">[448] CEAR: Certified Ensemble Adversarial Robustness in DNNs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01437](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01437) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Daniel Sadig, Mohammadreza Maleki, Hamed Karimi, Reza Samavi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Certified Robustness, Ensemble Learning
- **Venue**: 39th Canadian Conference on Artificial Intelligence (Canadian AI 2026)
- **Summary**: 深度神经网络易受对抗扰动影响。本文提出CEAR，一种基于集成的鲁棒方法，混合经验防御和认证防御，使用高斯噪声和温度混淆梯度，并提出两种投票机制。扩展随机化平滑以验证集成鲁棒性。在MNIST、CIFAR10和TinyImageNet上，平均认证准确率更高，鲁棒半径更大，迁移性更低。

<h3 id="449">[449] On the Evaluation of Spiking Neural Network Configurations for Network Intrusion Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01442](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01442) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raj Patel, David Amebley, Taye Akinrele, Shaswata Mitra, Sayanton Dibbo, Shahram Rahimi
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Intrusion Detection
- **Summary**: 针对网络入侵检测中深度学习模型计算需求高的问题，论文研究了脉冲神经网络（SNN）的配置（神经元模型和脉冲编码方案）。通过消融实验比较了9种神经元和3种编码方案在四个数据集上的性能，发现脉冲编码方案比神经元模型对检测质量影响更大，其中Latency编码与LeakyParallel神经元组合最优，平均准确率92.11%，宏F1 0.80，假阳性率2.01%。

<h3 id="450">[450] UR-JEPA: Uniform Rectifiability as a Regularizer for Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01443](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01443) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Triet M. Le
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: JEPA, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 针对联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）训练中的表示崩溃问题，论文提出UR-JEPA，使用均匀n-可微正则化（Carleson型平方函数）替代各向同性高斯正则化。实验表明UR-JEPA在Inet10上准确率提升0.83个百分点且方差更低，并在多个数据集上产生结构不同的投影表示（PCA谱指数下降）。

<h3 id="451">[451] Computation-Aware Kalman Filtering with Model Selection for Neural Dynamics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01468) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: JR Huml, Jonathan Wenger, John P. Cunningham
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: State Space Model, Computational Neuroscience, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 针对神经动力学建模中贝叶斯方法计算复杂度高而深度网络缺乏不确定性校准的问题，论文提出CASSM（计算感知状态空间模型），在规模不平衡（ trials \< neurons）情景下实现可推断的状态空间模型。方法在合成和真实数据上表现与深度网络相当，且不确定性校准显著优于以往贝叶斯扩展。

<h3 id="452">[452] Emergent Transfer of a Physics Foundation Model from Simulation to Laboratory Turbulence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01470](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01470) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Payel Mukhopadhyay, Stefan S. Nixon, Romain Watteaux, Michael McCabe, Alberto Bietti, Kyunghyun Cho, Cristiana Diaconu, Irina Espejo Morales, David Fouhey, Siavash Golkar, Tom Hehir, Shirley Ho, Jake Kovalic, Geraud Krawezik, Francois Lanusse, Tanya Marwah, Rudy Morel, Mariel Pettee, Helen Qu, Jeff Shen, Hadi Sotoudeh, Stuart B. Dalziel, Miles Cranmer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Scientific Computing, Transfer Learning
- **Summary**: 针对物理基础模型能否泛化到实验室实验的疑问，论文使用Rayleigh-Taylor不稳定性作为测试，微调连续动力学基础模型Walrus（仅用三个DNS模拟数据），零样本推广到实验室数据，预测到与实验一致的混合增长率，并推广到未训练的超稳定分层。结果表明基础模型可桥接模拟-实验差距。

<h3 id="453">[453] Hierarchical Online Prompt Mutation with Dual-Loop Feedback for Guardrailed Evidence Document Generation: A Production-Evaluation Case Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01472](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01472) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nataraj Agaram Sundar Tejas Morabia
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Prompt Engineering, Guardrail, Online Learning
- **Summary**: 针对高要求生产文档生成系统中语言模型需自适应、证据可靠且可审计的问题，论文提出HOPM，一种层次化在线提示变异框架，利用双环反馈（人工审核+自动评判）和护栏属性归因。在600个案例的消融实验中，完整HOPM将计数胜率从34.7%提升至45.7%，平均Likert质量从3.18提升至4.40，问题标记率从15.3%降至5.2%。

<h3 id="454">[454] Crazyflow: An Accurate, GPU-Accelerated, Differentiable Drone Simulator in JAX</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01478](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01478) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Martin Schuck, Marcel P. Rath, Yufei Hua, AbhisheK Goudar, SiQi Zhou, Angela P. Schoellig
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.MA; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Simulation, Robotics, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对现有无人机模拟器无法同时满足保真度、可微分性和大规模群集需求的问题，论文提出Crazyflow，一个基于JAX的GPU加速、可微分无人机模拟器。它比现有模拟器快一个数量级以上，可模拟数千个4000架无人机的群组，支持基于梯度的策略学习（亚厘米轨迹跟踪）和0.38秒内完成飞行中强化学习。

<h3 id="455">[455] MURMUR: An Efficient Inference System for Long-Form ASR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01483) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei-Tzu Lee, Keisuke Kamahori, Baris Kasikci
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, LLM Inference, KV Cache
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/uw-syfi/Murmur)
- **Summary**: 针对长格式自动语音识别（ASR）系统中准确率与延迟的权衡，论文提出Murmur推理系统，通过分块处理和滑动窗口KV缓存逐出策略，在保持准确率的同时将延迟降低4.2倍。在AMI-IHM上匹配单遍准确率，且令牌逐出仅导致不到1%相对tcpWER退化。

<h3 id="456">[456] LLM Consortium for Software Design Refinement: A Controlled Experiment on Multi-Agent Collaboration Topologies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01490](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01490) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nagarjuna Kanamarlapudi, Praveen K
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对多智能体LLM协作拓扑对软件架构设计的影响，论文通过2×2×2因子实验评估了12种协作拓扑（520次运行，8个设计任务）。发现结构性对抗变体（v4b）排名第一（加权集成4.637/5.0），跨模型评审排名第二，平行合并因令牌饥饿和弗兰肯斯坦效应表现最差。

<h3 id="457">[457] ClawHub Security Signals: When VirusTotal, Static Analysis, and SkillSpector Disagree</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01494) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Vincent Koc, Patrick Erichsen, Jacob Tomlinson, Agustin Rivera, Michael Appel, Nir Paz
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Agent Security, Malware Detection, Dataset
- **Summary**: 针对AI智能体技能安全中单独扫描器无法覆盖所有威胁的问题，论文发布ClawHub Security Signals数据集（67,453个技能版本），分析VirusTotal、静态分析和SkillSpector三组扫描器的不一致性。发现81.9%的标记技能仅被单个扫描器识别，恶意区域中72.8%被VirusTotal标记，表明需要分层治理。

<h3 id="458">[458] TimeSage-MT: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Evaluating Agentic Time Series Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01498](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01498) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaxuan Kong, Qingren Yao, Yuqi Nie, Yichen Li, Yilei Shao, Stefan Zohren, Anna Vettoruzzo, Joaquin Vanschoren, Ming Jin, Qingsong Wen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对现有时间序列基准仅支持单步任务而忽略多轮对话中演化需求的问题，论文提出TimeSage-MT，包含240个任务和2680轮对话的多轮时间序列推理基准。实验发现前沿LLM在决策导向任务上性能显著下降，暴露了记忆、不确定性处理和领域决策的缺陷。

<h3 id="459">[459] Move the Query, Not the Cache: Characterizing Cross-Instance Latent Attention Redistribution Across GPU Fabrics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01502](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01502) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bole Ma, Jan Eitzinger, Harald Köstler, Gerhard Wellein
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.NI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Distributed Computing
- **Summary**: 针对大型语言模型推理中查询与KV缓存跨GPU实例的问题，论文分析了多查询头潜在注意力（MLA）下查询路由与缓存移动的开销。通过实际H100集群测量，建立了拓扑感知成本模型，表明在解码阶段路由查询通常更优（微秒级往返 vs 毫秒级缓存重适配）。

<h3 id="460">[460] On the Limits of Token Reduction for Efficient Unified Vision Language Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01503](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01503) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyi Chen, Weiming Zhuang, Jingtao Li, Lingjuan Lv
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Token Compression, Training Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对统一视觉语言模型训练效率低的问题，论文研究了令牌减少加速的可行性。通过分析层注意力分配，发现视觉理解在后期存在冗余，而视觉生成持续依赖图像令牌。基于此设计任务特定加速器，但在统一训练中发现令牌丢失导致参数路径分岔，消除联合优化的协同增益。

<h3 id="461">[461] Agent Operating Systems (AOS): Integrating Agentic Control Planes into, and Beyond, Traditional Operating Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01508](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01508) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ankur Sharma, Deep Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Operating System, Operating System, Agent Architecture
- **Summary**: 针对传统操作系统无法适应长期、目标驱动、概率推理的智能体AI执行模型的问题，论文提出Agent操作系统（AOS）概念，将智能体控制平面集成或超越传统操作系统。定义了调度器、上下文管理、工具注册、策略执行和可观测性等职责，并映射到Linux和Windows原语。

<h3 id="462">[462] ProbMoE: Differentiable Probabilistic Routing for Mixture-of-Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01509](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01509) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Heng Zhao, Zilei Shao, Guy Van den Broeck, Zhe Zeng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Probabilistic Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对混合专家（MoE）模型中top-k路由不可微导致梯度估计困难的问题，论文提出ProbMoE，将专家选择建模为基数约束子集上的概率分布，利用精确边际概率进行梯度传递。实验表明ProbMoE Exact-k路由性能与基线相当且专家利用率更高，动态k路由以更少专家达到可比性能。

<h3 id="463">[463] Compliance-Scored Best-of-N Guardrail Orchestration for Multimodal Document Generation in Payments Dispute Defense</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01513](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01513) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nataraj Agaram Sundar, Tejas Morabia
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Guardrail, Multimodal Learning, Document Generation
- **Summary**: 针对高要求企业文档生成中模式正确性、政策合规和低延迟的需求，论文提出一个护栏编排层，结合多候选生成和合规评分用于早期退出。在支付争议防御场景中，变量队列计数胜率比对照组高11.0个百分点（p\<0.001），质量信号提升。

<h3 id="464">[464] TN-SHAP-G: Graph-Structured Tensor Network Surrogates for Shapley Values and Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01540](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01540) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Farzaneh Heidari, Guillaume Rabusseau
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Graph Neural Network, Tensor Network
- **Summary**: 针对黑盒模型中Shapley值计算需指数级子集空间的问题，论文提出TN-SHAP-G，利用图结构输入学习紧致的图对齐多线性代理（张量网络），从少量查询中恢复一阶和高阶Shapley指数。在分子基准上精确匹配精确Shapley值，并有效扩展到大规模图。

<h3 id="465">[465] Self-Conditioned Positional HNSW for Overlap-Aware Retrieval in Chunked-Document RAG Systems: Method and Industrial Evidence-Quality Audit</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01542](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01542) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nataraj Agaram Sundar, Tejas Morabia
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.DB; cs.IR
- **Tags**: RAG, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对分块文档RAG中重叠块导致重复证据的问题，提出Self-Conditioned Positional HNSW方法，通过在块嵌入中添加低维位置码和两遍查询程序减少冗余。文本审计显示574/770评分为3/5，OCR审计中干净截图通过率95%，手写模糊截图45%。

<h3 id="466">[466] GJDNet: Robust Graph Neural Networks via Joint Disentangled Learning Against Adversarial Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01560) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Canyixing Cui, Tao Wu, Xingping Xian, Xiao-Ke Xu, Mao Wang, Weina Niu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 针对图神经网络易受对抗攻击的问题，提出GJDNet，通过联合解耦节点表示和决策空间，引入球形决策边界来增强鲁棒性。实验表明在不同连接模式的图上均表现强鲁棒性。

<h3 id="467">[467] Defenses & Enablers For Skill Injection Attacks on Terminal Based Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01567](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01567) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yoshinari Fujinuma, Varun Gangal, Traian Rebedea, Makesh Narasimhan Sreedhar, Prasoon Varshney, Rebecca Qian, Anand Kannappan
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理重用技能文件带来的攻击面，评估了动态和静态守护者防御方法。在三个LLM代理家族上将攻击成功率降低一半以上，动态守护者在攻击重构下仍保持鲁棒性（ASR降至18.6%）。

<h3 id="468">[468] Identifying High-Confidence Social Biases in LLMs for Trustworthy Conversational Tutoring Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01584) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aitor Arronte Alvarez, Naiyi Xie Fincham
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Bias Mitigation
- **Venue**: AIED 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在对话教学场景中放大社会偏见的问题，提出数据集生成方法评估偏见检测。发现LLM在对话上下文中对偏见判断过度自信，且自信度影响推理和反馈，构成风险。

<h3 id="469">[469] Estimating Mutual Information between Time Series and Temporal Event Sequences Across Diverse Analysis Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01602) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haoji Hu, Huaqing Mao, Yijun Lin, Xiaowei Jia, Jinwei Zhou, Minoh Jeong, Yao-Yi Chiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Time Series Analysis, Mutual Information Estimation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/HaojiHu/Multimodal-Temporal-Data-Quantification)
- **Summary**: 针对连续时间序列与离散事件序列间依赖关系量化缺乏稳健方法的问题，提出非参数互信息估计器，无需数据转换。在因果分析、重复发现等任务上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="470">[470] FedMTFI: Feature Importance Based Optimized Multi Teacher Knowledge Distillation in Heterogeneous Federated Learning Environment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01607](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01607) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nazmus Shakib Shadin, Aaron Cummings, Xinyue Zhang, Bobin Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Venue**: IJCNN 2026
- **Summary**: 针对联邦学习中设备异构和非IID数据问题，提出FedMTFI，结合多教师知识蒸馏和SHAP特征重要性训练全局学生模型。实验表明在非IID条件下准确率优于传统FL算法。

<h3 id="471">[471] TechGraphRAG: An Agentic Graph-Augmented RAG Framework for Technical Literature Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01613) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kanwar Bharat Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对技术文献推理中单一检索不足，提出TechGraphRAG，结合知识图谱和自主检索增强生成，包含13步流水线。在约2100篇论文语料上实现证据充分的推理，并配备质量检查。

<h3 id="472">[472] EvoPool: Evolutionary Programmatic Annotation for Label-Efficient Specialized Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01617](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01617) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Xu, Yaolun Zhang, Xuan Ouyang, Huazheng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Annotation, Evolutionary Algorithm, Multi-Agent System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/tianyi0216/EvoPool)
- **Summary**: 针对专业领域标注成本高的问题，提出EvoPool进化多代理框架，通过进化注释器代码生成监督信号。在7/8任务上优于最强LLM基线，平均提升+0.141 macro-F1。

<h3 id="473">[473] Demystifying Multimodal Biomolecular Co-design With Intrinsic Geodesic Coupling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01628](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01628) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Keyue Qiu, Xintong Wang, Zhilong Zhang, Hao Zhou, Wei-Ying Ma
- **Subjects**: q-bio.BM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Drug Discovery, Protein Engineering
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对生物分子联合设计中模态间耦合不当导致高方差监督的问题，提出GeoCoupling优化异构模态间时间耦合。在药物设计和蛋白质设计任务上改进了物理有效性和多样性。

<h3 id="474">[474] A Framework for Graph-Conditioned Hierarchical Shapley Attribution in Patent Valuation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01632) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joy Bose
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Shapley Values, Knowledge Graph, Patent Valuation
- **Summary**: 针对专利估值中单个专利贡献难以量化的问题，提出PatentXAI，利用Shapley值在知识图上进行层次化归因。在n=100时运行时间10ms/专利，与参考差异0.062。

<h3 id="475">[475] E4GEN: Event-level Explainable Extreme-Enhanced Time-series Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01634](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01634) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lin Jiang, Dahai Yu, Ximiao Li, Guang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Generation, Diffusion Model, Explainable AI
- **Summary**: 针对时间序列生成中极端事件捕捉不足的问题，提出E4GEN可解释扩散框架，通过自适应激活和极端控制网络生成极端事件。在6个数据集上全面优于现有模型。

<h3 id="476">[476] AlphaToken: Decoupling Adaptation and Stability for Path-Aware Response Token Valuation in LLM Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01635](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01635) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liu Qing, Ou Wu, Yi Du
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Fine-Tuning, Preference Optimization
- **Summary**: 针对LLM后训练中token选择缺乏原则性问题，提出AlphaToken，将token价值分解为适应性和稳定性，采用路径感知和Fisher漂移代理。提升微调和偏好优化性能并缓解遗忘。

<h3 id="477">[477] Easier to Mislead Than to Correct: Harmful and Beneficial Revision in LLM Conformity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01637](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01637) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiaming Qu, Lucheng fu, Yibo Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Social Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对LLM在多智能体系统中从众的风险，研究社会线索对修正行为的影响。发现多数意见更容易误导正确模型而非纠正错误，且推理干预无法可靠减少有害修正。

<h3 id="478">[478] MINTS: Minimalist Thompson Sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01655](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01655) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaizheng Wang
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Thompson Sampling
- **Summary**: 针对贝叶斯框架中参数约束复杂的问题，提出最小化贝叶斯框架仅对最优位置设先验。MINTS算法在多臂老虎机问题上实现近最优遗憾保证，并自适应结构。

<h3 id="479">[479] DOT-MoE: Differentiable Optimal Transport for MoEfication</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01666](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01666) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Udbhav Bamba, Arnav Chavan, Aryamaan Thakur, Steve Teig, Deepak Gupta
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Model Compression, Optimal Transport
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对预训练稠密模型转换为MoE时专家划分依赖启发式方法的问题，提出DOT-MoE，利用可微最优传输学习神经元分配和路由。保留90%性能，减少50%激活参数。

<h3 id="480">[480] Time-Aware Diffusion based on Preference Disentanglement for Generative Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01670) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bangguo Zhu, Peng Huo, Yuanbo Zhao, Zhicheng Du, Jun Yin, Senzhang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 针对扩散生成推荐器忽略用户偏好时间非平稳分布的问题，提出TDPM，通过时间感知扩散和偏好解耦（周期偏好和点偏好）生成推荐。在三个数据集上HR@20提升29.21%，NDCG@20提升25.45%。

<h3 id="481">[481] Off-the-Shelf LLMs as Process Scorers: Training-Free Alternative to PRMs for Mathematical Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01682) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Atoosa Chegini, Soheil Feizi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文针对PRM需要逐步骤标签训练的问题，提出了一种无需训练的替代方案：基于块级别引导生成（Chunk-Level Guided Generation），利用现成的大语言模型作为过程评分器。在GSM8K、MATH等基准上，对比度引导选择（CGS）在匹配引导预算下超越了多数投票和PRM引导搜索，并产生了更短的推理轨迹。

<h3 id="482">[482] HAIM: Human-AI Music Datasets for AI Music Production Tracking Benchmark</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01686](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01686) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seonghyeon Go, Yumin Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Music Generation, AI-Generated Content
- **Summary**: 当前AI音乐检测局限于二分类，无法反映实际制作流程中的AI介入。论文定义了“AI音乐追踪”任务，并发布了HAIM数据集，包含多阶段AI干预标签。评估表明现有检测器存在系统性缺陷，该基准推动领域走向细粒度评估。

<h3 id="483">[483] RPCASSM: Robust PCA State Space Model For Infrared Small Target Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01689](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01689) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pingping Liu, Aohua Li, Yubing Lu, Jin Kuang, Tongshun Zhang, Qiuzhan Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: State Space Model, Infrared Small Target Detection
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/PepperCS/RPCASSM)
- **Summary**: 针对红外小目标检测中现有状态空间模型难以准确建模目标边缘的问题，提出RPCASSM网络，基于鲁棒主成分分析范式，设计背景和目标状态空间模块，分别利用空间异质信号显著性和目标稀疏高亮特性。在基准数据集上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="484">[484] Understanding Identity Continuity in Thermal Video through Scene-Level Consistency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01694) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wei-Chieh Sun, Gyungmin Ko, Heejae Kwon, Hsiang-Wei Huang, Jenq-Neng Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Object Tracking, Scene Understanding
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Workshop on SVC
- **Summary**: 热成像行人多目标追踪中因外观线索弱导致轨迹碎片化。论文通过轻量级后处理（在线短间隙重映射和离线轨迹片段重连）恢复身份连续性，并在PBVS基准上将IDF1从82.25提升至84.93。结果表明场景级时空一致性比局部帧间关联更关键。

<h3 id="485">[485] JenBridge: Adaptive Long-Form Video Soundtracking across Scene Transitions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01703](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01703) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiashuo Yu, Yao Yao, Boyu Chen, Alex Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Video Generation, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 长视频配乐面临场景过渡不连贯的挑战。提出JenBridge框架，采用基于流匹配的Transformer模型，结合双文本-视觉条件对齐，并创新性地利用LLM代理智能选择过渡风格。在LVS基准上，在过渡自然性和叙事连贯性方面显著超越现有方法。

<h3 id="486">[486] Two-Fidelity Best-Action Identification for Stochastic Minimax Tree</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01708](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01708) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peter Chen, Xi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Monte Carlo Tree Search, Best-Action Identification
- **Summary**: 在随机最小极大树中的置信固定最佳动作识别（BAI）问题中，启发式评估廉价但有偏，精确模拟昂贵。提出2FFS双保真度树搜索算法，结合最小极大扩展和MCTS采样。理论证明了固定置信度正确性，实验表明比现有BAI-MCTS基线使用更少样本。

<h3 id="487">[487] Fair Finetuning Mitigates Distribution Inference Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01719](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01719) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rakshit Naidu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Fairness, Privacy, Distribution Inference Attack
- **Summary**: 机器学习模型可能泄露训练数据的分布信息。提出公平微调（FFt）方法，在等几率约束下对互补分布样本进行微调，理论证明了对抗优势的上界。在六个数据集上，FFt将对抗准确率差距降至检测阈值以下。

<h3 id="488">[488] Post-Deterministic Distributed Systems: A New Foundation for Trustworthy Autonomous Infrastructure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01722](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01722) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jun He, Deying Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Distributed Systems, Autonomous Systems, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 自主推理引擎挑战了传统分布式系统的确定性假设。提出后确定性分布式系统（PDDS）模型，包含五个架构支柱，并定义了新的故障分类。该模型将经典分布式计算作为零歧义特例，为自主基础设施提供新基础。

<h3 id="489">[489] Shortcut to Nowhere: Demystifying Deep Spurious Regression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01723](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01723) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Guanrong Xu, Jessica Li, Hao Wang, Yuzhe Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Domain Generalization, Spurious Correlation
- **Summary**: 回归任务中的虚假相关性尚未被充分研究。定义了深度虚假回归（DSR），并提出利用标签和特征空间中虚假属性的相似性进行校准。在跨计算机视觉、环境感知和LLM回归的多个数据集上验证了优越性能。

<h3 id="490">[490] Argument Collapse: LLMs Flatten Long-Form Public Debate</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01736](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01736) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yekyung Kim, Yapei Chang, Chau Minh Pham, Mohit Iyyer
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Text Generation, Argument Collapse
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型生成公开论证时可能使辩论扁平化。研究了“论证坍塌”现象，发现人类主要论点在辩论中65.3%是唯一的，而LLM仅3.4%。即使要求多样性，LLM也只覆盖约一半的人类论点空间，且子论点更笼统。

<h3 id="491">[491] THRD: A Training-Free Multi-Turn Defense Framework for Jailbreak Attacks on Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01738](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01738) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhiqing Ma, Zhonghao Xu, Dong Yu, Chen Kang, Changliang Li, Pengyuan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Jailbreak Attack, Multi-Turn Interaction
- **Summary**: 多轮越狱攻击利用对话动态，现有防御忽略时间累积。提出THRD框架，显式建模时间风险累积，包括回合级风险评估、历史上下文分析、响应评估和决策模块。将攻击成功率降至0.2%-4.0%，且模型效用损失小于1.5%。

<h3 id="492">[492] SECUREVENT: Hybrid AI/ML Security Monitoring for Distributed Event-Based Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01741) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Federated Learning, Cybersecurity
- **Summary**: 分布式事件系统攻击面扩大。提出SECUREVENT混合AI/ML安全监控架构，结合认证传输、主题授权、在线异常检测、图特征、复杂事件规则和联邦学习。在合成攻击上原型验证显示混合监控提高了召回率并保持低假阳性率。

<h3 id="493">[493] Construction of Historical Knowledge Graphs Based on BERT and Graph Neural Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01747](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01747) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ping Li, Bartlomiej Brzozka
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Graph Neural Network, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 历史文本存在语言歧义和上下文限制。提出结合BERT和图神经网络的架构，用于知识图谱构建。在市政记录、议会文件和历史通信数据集上，该联合系统在精确率、召回率和F1分数上优于传统方法和深度学习基线。

<h3 id="494">[494] Adaptive Auto-Harness: Sustained Self-Improvement for Agentic System Deployment on Open-Ended Task Streams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01770](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01770) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zewen Liu, Zhan Shi, Yisi Sang, Bing He, Minhua Lin, Tianxin Wei, Dakuo Wang, Benoit Dumoulin, Wei Jin, Hanqing Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Self-Improvement
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/A-EVO-Lab/AdaptiveHarness)
- **Summary**: 自动优化系统在开放任务流上因分布变化而性能下降。提出自适应自动优化框架，包含有状态多智能体进化器、优化树和人工干预钩子。在预测市场、安全竞赛和事件预测流上优于五种基线。

<h3 id="495">[495] FLARE: Diffusion for Hybrid Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01774](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01774) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuchen Zhu, Jing Shi, Chongjian Ge, Hao Tan, Yiran Xu, Wanrong Zhu, Jason Kuen, Koustava Goswami, Rajiv Jain, Yongxin Chen, Molei Tao, Jiuxiang Gu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, LLM Inference, Diffusion Language Model
- **Summary**: 自回归LLM解码瓶颈，混合注意力和扩散语言模型结合困难。提出FLARE转换框架，支持同一检查点进行AR和扩散解码。在多个规模上，FLARE与开源dLLM竞争力相当，并在单GPU并发服务中实现持续吞吐量提升。

<h3 id="496">[496] Breaking the Information Silo: Semantic Personas for Cross-Domain Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01783) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jonathan Mayo, Moshe Unger, Konstantin Bauman
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Cross-Domain Recommendation
- **Summary**: 跨域推荐常需共享用户或物品，不现实。提出SPHERE，利用LLM诱导共享行为词汇，生成语义用户画像，并通过社区画像传递知识。在Amazon Books、Goodreads和Steam上，一致优于NCF、SVD++和LightGCN。

<h3 id="497">[497] STaR-KV: Spatio-Temporal Adaptive Re-weighting for KV Cache Compression in GUI Vision-Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="498">[498] Multilinguality of Large Language Models From a Structural Perspective</h3>

<h3 id="508">[508] Boosting Multimodal Federated Learning via Chained Modality Optimization</h3>

<h3 id="509">[509] RadioMaster: Multi-Agent System for Autonomous Radio Signal Generation</h3>

<h3 id="512">[512] RA-LWLM: Retrieval-Augmented In-Context Localization with Wireless Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01899](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01899) (cross-listed)
