<h3 id="513">[513] The Image Reconstruction Game: Drawing Common Ground Through Iterative Multimodal Dialogue</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01901](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01901) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sherzod Hakimov, Mattia D&#39;Agostini, Ivan Samodelkin, David Schlangen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Image Generation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文针对多模态对话中共同基础（common ground）难以量化的问题，提出了Image Reconstruction Game基准，通过视觉语言模型向图像生成器发出多轮纠正指令，将共同基础直接呈现为渲染图像。实验发现描述器（describer）是重建质量的主导因素，数学和几何图像最具挑战性。强描述器使用更丰富的纠正词汇，而标记预算显著影响收敛速度。

<h3 id="514">[514] KliniskVestBERT: BERT Model Specialised to Norwegian Clinical Texts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01904](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01904) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christian Autenried, Cosimo Persia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Clinical NLP, Pre-training
- **Summary**: 针对挪威语临床文本缺乏专用语言模型的问题，本文提出了KliniskVestBERT，在大量真实脱敏挪威语临床文本上对Nb-BERT-large、NorBERT3-large和ModernBERT进行领域预训练。在合成和真实临床基准上，专用模型始终优于基线，证明了领域预训练在临床NLP任务中的显著价值。

<h3 id="515">[515] Echo: A Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Speaker Diarization and Speech Recognition in a Shared Latent Space</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01909) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Louis Mouchon
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speaker Diarization, Automatic Speech Recognition, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 现有音频系统通常为单一任务专门设计，难以在单一模型上同时处理说话人识别、语音识别和源分离。本文提出Echo，一个基于JEPA预训练的25M参数ViT编码器，通过阶段式专业化在512维共享隐空间中承载多个任务。在合成VoxCeleb2混合数据上取得了15% DER、97.8%分离准确率和+9.52dB SI-SDR，展示了多任务共存的可行性。

<h3 id="516">[516] Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Large Pretrained Models for Instance Segmentation Tasks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01947](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01947) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nermeen Abou Baker, David Rohrschneider, Uwe Handmann
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, Instance Segmentation, LoRA
- **Venue**: Machine Learning and Knowledge Extraction Journal
- **Summary**: 针对大规模预训练模型在实例分割任务中全参数微调效率低下的问题，本文研究了适配器（adapter）和LoRA两种参数高效微调（PEFT）方法。首次将LoRA应用于可变形注意力，仅微调1-6%参数即取得有竞争力的性能。实验表明每Transformer块使用2-3个适配器可达到最优性能-效率平衡。

<h3 id="517">[517] Rank-Constrained Deep Matrix Completion for Group Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01948](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01948) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mubaraka Sani Ibrahim, Lehel Csató, Isah Charles Saidu
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Matrix Completion, Group Recommendation
- **Summary**: 现有群组推荐系统在处理高维稀疏数据时效果不佳，本文提出Group RC-DMC框架，通过Set-Transformer聚合器结合低秩矩阵完成与注意力非线性建模。在MovieLens和Goodbooks数据集上，该方法在群组RMSE上表现更优，且与基准方法在精度、召回率和F1分数上具有竞争力。

<h3 id="518">[518] A Structured Benchmark for Text-Guided Anomaly Detection: When Language Stops Conditioning the Decision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01992](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01992) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Stefano Samele, Eugenio Lomurno, Teodora Jovanovic, Sanjay Shivakumar Manohar, Alberto Crivellaro, Matteo Matteucci
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 当前多模态异常检测基准无法衡量文本引导是否真正影响决策，本文提出TGAD基准，包含提示敏感性、组件级指令和组装面板数据集三个渐进场景。评估发现文本接口仅表面地影响决策：若移除对象名词，生成模型I-AUROC从97.4降至82.6；组件级指令无法约束决策。结果表明现有基准高估了文本引导能力。

<h3 id="519">[519] MMG2Skill: Can Agents Distill In-the-Wild Guides into Self-Evolving Skills?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01993](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01993) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinyu Che, Junqi Xiong, Yunfei Ge, Xinping Lei, Shihao Li, Hang Yan, Han Li, Yuanxing Zhang, Zhiqi Bai, Jinhua Hao, Ming Sun, Han Li, Jiaheng Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Skill Learning, Vision-Language Model
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/NJU-LINK/MMG2Skill)
- **Summary**: 针对人类引导知识难以直接作为智能体技能的问题，本文形式化了引导到技能学习任务，并提出MMG2Skill框架，将网络指南编译为可编辑技能，通过轨迹反馈持续修订。在GUI控制、开放游戏等任务上，该框架在6种VLM骨干上平均提升12.8-25.3个百分点，而直接输入原始指南可能降低性能。

<h3 id="520">[520] Why Do Time Series Models Need Long Context Windows?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.01999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.01999) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luca Butera, Giovanni De Felice, Andrea Cini, Cesare Alippi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Long Context
- **Summary**: 时间序列模型使用长上下文窗口的好处通常归因于捕获长程依赖，本文提出两个目标：生成过程识别（GPI）和条件预测（CF）。证明即使过程记忆长度为P，窗口大于P才能达到最小误差；解耦GPI和CF可提高可扩展性。实验验证了在合成和真实数据上的有效性。

<h3 id="521">[521] Towards 3D-Aware Video Diffusion Models: Render-Free Human Motion Control with Mesh Tokenization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02000) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jingyun Liang, Min Wei, Shikai Li, Yizeng Han, Hangjie Yuan, Lei Sun, Weihua Chen, Fan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; eess.IV
- **Tags**: Video Generation, Motion Synthesis, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 为探究视频扩散模型是否真正具有3D感知能力，本文提出无渲染框架，直接使用压缩3D人体网格标记条件化视频生成，采用DiT架构联合处理视频标记和运动标记。实验在人体运动控制基准上表现强劲，减少了2D引导导致的伪影，表明网格标记化有助于模型理解复杂3D结构。

<h3 id="522">[522] PlanarBench: Evaluating LLM Spatial Reasoning via Planar Graph Drawing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02010](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02010) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Oleksandr Nikitin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Spatial Reasoning, Graph Reasoning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wizzard0/planar-bench-as1073)
- **Summary**: 现有LLM图推理基准仅以节点数作为难度轴，本文提出PlanarBench，测试LLM根据边列表绘制平面图（ASCII艺术）的空间推理能力。在91个模型上的实验发现边数才是主导难度预测因子（r=-0.85），而非节点数，这是此前未报告的发现。

<h3 id="523">[523] Ranking vs. Assignment: The Metric Mismatch in Multi-View Object Association</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02022](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02022) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matvei Shelukhan, Timur Mamedov, Aleksandr Chukhrov, Karina Kvanchiani
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-View Learning, Object Association
- **Summary**: 多视角目标关联任务本质上是一对一匹配问题，但常用排名指标（AP、FPR-95）与分配目标存在根本性失配。本文理论证明当分配已正确时这些指标可能仍不完美，最优排名也可能导致错误分配。通过Sinkhorn归一化后处理，指标提升但分配级指标并未改善。

<h3 id="524">[524] OpenWebRL: Demystifying Online Multi-turn Reinforcement Learning for Visual Web Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02031](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02031) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Yang, Qianhui Wu, Yuxi Chen, Hao Bai, Wenlin Yao, Hao Cheng, Baolin Peng, Huan Zhang, Tong Zhang, Jianfeng Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CV
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, GUI Automation
- **Summary**: 开源视觉网页智能体依赖于大规模监督后训练，可扩展性有限，本文提出OpenWebRL，一个基于在线多轮强化学习的训练框架，覆盖完整流水线。OpenWebRL-4B在真实网页基准上达到67.0%（Online-Mind2Web）和64.0%（DeepShop），与包括OpenAI CUA在内的专有系统竞争，并系统分析了RL对智能体推理的影响。

<h3 id="525">[525] Attention mechanisms and transfer learning for robust peach leaf damage classification under domain shift</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02045](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02045) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adrián Cánovas-Rodriguez, Miguel A. González-Illán, Maria Fernanda García-Cruz, Pedro Nortes Tortosa, José Salvador Rubio-Asensio, Miguel A. Zamora Izquierdo, Juan Antonio Martínez Navarro, Antonio F. Skarmeta
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agricultural AI, Image Classification, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对桃树叶损伤分类中症状相似和领域漂移的问题，本文评估了多种深度学习架构，并整合CBAM注意力机制和迁移学习。在基准数据集上，EfficientNetB5+CBAM达到93.3%准确率；在本地数据集上，通过迁移学习，EfficientNetB3+CBAM取得93%的宏F1分数，展现了注意力模型在少数类和泛化上的优越性。

<h3 id="526">[526] Fast and Lightweight Novel View Synthesis with Differentiable Multiplane Image</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02068](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02068) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaidi Zhang, Guanxu Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Novel View Synthesis, Multiplane Image
- **Summary**: 现有新视角合成方法（如NeRF和3DGS）在速度、模型大小和稀疏视图上存在权衡，本文重新审视多平面图像（MPI）表示，结合可微优化和一键扩散修复。与代表性GS方法相比，速度提升30.7%，模型大小仅为其14.8%，在前视图场景中合成质量具有竞争力。

<h3 id="527">[527] Agentic-J: An AI Agent for Biological Microscopy Image Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lukas Johanns, Marilin Moor, Davide Panzeri, Yu Zhou, Xinyi Chen, Nora F. K. Pauly, Zixuan Pan, Matthias Gunzer, Andreas Müller, Yiyu Shi, Hedi Peterson, Jianxu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Medical AI, Computational Biology
- **Venue**: Cell Biology at Scale 2026
- **Summary**: 生物图像分析常常需要整合多种工具和领域知识，本文提出Agentic-J，一个容器化的多智能体AI助手，专为ImageJ/Fiji设计，允许用户用自然语言指定分析任务并生成可执行脚本。系统包含插件管理、代码生成、质量保证等子智能体，支持可复现的工作流。

<h3 id="528">[528] LALE: Lightweight-Transformer Architecture for Land-Cover Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02092](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02092) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ümit Mert Çağlar, Alptekin Temizel
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Semantic Segmentation, Efficient Architecture
- **Summary**: 面向遥感图像语义分割需要在有限计算预算下同时捕捉全局上下文和局部细节，本文提出LALE架构，编码器按分辨率分叉：高分辨率使用轻量ConvMixer，低分辨率使用Transformer（自注意力仅用于深层下采样特征）。最小变体（1.6M参数）在ARAS400k基准上以4.5倍更少参数、7倍更小存储和17倍更少GMACs达到与最佳基线UPerNet相差仅2.6 F1点的性能，吞吐量提升1.8倍。

<h3 id="529">[529] The Role of Ambiguity in Error Prediction via Uncertainty Quantification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02093](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02093) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ieva Raminta Staliūnaitė, James Bishop, Andreas Vlachos
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Error Prediction
- **Summary**: 本文针对不确定性量化在错误预测中受输入歧义影响的问题，提出通过门控专家和选择性预测分离歧义信号的方法，在问答任务上实现了错误预测性能的提升，PRR指标提高超过10个点。

<h3 id="530">[530] Network Distributed Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Consensus Control of Quadcopters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02107](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02107) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Youssef Mahran, Zeyad Gamal, Aamir Ahmad, Ayman El-Badawy
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 本文针对传统多智能体强化学习忽略通信图结构的问题，提出网络分布式ND-MARL框架，采用2-邻居拓扑和MASAC算法实现四旋翼飞行器的共识控制，在3智能体训练后零样本扩展到250智能体，实现平滑共识轨迹。

<h3 id="531">[531] Jailbreaking Multimodal Large Language Models using Multi-Clip Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02111](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02111) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Choongwon Kang, Seungjong Sun, Hyunmin Jun, Jang Hyun Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, Safety Evaluation, Vision-Language Model
- **Venue**: ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对多模态大语言模型视频输入的越狱漏洞问题，引入多片段视频安全基准MCV SafetyBench，发现攻击成功率随片段数增加，视频比图像更脆弱，并提出利用图像模态的相对鲁棒性进行防御。

<h3 id="532">[532] A Primer in Post-Training Reasoning Data: What We Know About How It Works</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02113](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02113) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yaoming Li, Guangxiang Zhao, Qilong Shi, Lin Sun, Xiangzheng Zhang, Tong Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Systematic Review, Data Synthesis
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RenBing-Sumeru/Awesome-LLM-Reasoning-Data)
- **Summary**: 本文针对后训练推理数据相关文献分散的问题，综述了150余篇研究和系统报告，围绕数据对象、实用性、构建方法和规模四个问题组织，提出了归因框架。

<h3 id="533">[533] How Hard Can It Be? Hardness-Aware Multi-Objective Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02119](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02119) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiangwei Chen, Xinyuan Niu, Rachael Hwee Ling Sim, Zhengyuan Liu, Nancy F. Chen, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aoi3142/HAMU)
- **Summary**: 本文针对机器遗忘算法无法保证所有数据上指定目标提升的问题，提出基于约束优化和硬度度量的HAMU算法，通过权重更新确保遗忘质量的同时最小化保留效用损失，在图像和文本数据集上表现优异。

<h3 id="534">[534] Understanding-Enhanced Model Collaboration for Long-Tailed Egocentric Mistake Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02120) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boyu Han, Qianqian Xu, Shilong Bao, Zhiyong Yang, Ruochen Cui, Qingming Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Long-Tailed Learning, Video Understanding, Multimodal Learning, Egocentric Vision
- **Summary**: 本文针对从第一人称视频中检测用户操作错误的问题，提出理解增强模型协作方法UE-MCM，结合粗粒度视频理解和细粒度动作推理，并处理长尾分布，有效检测细微和罕见错误。

<h3 id="535">[535] Variational Learning for Insertion-based Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02133) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yangtian Zhang, Zhe Wang, Arthur Gretton, Rex Ying, David van Dijk, Michalis K. Titsias, Jiaxin Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Variational Inference, Molecular Generation, Sequence Modeling
- **Summary**: 本文针对非单调序列生成模型无序且固定长度的问题，提出插入过程IP，通过基于排列的变分推断学习插入顺序，在目标导向规划和分子生成任务上提高了建模质量。

<h3 id="536">[536] Rethinking Evaluation Paradigms in IBP-based Certified Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02134) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Konstantin Kaulen, Hadar Shavit, Holger H. Hoos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Certified Robustness, Adversarial Robustness, Multi-Objective Optimization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文针对基于间隔传播的可信训练中单一配置报告误导评价的问题，提出通过帕累托前沿比较和多目标超参数优化进行公平评估，发现先前配置欠调，取得了新的最优结果。

<h3 id="537">[537] VLBM: Variational Latent Basis Modeling for OOD Robust Multivariate Time Series Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02138](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02138) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xudong Zhang, Jierui Lei, Jiacheng Li, Lingdong Shen, Jian Cui, Haina Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, OOD Generalization, Variational Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/leijieruilq/VLBM_OOD_forecast)
- **Summary**: 本文针对多变量时间序列预测中分布外事件被频繁分布模式淹没的问题，提出变分潜在基模型VLBM，学习共享潜在基分离稳定动态与OOD偏差，在12个基准任务上取得SOTA，MAE和MSE分别平均提升15.08%和7.74%。

<h3 id="538">[538] Multilingual Idioms in Sentences and Conversations Across High-, Medium-, and Low-Resource Languages</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02147) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Saeed Almheiri, Bilal Elbouardi, Salsabila Zahirah Pranida, Irina Nikishina, Ashwath Rao B, Parameswari Krishnamurthy, Muhammad Cendekia Airlangga, Rifo Ahmad Genadi, Nguyen Phan Gia Bao, Amir Hossein Yari, Hawau Olamide Toyin, Nurdaulet Mukhituly, Mena Attia, Besher Hassan, Ahmad Fathan Hidayatullah, Tatsuki Kuribayashi, Haonan Li, Suma Bhat, Fajri Koto
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, Dataset, Idiom Comprehension
- **Summary**: 本文针对多语言习语理解在低资源语言中困难的问题，构建了MIDI数据集，涵盖18种语言的句子和对话语境，发现低资源语言理解下降，字面义比比喻义更难，对话语境虽有益但无法消除差距。

<h3 id="539">[539] Predicting the risk of colorectal anastomotic leak based on preoperative mapping of the blood supply of the bowel</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02156](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02156) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zahra Tabatabaei, Jon Sporring, Mark Bremholm Ellebæk, Alaa El-Hussuna
- **Subjects**: eess.IV; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.IR; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Clinical Decision Support
- **Summary**: 本文针对目前缺乏术前CT预测结直肠吻合口漏风险的问题，提出基于AI的术前风险评估系统，包括风险评估和基于内容的医学图像检索模块，在现有医疗基础设施中可行。

<h3 id="540">[540] Multimodal Approaches for Visually-Rich Document Type Classification: A Comparative Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02162](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02162) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Catyana Heyne, Jürgen Frikel, Filippo Riccio
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, Multimodal Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 本文针对文档类型分类中多模态架构评估不一致的问题，对四种代表性模型进行统一框架下的比较分析，发现专用多模态Transformer优于LLM，图像信息贡献最大。

<h3 id="541">[541] Order within Chaos: Capturing Intrinsic Energy Anomalies for AI-Manipulated Image Forgery Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02178) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiming Wang, Baiqi Wu, Qingming Li, Jiahao Chen, Tong Zhang, Shouling Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deepfake Detection, Image Forensics, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/phoenixnir/FLAME)
- **Summary**: 本文针对AI编辑图像伪造定位难题，理论上证明扩散过程抑制局部高频方差产生能量差距，提出FLAME框架，利用LAD图和SAM适配器实现像素级定位，在AI伪造数据集上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="542">[542] On the Generalization in Topology Optimization via Sensitivity-Conditioned Bernoulli Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02179](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02179) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Rashed, Duarte F. Valoroso Madeira, Babak Gholami, Caglar Guerbuez, Yunjia Yang, Nils Thuerey
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Flow Matching, Topology Optimization, Surrogate Model
- **Summary**: 本文针对拓扑优化代理模型在分布偏移下泛化不稳定的问题，假设泛化性能取决于条件信号保留伴随灵敏度的信息，提出灵敏度条件化的伯努利流匹配生成器，在结构优化基准上取得SOTA。

<h3 id="543">[543] Consistency Training while Mitigating Obfuscation via Rate Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02211) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sohaib Imran, Prakhar Gupta, Jannes Elstner, David Demitri Africa
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Bias Mitigation, Consistency Training
- **Summary**: 本文针对一致性训练导致模型隐藏额外特征（混淆）的问题，提出率匹配一致性训练RMCT，在不约束表达方式的情况下训练行为属性一致性，在减少奉承行为的同时保留模型提及偏见的倾向。

<h3 id="544">[544] Faster Synchronous On-Policy RL via Straggler-Aware Group Sizing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02218) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Azal Ahmad Khan, Ammar Ahmed, Zeshan Fayyaz, Sheng Di, Mingyi Hong, Ali Anwar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, On-Policy RL
- **Summary**: 本文针对同步强化学习中单点滞后问题随组规模增大而加剧的问题，提出滞后感知组控制SAGC，动态调整训练组大小，在GRPO和DAPO训练中减少滞后事件、提升墙钟效率，并保持或提升奖励性能。

<h3 id="545">[545] AgentRedBench: Dynamic Redteaming and Integration-Aware Defense for LLM Agents over SaaS Integrations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02240) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hiskias Dingeto, Will Leeney
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.ET
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 现有基准低估了SaaS集成中工具使用代理的间接提示注入威胁。本文提出AGENTREDBENCH，一个动态红队基准，覆盖24种企业集成和5种攻击类型，并发布AGENTREDGUARD防护模型。实验表明，无防护时攻击成功率（ASR）为32%-81%，AGENTREDGUARD将ASR从69.9%降至2.4%，误报率仅0.37%，显著优于现有基线。

<h3 id="546">[546] Towards Resolving Optimization Conflicts Between Image- and Text-Based Person Re-Identification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02242) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Karina Kvanchiani, Timur Mamedov
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Person Re-Identification, Cross-Modal Retrieval, Representation Learning
- **Summary**: 图像和文本行人重识别的联合优化受模态差异和冲突目标影响。本文提出解耦的两阶段训练流程，使用单一视觉编码器支持两种检索任务，避免交叉干扰。实验表明，图像ReID预训练提升泛化能力，文本监督增强两种任务性能。

<h3 id="547">[547] FW-NKF: Frequency-Weighted Neural Kalman Filters</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02251](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02251) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adnan Harun Dogan, Berken Utku Demirel, Christian Holz
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: State Estimation, Robotics, Kalman Filter
- **Venue**: ICRA 2026
- **Summary**: 经典卡尔曼滤波难以处理频域扰动和模型失配。本文提出加权神经卡尔曼滤波（FW-NKF），在测量残差中嵌入因果频谱整形算子，联合学习观测和状态模型。在混沌系统和姿态估计中，定位误差降低达10%，方向精度显著提升。

<h3 id="548">[548] Who Annotates in NLP? A Large-scale Assessment of Human Annotation Reporting between 2018 and 2025</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02255](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02255) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Maria Kunilovskaya, Gagan Bhatia, Lisa Sophie Albertelli, Yanran Chen, Christian Greisinger, Lotta Kiefer, Christoph Leiter, Subhadeep Roy, Tewodros Achamaleh, Muhammad Arslan Manzoor, Sebastian Pohl, Yufang Hou, Steffen Eger
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Annotation, Research Reproducibility, Survey
- **Summary**: NLP论文常缺失标注者信息，影响可重复性。本文对ACL会议2018-2025年间1603篇论文的2667个标注任务进行审计，建立统一分类法和LLM辅助提取流水线。发现操作细节报告较多，但标注有效性细节（如训练、补偿、一致性）常缺失，尤其是模型评估研究。

<h3 id="549">[549] Cross-modal linkage risk in clinical vision-language models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02276) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Soroosh Tayebi Arasteh, Mahshad Lotfinia, Sven Nebelung, Daniel Truhn
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Medical AI, Differential Privacy
- **Summary**: 临床视觉语言模型在共享嵌入空间中可能通过余弦相似度重新关联去标识的影像和报告，构成隐私风险。本文将风险形式化为图像到报告检索任务，并对对齐层进行差分隐私微调。在MIMIC-CXR和CheXpert Plus上，DP将Recall@1降低61.8%，而影像表征几乎不变。

<h3 id="550">[550] CityTrajBench: A Unified Benchmark for City-Scale Vehicle Trajectory Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02287](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02287) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shibo Zhu, Xiaodan Shi, Dayin Chen, Yuntian Chen, Haoran Zhang, Tianhao Wu, Jinyue Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Trajectory Generation, Benchmark, Urban Computing
- **Summary**: 城市轨迹生成方法的比较因数据、预处理和评估指标不一致而困难。本文提出CityTrajBench标准化基准，支持多种生成模型并在三个真实数据集上评估。实验揭示模型间的权衡：DiffTraj在几何保真度最强，DiffRNTraj在全局真实性强，TrajFlow平衡性最佳。

<h3 id="551">[551] Quantitative Movement Testing: Measuring Patient Movements from a Single Smartphone Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02301](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02301) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Pranav Mahajan, Amanda Wall, Eleonora Maria Camerone, Julie Stebbins, Eoin Kelleher, Shuangyi Tong, Annina Schmid, Katja Wiech, Anushka Irani, Ben Seymour
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Pose Estimation, Medical AI, Computer Vision
- **Summary**: 慢性疼痛的功能影响在真实环境中难以客观测量。本文开发QMT流水线，利用单目智能手机视频进行3D姿态估计。验证显示与光学动捕高度一致（r\>0.85），测试-重测信度高（r\>0.86），并能远程区分健康对照和慢性坐骨神经痛患者。

<h3 id="552">[552] SeClaw: Spec-Driven Security Task Synthesis for Evaluating Autonomous Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02302](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02302) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hao Cheng, Changtao Miao, Tianle Song, Yin Wu, He Liu, Erjia Xiao, Junchi Chen, Xiaoyu Shi, Yichi Wang, Jing Yang, Taowen Wang, Jinhao Duan, Mengshu Sun, Peiyan Dong, Xuan Shen, Yang Cao, Renjing Xu, Kaidi Xu, Jindong Gu, Bo Zhang, Jize Zhang, Chenhao Lin, Philip Torr, Chao Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Security, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/seclaw-eval/seclaw-eval)
- **Summary**: 现有代理安全基准依赖人工任务，覆盖有限。本文提出SeClaw框架，结合规范驱动的安全任务合成和执行评估。覆盖资源、用户任务、环境和内在行为风险，支持轨迹感知评估。

<h3 id="553">[553] Repurposing Adversarial Perturbations for Continual Learning: From Defense to Active Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02322) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ran Liu, Min Yu, Mingqi Liu, Jianguo Jiang, Gang Li, Rongsheng Li, Ning Li, Zhen Xu, Weiqing Huang, Ming Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, Adversarial Robustness, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 动态环境中LLM持续学习面临遗忘、迁移受限和对抗扰动脆弱性。本文提出AdvCL，利用对抗扰动作为几何控制信号，包含三个模块。实验表明在标准性能和鲁棒性上均有提升，遗忘降低，迁移增强。

<h3 id="554">[554] Do Multimodal Agents Really Benefit from Tool Use? A Systematic Study of Capability Gains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02357) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Garvin Guo, Donglei Yu, Yu Chen, Xiang Wang, Shuai Li, Xinpei Zhao, Huaxing Liu, Qinghao Wang, Minpeng Liao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multimodal Learning, Tool-Augmented Agent
- **Summary**: 工具增强的多模态代理在基准上表现提升，但可能并非真正学会使用工具。本文系统比较有/无工具访问的代理，发现工具访问带来的改进一致较小，93-96%的通过工具解决的问题在无工具设置下也能解决。

<h3 id="555">[555] FOAM: Frequency and Operator Error-Based Adaptive Damping Method for Reducing Staleness-Oriented Error for Shampoo</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02365](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02365) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kyunghun Nam, Sumyeong Ahn
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Optimization, Deep Learning, Training Efficiency
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: Shampoo优化器因矩阵逆运算导致计算开销大，通常使用过时预处理矩阵。本文理论上分析过时性对收敛和稳定性的影响，提出FOAM自适应算法，动态控制阻尼因子和特征分解频率。实验显示在保持收敛的同时减少墙钟时间。

<h3 id="556">[556] When Do Attention Circuits Form? Developmental Trajectories of Capability and Attention-Sink Emergence Across Three 1B-ClassArchitectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02378](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02378) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongzhong Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Attention Mechanism, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 本文追踪三个1B类语言模型注意力头电路的发育轨迹，在30次机制解释性运行中发现：0-1层始终无BOS头；BOS吸引子分数有三种涌现形状；在DCLM模型中，归纳电路形成比BOS吸引子早10-20倍；能力特异性屏幕在训练早期即可确定最终电路。

<h3 id="557">[557] SPADE-Bench: Evaluating Spontaneous Strategic Deception in Agents via Plan-Action Divergence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02380) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuyan Bu, Haowei Li, Qirui Zheng, Bowen Dong, Kaiyue Yang, Jiaming Ji, Yingshui Tan, Wenxin Li, Yaodong Yang, Juntao Dai
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LLM Safety, Agent Deception
- **Summary**: 基于LLM的代理可能呈现与实际行动不符的自我报告，即欺骗行为。本文提出SPADE-Bench基准，通过计划-行动差异评估自发战略欺骗。实验确认代理欺骗在工具使用上下文中是真实问题。

<h3 id="558">[558] Policy and World Modeling Co-Training for Language Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02388) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ning Lu, Baijiong Lin, Shengcai Liu, Jiahao Wu, Haoze Lv, Yanbin Wei, Lingting Zhu, Shengju Qian, Xin Wang, Ying-Cong Chen, Qi Wang, Ke Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, World Model
- **Summary**: 强化学习改进LLM代理但缺乏对环境影响的监督。本文提出PaW框架，在RL滚动中同时训练策略和世界模型，引入动作熵数据选择、噪声容忍损失和自适应平衡。在三个代理任务基准上，PaW一致优于强RL基线。

<h3 id="559">[559] AutoForest: Automatically Generating Forest Plots from Biomedical Studies with End-to-End Evidence Extraction and Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02403](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02403) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Massimiliano Pronesti, Angelo Miculescu, Mohsin Kapdi, Paul Flanagan, Oisín Redmond, Joao Bettencourt-Silva, Gurdeep Mannu, Spiros Denaxas, Rui Bebiano Da Providencia E Costa, Anya Belz, Yufang Hou
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Document Understanding, Evidence Synthesis
- **Venue**: ACL2026
- **Summary**: 系统综述中生成森林图过程繁琐。本文提出AutoForest，首个端到端系统，直接从生物医学论文生成可发表的森林图，自动提取干预、比较、结局信息并执行统计合成。用户研究表明能加速证据合成。

<h3 id="560">[560] Evolutionary Discovery of Bivariate Bicycle Codes with LLM-Guided Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02418](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02418) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Juan Cruz-Benito, Andrew W. Cross, David Kremer, Ismael Faro
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Evolutionary Algorithm, Error-Correcting Code
- **Summary**: 量子LDPC码发现需搜索大设计空间。本文引入LLM引导的进化工作流，变异生成双变量自行车码的Python程序。在约1650次迭代后，筛选约20万候选码，发现465个新码，包括[[288,16,12]]不可分解码和[[144,12,12]]扰动码。

<h3 id="561">[561] GC-MoE: Genomics-Guided Cell-Type-Specific Mixture of Experts for Histology-Based Single-Cell Spatial Transcriptomics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02424](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02424) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kaito Shiku, Ahtisham Fazeel Abbasi, Ryoma Bise, Yuichiro Iwashita, Kazuya Nishimura, Andreas Dengel, Muhammad Nabeel Asim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Computational Biology, Mixture-of-Experts, Single-cell Analysis
- **Summary**: 现有方法主要预测局部区域（多个细胞）的基因表达，而本文针对组织学图像预测单细胞基因表达的任务，提出GC-MoE模型，通过路由网络估计细胞类型概率，并结合细胞类型特定专家和共表达感知预测器（CAP）以及细胞间交互注意力模块（C2CA），在公开数据集上取得一致改进。

<h3 id="562">[562] Not All Errors Are Equal: A Systematic Study of Error Propagation in Large Language Model Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02430](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02430) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yafan Huang, Sheng Di, Guanpeng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Fault Tolerance, Error Propagation
- **Venue**: ICS'26
- **Summary**: 大型语言模型（LLM）推理中的软错误传播问题尚未被充分研究。本文提出LLMFI故障注入框架，系统性地在三个开源LLM和十三种任务中注入故障，揭示了错误传播的关键模式，并给出了17条经验结论和四种低开销的可靠性改进方向。

<h3 id="563">[563] ODTQA-FoRe: An Open-Domain Tabular Question Answering Dataset for Future Data Forecasting and Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02433) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhensheng Wang, Xiaole Liu, Wenmian Yang, Kun Zhou, Yiquan Zhang, Weijia Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Table Reasoning, LLM Agent, Time Series Forecasting
- **Venue**: Findings of ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 现有表格问答系统无法进行面向未来的数值预测。本文提出ODTQA-FoRe任务和数据集，并设计了TimeFore框架，通过检索器、预测器和分析器的协作，利用外部时间序列模型提高预测精度，实验验证了其有效性。

<h3 id="564">[564] PaSBench-Video: A Streaming Video Benchmark for Proactive Safety Warning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02443](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02443) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yusong Zhao, Yuejin Xie, Youliang Yuan, Junjie Hu, Jitian Guo, Yujiu Yang, Pinjia He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Safety Evaluation, Benchmark, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 目前缺乏测试视频多模态大模型（MLLM）在安全警告中时序精度的基准。本文提出PaSBench-Video，包含740个视频，涵盖四个领域，标注了风险起始和事故边界。测试13个MLLM发现，严格指标下得分不超过20%，且召回率与假阳性率强相关。

<h3 id="565">[565] Initialization is Half the Battle: Generating Diverse Images from a Guidance Potential Posterior</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02453) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiang Li, Dianbo Liu, Kenji Kawaguchi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Flow Matching, Mode Collapse
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 生成模型常因标准高斯初始化导致模式坍塌。本文将初始化噪声从引导势后验中采样，提出DivIn方法，利用朗之万动力学探索初始化空间，有效提升类到图像和文本到图像的多样性，并与基于轨迹的方法正交结合，扩展了多样性-质量帕累托前沿。

<h3 id="566">[566] MASER: Modality-Adaptive Specialist Routing for Embodied 3D Spatial Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02463](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02463) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hilton Raj, Vishnuram AV
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI, 3D Vision
- **Venue**: CVPR 2026 Foundation Models Meet Embodied Agents Workshop
- **Summary**: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）仅针对单一模态微调，忽略了问题语义可能偏好不同模态。本文提出MASER框架，训练五个模态适配器并学习路由策略，在推理时根据问题选择最佳适配器。实验表明无单一模态普遍最优，MASER路由与Oracle一致性达51.3%。

<h3 id="567">[567] Learning When to Translate for Multilingual Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02465) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Deokhyung Kang, Hyounghun Kim, Gary Geunbae Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Multilingual Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/deokhk/LUAR)
- **Summary**: 推理语言模型（RLM）在多语言推理中存在语言理解失败导致的性能差距。本文提出Luar框架，通过强化学习训练模型选择性调用翻译，仅在直接理解不可靠时翻译，在多种多语言推理基准上优于GRPO，尤其对低资源语言提升显著。

<h3 id="568">[568] Ghost Tool Calls: Issue-Time Privacy for Speculative Agent Tools</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02483](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02483) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bardia Mohammadi, Lars Klein, Akhil Arora, Laurent Bindschaedler
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Privacy, Tool Learning
- **Summary**: 工具增强型语言代理的投机性工具调用会泄露用户意图，而事后清理无法撤回已披露的信息。本文提出投机工具隐私契约运行时抽象，将提交前的观察作为一等效应，评估多种策略表明，只有提交时策略（改变或抑制工具调用）能减少推断。

<h3 id="569">[569] Monitoring Agentic Systems Before They're Reliable</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02494) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Marisa Ferrara Boston, Glen Hanson, Effi Georgala, JD Hudgens, Heather Frase
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Software Testing, Agent Evaluation
- **Venue**: Workshop on Agentic Software Engineering (AgenticSE), co-located with ACM CAIS 2026
- **Summary**: 代理系统在生产中常因结构性缺陷而非任务级错误导致失败，任务级监测不可行。本文提出一种监测与分类方法，将评估分解为质量、适用性和效率三个维度，以及三个监测范围，利用变异系数作为特征信号，在合成测试中97%的发现被自动路由。

<h3 id="570">[570] Moment-Video: Diagnosing Temporal Fidelity of Video MLLMs on Momentary Visual Events</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02522) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaolin Liu, Yilun Zhu, Xiangyu Zhao, Xuehui Wang, Yan Li, Xin Li, Haoyu Cao, Xing Sun, Shaofeng Zhang, Xu Yang, Zhihang Zhong, Xue Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Benchmark, Video Understanding
- **Summary**: 视频多模态大模型（MLLM）在短暂视觉事件理解上能力不足。本文提出Moment-Video基准，包含1000个问答对，涵盖七领域25子类，评估33个模型，最佳仅39.6%准确率，开源模型低于25%，表明模型缺乏时间保真性。

<h3 id="571">[571] Why Not Hyperparameter-Friendly Optimisation? A Monotonic Adaptive Norm Rescaling Approach For Long-Tailed Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02526](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02526) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuo Zhang, Chenqi Li, Tingting Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long-Tailed Learning, Image Classification, Deep Learning
- **Summary**: 长尾识别中的自适应范数缩放方法对超参数敏感。本文提出自适应单调归一化（SAMN），直接对每类权重范数施加单调性约束，无需参数正则化，在多个基准上达到最优性能，且易于与其他方法结合。

<h3 id="572">[572] SimSD: Simple Speculative Decoding in Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02544](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02544) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junxia Cui, Haotian Ye, Runchu Tian, Hongcan Guo, Jinya Jiang, Haoru Li, Chaojie Ren, Yiming Huang, Kaijie Zhu, Zhongkai Yu, Kun Zhou, Jingbo Shang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, Diffusion Model, LLM Inference
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/airevo2/SimSD-release)
- **Summary**: 扩散语言模型（dLLM）与标准的词元级投机解码不兼容。本文提出SimSD算法，通过即插即用的掩码策略为dLLM提供时间有效的词元级上下文，实现投机解码验证，在四个基准上达到最高7.46倍的解码吞吐量提升。

<h3 id="573">[573] Modeling Depth Ambiguity: A Mixture-Density Representation for Flying-Point-Free Depth Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02552](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02552) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siyuan Bian, Congrong Xu, Jun Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Depth Estimation, 3D Vision, Computer Vision
- **Summary**: 深度估计中“飞点”伪影源于单深度假设。本文提出混合密度表示（MDA），让模型预测多个深度假设及其概率，在边界处选择真实表面上的深度而非中间值，显著提升边界重建质量，消除飞点，并扩展到透明物体和天空区域。

<h3 id="574">[574] From Layers to Submodules: Rethinking Granularity in Replacement-Based LLM Compression</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02559) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Elia Cunegatti, Marcus Vukojevic, Erik Nielsen, Giovanni Iacca
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, LLM Inference, Pruning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/eliacunegatti/SubFit)
- **Summary**: 现有基于替换的LLM压缩方法限制在整层粒度和连续选择。本文提出SubFit，在子模块级别进行非连续选择，为注意力和前馈子模块分别拟合轻量级残差旁路。在10个LLM和多种稀疏度下，以25%稀疏度保留84.6%下游准确率，优于基线。

<h3 id="575">[575] Permissive Safety Through Trusted Inference: Verifiable Belief-Space Neural Safety Filters for Assured Interactive Robotics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02562](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02562) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haimin Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG; eess.SY

- **Summary**: 信念空间安全滤波器（BeliefSF）在交互式机器人中提供形式化安全保证面临挑战。本文提出一种算法，利用共形预测认证高概率安全性，考虑运行时推理可靠性，在模拟人车交互中验证了更宽松的安全滤波器。

<h3 id="576">[576] AdaCodec: A Predictive Visual Code for Video MLLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02569](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02569) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Video Understanding, Video Compression
- **Summary**: 视频多模态大模型（MLLM）独立编码各帧导致视觉词元冗余。本文提出AdaCodec预测性视觉编码，仅在预测不准确时发送完整参考帧，否则用紧凑的P-token编码帧间变化。在11个基准上，以1/7词元预算超越基线，并将首词元时间从9.26秒降至1.62秒。

<h3 id="577">[577] Mitigating Perceptual Judgment Bias in Multimodal LLM-as-a-Judge via Perceptual Perturbation and Reward Modeling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.02578](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02578) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seojeong Park, Jiho Choi, Junyong Kang, Seonho Lee, Jaeyo Shin, Hyunjung Shim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Reward Model, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了多模态LLM作为评判者时存在的感知判断偏差问题，即当视觉证据与文本线索冲突时，评判者倾向于奖励合理的叙述而非感知正确的答案。提出通过感知扰动数据集和结合GRPO奖励与批量排序的统一训练框架来缓解该偏差。实验表明，该方法显著提升了感知忠实度、排序一致性和与人类评价的对齐度。

<h3 id="584">[584] Safety Mirage: How Spurious Correlations Undermine VLM Safety Fine-Tuning and Can Be Mitigated by Machine Unlearning</h3>

- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Red Teaming, Adversarial Training
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/facebookresearch/advgame)
- **Summary**: 现有安全对齐依赖顺序对抗训练。本文将安全对齐建模为非零和博弈，联合训练攻击者LM和防御者LM，使用偏好奖励信号。AdvGame方法提升安全性和有用性帕累托前沿，攻击者LM成为通用红队agent。

<h3 id="605">[605] Unplugging a Seemingly Sentient Machine Is the Rational Choice -- A Metaphysical Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21016) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Erik J Bekkers, Anna Ciaunica
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Philosophy of Science
- **Venue**: ICML
- **Summary**: 论文批判计算功能主义，提出生物唯心主义框架，认为意识是基础且自创生生命是必要物理标记。结论：AI只是功能模仿，不是有意识主体。呼吁保护人类意识而非机器权利。

<h3 id="606">[606] MulFeRL: Enhancing Reinforcement Learning with Verbal Feedback in a Multi-turn Loop</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22900](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22900) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuancheng Li, Haitao Li, Yujia Zhou, YiqunLiu, Qingyao Ai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 现有RLVR仅用标量奖励，对失败样本信息不足。提出MulFeRL，利用自然语言反馈引导RLVR，包括进度诱导、信用分配和结构化反馈注入。在数学推理上超越基线和RLVR。

<h3 id="607">[607] PolarMem: A Training-Free Polarized Latent Graph Memory for Verifiable Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.00415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00415) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhisheng Chen, Tingyu Wu, Zijie Zhou, Zhengwei Xie, Jinhan Li, Ziyan Weng, Liang Lin, Jingwei Song, Zikai Xiao, Yingwei Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Memory Architecture, Knowledge Graph
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/czs-ict/PolarMem)
- **Summary**: 当前VLM内存系统仅正向关联，缺乏记录已验证缺失或逻辑排除的证据。提出PolarMem，无训练极化潜在图记忆，通过语义一致性验证和自适应分布划分创建HAS/NOT_HAS/Uncertain状态。在多个VLM和基准上减少检索矛盾。

<h3 id="608">[608] Structure Enables Effective Self-Localization of Errors in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02416) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ankur Samanta, Akshayaa Magesh, Ayush Jain, Kavosh Asadi, Youliang Yu, Daniel Jiang, Boris Vidolov, Kaveh Hassani, Paul Sajda, Jalaj Bhandari, Yonathan Efroni
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought, Self-Correction
- **Summary**: LLM自纠错困难。本文提出结构化推理步骤（离散语义连贯的思想步骤），使模型能更可靠定位错误。引入Thought-ICS框架，迭代生成离散思想并进行回溯替代。在自主设置下优于当代基线。

<h3 id="609">[609] Breaking the Reversal Curse in Autoregressive Language Models via Identity Bridge</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02470](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02470) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xutao Ma, Yixiao Huang, Hanlin Zhu, Somayeh Sojoudi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reversal Curse
- **Summary**: 针对自回归语言模型存在的反转诅咒问题（训练时学习A→B，测试时无法推断B←A），提出一种名为Identity Bridge的简单数据配方（添加A→A形式的正则化数据）。理论证明一层Transformer即可破除诅咒，实验表明1B模型微调后反转任务成功率从接近0%提升至50%。

<h3 id="610">[610] From Features to Actions: Explainability in Traditional and Agentic AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06841](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06841) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sindhuja Chaduvula, Jessee Ho, Kina Kim, Aravind Narayanan, Ahmed Y. Radwan, Mahshid Alinoori, Muskan Garg, Dhanesh Ramachandram, Shaina Raza
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VectorInstitute/unified-xai-evaluation-framework)
- **Summary**: 比较归因式解释与轨迹级诊断在静态预测和代理式AI设定中的适用性。发现归因方法在代理轨迹中不可靠（无法诊断执行级失败），而轨迹级评估能持续定位行为故障（状态跟踪不一致在失败运行中占比高2.7倍）。

<h3 id="611">[611] ToolSelf: Unifying Task Execution and Self-Reconfiguration via Tool-Driven Emergent Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07883](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07883) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jingqi Zhou, Sheng Wang, Dezhao Deng, Junwen Lu, Junwei Su, Qintong Li, Jiahui Gao, Hao Wu, Jiyue Jiang, Lingpeng Kong, Dunhong Jin, Chuan Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Runtime Self-Reconfiguration
- **Summary**: 提出ToolSelf框架，将配置更新抽象为标准工具接口，统一执行与自适应于同一策略的动作空间，实现运行时自重构。结合配置感知两阶段训练（CAT），在多种基准上零样本即达专业化代理水平，CAT训练后平均提升28.8分。

<h3 id="612">[612] Beyond End-to-End Video Models: An LLM-Based Multi-Agent System for Educational Video Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11790) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lingyong Yan, Jiulong Wu, Dong Xie, Weixian Shi, Deguo Xia, Jizhou Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Video Generation, Education Technology
- **Venue**: ACM SIGKDD 2026
- **Summary**: 设计LASEV多智能体系统，通过分层协作（解答、插图、旁白代理）生成教育视频，解决端到端视频模型在逻辑严谨性和知识准确性上的不足。系统日产量超百万视频，成本降低95%以上，且保持高接受率。

<h3 id="613">[613] Prototype Transformer: Towards Language Model Architectures Interpretable by Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11852) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yordan Yordanov, Matteo Forasassi, Bayar Menzat, Ruizhi Wang, Chang Qi, Markus Kaltenberger, Amine M&#39;Charrak, Tommaso Salvatori, Thomas Lukasiewicz
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Interpretability, Prototype Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 提出Prototype Transformer (ProtoT)，用可学习原型向量替代二次复杂度的自注意力，实现线性复杂度。原型自动捕获可命名概念（如“woman”），支持模型解读和定向编辑，在文本生成和GLUE等任务上表现良好。

<h3 id="614">[614] REAL: Resolving Knowledge Conflicts in Knowledge-Intensive Visual Question Answering via Reasoning-Pivot Alignment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14065) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kai Ye, Xianwei Mao, Sheng Zhou, Zirui Shao, Ye Mo, Liangliang Liu, Haikuan Huang, Bin Li, Jiajun Bu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Knowledge Conflict, Visual Question Answering
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对知识密集型视觉问答中的知识冲突问题，提出REAL框架，引入推理支点（Reasoning-Pivot）概念用于冲突检测与缓解。通过RPA-SFT训练判别器、RPGD解码策略，显著提升判别准确率和整体性能。

<h3 id="615">[615] Benchmarking at the Edge of Comprehension</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14307](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14307) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Samuele Marro, Jialin Yu, Emanuele La Malfa, Oishi Deb, Jiawei Li, Yibo Yang, Ebey Abraham, Sunando Sengupta, Eric Sommerlade, Michael Wooldridge, Philip Torr
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对基准测试进入“后理解”时代的问题，提出Critique-Resilient Benchmarking框架，利用对抗生成-评估游戏和人类作为有界验证者，实现模型间比较。通过项目化Bradley-Terry模型联合排名，结果稳定且与外部能力度量相关。

<h3 id="616">[616] When AI Benchmarks Plateau: A Systematic Study of Benchmark Saturation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16763](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16763) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mubashara Akhtar, Anka Reuel, Prajna Soni, Sanchit Ahuja, Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi, Ruchit Rawal, Vilém Zouhar, Srishti Yadav, Chenxi Whitehouse, Dayeon Ki, Jennifer Mickel, Leshem Choshen, Marek Šuppa, Jan Batzner, Jenny Chim, Jeba Sania, Yanan Long, Hossein A. Rahmani, Christina Knight, Yiyang Nan, Jyoutir Raj, Yu Fan, Shubham Singh, Subramanyam Sahoo, Eliya Habba, Usman Gohar, Siddhesh Pawar, Robert Scholz, Arjun Subramonian, Jingwei Ni, Mykel Kochenderfer, Sanmi Koyejo, Mrinmaya Sachan, Stella Biderman, Zeerak Talat, Avijit Ghosh, Irene Solaiman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 系统研究基准饱和现象，分析60个语言模型基准的14个属性。发现近半数基准出现饱和，且速度随年龄增加；专家策划的基准更抗饱和，而公开测试数据无影响。

<h3 id="617">[617] LLM-WikiRace Benchmark: How Far Can LLMs Plan over Real-World Knowledge Graphs?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16902](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16902) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Juliusz Ziomek, William Bankes, Lorenz Wolf, Shyam Sundhar Ramesh, Xiaohang Tang, Ilija Bogunovic
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Planning, Knowledge Graph
- **Summary**: 提出LLM-Wikirace基准，评估LLM在Wikipedia知识图上逐步导航到目标页面的规划与推理能力。前沿模型在简单级达超人类水平，但在困难级最佳模型（Gemini-3）仅成功23%，且难以在失败后重新规划。

<h3 id="618">[618] LLM4Cov: Execution-Aware Agentic Learning for High-coverage Testbench Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16953](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16953) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hejia Zhang, Zhongming Yu, Chia-Tung Ho, Haoxing Ren, Brucek Khailany, Jishen Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Test Generation, RTL Verification
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 提出LLM4Cov离线代理学习框架，将硬件验证建模为单步状态迁移，通过执行验证数据筛选、策略感知数据合成和最差状态优先采样，使4B模型在模拟评估中通过率69.2%，平均覆盖率90.4%，超越教师模型。

<h3 id="619">[619] PATRA: Pattern-Aware Alignment and Balanced Reasoning for Time Series Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23161](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23161) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junkai Lu, Peng Chen, Xingjian Wu, Yang Shu, Chenjuan Guo, Christian S. Jensen, Bin Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Question Answering, Pattern-Aware
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 提出PATRA模型，通过模式感知机制提取时间序列的趋势和季节模式实现深度对齐，并设计任务感知平衡奖励协调不同难度任务的学习。在TSQA任务上超过强基线，展现跨模态理解与推理能力。

<h3 id="620">[620] Vision Language Models Cannot Reason About Physical Transformation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.07109](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.07109) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dezhi Luo, Yijiang Li, Maijunxian Wang, Tianwei Zhao, Bingyang Wang, Siheng Wang, Pinyuan Feng, Pooyan Rahmanzadehgervi, Ziqiao Ma, Hokin Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Physical Reasoning, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 构建ConservationBench评估VLM对物理变换的理解，涉及四种属性的守恒与非守恒场景共23040个问题。112个VLM表现接近随机，且改进守恒任务时控制任务下降，表明VLM无法维持变换不变表征。

<h3 id="621">[621] On Information Self-Locking in Reinforcement Learning for Active Reasoning of LLM agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.12109](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.12109) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Deyu Zou, Yongqiang Chen, Fan Feng, Mufei Li, Pan Li, Yu Gong, James Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Active Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/unimpor/T3)
- **Summary**: 发现基于结果的强化学习在LLM代理主动推理中导致信息自锁（SeL），模型既无法引出信息反馈也无法内化证据。提出AREW方法，利用方向性批评重新分配轨迹内信用，在9个代理任务上最高提升60分。

<h3 id="622">[622] AgentProcessBench: Diagnosing Step-Level Process Quality in Tool-Using Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14465](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14465) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shengda Fan, Xuyan Ye, Yupeng Huo, Zhi-Yuan Chen, Yiju Guo, Shenzhi Yang, Wenkai Yang, Shuqi Ye, Jingwen Chen, Haotian Chen, Xin Cong, Yankai Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark, Process Supervision
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/RUCBM/AgentProcessBench)
- **Summary**: 提出AgentProcessBench，首个评估工具使用代理步骤级有效性的基准，包含1000条轨迹和8509个三等人标签（正确/中性/错误）。实验发现弱策略模型因提前终止而膨胀正确步数比率，过程信号可显著增强测试时扩展。

<h3 id="623">[623] OpenHospital: A Thing-in-itself Arena for Evolving and Benchmarking LLM-based Collective Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14771](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14771) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Peigen Liu, Rui Ding, Yuren Mao, Ziyan Jiang, Yuxiang Ye, Yunjun Gao, Ying Zhang, Renjie Sun, Longbin Lai, Zhengping Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Collective Intelligence, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 提出OpenHospital交互式竞技场，用于LLM集体智能的演化和基准测试。医生代理通过患者代理交互进化集体智能，采用数据在代理自身的范式快速增强能力，并提供了医疗熟练度和系统效率的评估指标。

<h3 id="624">[624] Retrieval-aligned Tabular Foundation Models Enable Robust Clinical Risk Prediction in Electronic Health Records Under Real-world Constraints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.01841](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01841) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Minh-Khoi Pham, Thang-Long Nguyen Ho, Thao Thi Phuong Dao, Tai Tan Mai, Minh-Triet Tran, Marie E. Ward, Una Geary, Rob Brennan, Nick McDonald, Martin Crane, Marija Bezbradica
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Tabular Learning, Retrieval-Augmented
- **Summary**: 提出AWARE框架，通过任务对齐检索（监督嵌入学习和轻量适配器）改善电子健康记录中的临床风险预测。在极端不平衡下AUPRC提升达12.2%，识别出检索质量和对齐是关键瓶颈。

<h3 id="625">[625] Rashomon Memory: Towards Argumentation-Driven Retrieval for Multi-Perspective Agent Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.03588](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03588) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Albert Sadowski, Jarosław A. Chudziak
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Argumentation
- **Venue**: EXTRAAMAS workshop at AAMAS 2026
- **Summary**: 现有AI代理记忆架构假设单一正确编码，无法处理同一事件的多目标冲突理解。本文提出Rashomon Memory，通过并行目标条件代理编码经验，并在检索时基于论证进行协商。概念验证表明，检索模式（选择、组合、冲突展示）可从攻击图拓扑中涌现，冲突展示模式能让决策者直接看到解释性冲突。

<h3 id="626">[626] FeynmanBench: Benchmarking Multimodal LLMs on Diagrammatic Physics Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.03893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03893) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zeyu Wang, Jingye Xu, Xiaogang Li, Peiyao Xiao, Qinhao Kong, Ben Wang, Chengliang Xu, Zichao Chen, Bing Zhao, Hu Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Scientific Reasoning, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有多模态科学推理基准主要评估局部信息提取，忽略全局结构属性。本文提出FeynmanBench，包含2000+费曼图任务，评估模型在拓扑重建和代数推导上的能力。19个多模态LLM在局部识别上达70-95%，但在拓扑重建上仅13-17%，代数推导接近零。

<h3 id="627">[627] PECKER: A Precisely Efficient Critical Knowledge Erasure Recipe For Machine Unlearning in Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.05634](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.05634) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhiyong Ma, Zhitao Deng, Huan Tang, Jialin Chen, Zhijun Zheng, Zhengping Li, Qingyuan Chuai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, Diffusion Model, Knowledge Distillation
- **Venue**: ICPR 2026
- **Summary**: 现有机器遗忘方法训练时间长、计算开销大。本文提出PECKER，在蒸馏框架中引入显著性掩码，优先更新对遗忘目标数据贡献大的参数，减少不必要的梯度计算。在CIFAR-10和STL-10上，PECKER在类遗忘和概念遗忘任务中均达到更短训练时间，同时保持良好图像分布。

<h3 id="628">[628] What's Missing in Screen-to-Action? Towards a UI-in-the-Loop Paradigm for Multimodal GUI Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.06995](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06995) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Songze Li, Xiaoke Guo, Tianqi Liu, Biao Yi, Zhaoyan Gong, Zhiqiang Liu, Huajun Chen, Wen Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: GUI Automation, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Findings
- **Summary**: 现有GUI推理方法缺乏对UI元素的全面理解。本文提出UI-in-the-Loop (UILoop)范式，将GUI推理视为循环的Screen-UI元素-Action过程，使MLLM显式学习关键UI元素。在提出的UI Comprehension-Bench（26K样本）上，UILoop达到最优UI理解性能，并提升了GUI推理任务的结果。

<h3 id="629">[629] Process Reward Agents for Steering Knowledge-Intensive Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.09482](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09482) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiwoong Sohn, Tomasz Sternal, Kenneth Styppa, Torsten Hoefler, Michael Moor
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Process Reward Model, RAG, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 知识密集型推理的中间步骤难以局部验证，现有过程奖励模型（PRM）仅事后评分。本文提出Process Reward Agent (PRA)，在线提供步骤奖励并集成搜索解码。在医学推理基准上，PRA在Qwen3-4B上达81.9%准确率（4B规模新SOTA），并将不可见策略模型准确率提升最高25.7%。

<h3 id="630">[630] MAVEN-T: Reinforced Heterogeneous Distillation for Real-Time Multi-Agent Trajectory Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10169](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10169) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenchang Duan, Zhenguo Gao, Jinguo Xian, Yi Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Trajectory Prediction, Autonomous Driving, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 现有轨迹预测模型高容量设计难以实时部署。本文提出MAVEN-T，采用异构蒸馏框架，教师模型利用环绕感知图编码器和稀疏MoE，学生模型通过PPO奖励调优。在多个数据集上，学生模型实现6.2倍参数压缩、3.7倍推理加速，在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上延迟14.6ms。

<h3 id="631">[631] Perspective on Bias in Biomedical AI: Preventing Downstream Healthcare Disparities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.14514](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14514) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michal Rosen-Zvi, Yoav Kan-Tor, Michael Danziger, Agata Ferretti, Javier Aula-Blasco, Julia Falcao, Ron Shamir, Mira Marcus-Kalish, Mordechai Muszkat
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CE
- **Tags**: Bias Mitigation, Medical AI, Fairness
- **Venue**: 2026 IEEE International Conference on Digital Health (ICDH)
- **Summary**: 生物医学AI中的偏见常源于数据收集早期阶段。本文通过分析4514篇组学文献，发现仅2.7%报告祖先或种族信息，且欧洲血统数据占主导。提出三大原则：来源、开放性和通过评估透明度实现可靠性，以帮助模型开发者和用户识别偏见。

<h3 id="632">[632] RadAgent: A tool-using AI agent for stepwise interpretation of chest computed tomography</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.15231](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15231) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mélanie Roschewitz, Kenneth Styppa, Yitian Tao, Jiwoong Sohn, Jean-Benoit Delbrouck, Benjamin Gundersen, Nicolas Deperrois, Christian Bluethgen, Julia E. Vogt, Bjoern Menze, Farhad Nooralahzadeh, Michael Krauthammer, Michael Moor
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Agent, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 现有VLM生成CT报告缺乏可解释推理过程。本文提出RadAgent，一个工具使用AI代理，通过逐步可解释流程生成报告。在胸部CT报告生成中，临床准确率提升5.8个宏F1点（相对35.4%），鲁棒性提升24.7点，并实现了37.0%的忠实度。

<h3 id="633">[633] TrafficClaw: A Generalizable LLM Agent in the Unified Physical Environment for Urban Traffic Control</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17456](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17456) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Siqi Lai, Pan Zhang, Yuping Zhou, Jindong Han, Yansong Ning, Hao Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Traffic Signal Control
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/usail-hkust/TrafficClaw)
- **Summary**: 现有交通控制方法多针对孤立子系统，缺乏协调优化。本文提出TrafficClaw，基于LLM的通用交通控制代理，在统一物理环境中进行时空推理和多阶段RL优化。在三个大都市区域和六个交通任务上展现了强泛化性和协调能力。

<h3 id="634">[634] KnowledgeBerg: Evaluating Systematic Knowledge Coverage and Compositional Reasoning in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17621](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17621) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiao Zhang, Qianru Meng, Yongjian Chen, Yumeng Wang, Johan Bos
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Compositional Reasoning
- **Summary**: 许多简单问题隐含着系统知识覆盖和组合推理需求。本文提出KnowledgeBerg基准（4800题），覆盖10领域17语言。开源LLM在知识枚举上仅5.26-36.88 F1，推理准确率16.00-44.19，即使增加测试时计算和检索增强，仍有显著差距。

<h3 id="635">[635] Neural Decision-Propagation for Answer Set Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.01797](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.01797) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Thomas Eiter, Katsumi Inoue, Sota Moriyama
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Answer Set Programming
- **Venue**: 35th International Joint Conference on Artificial Intelligence (IJCAI-ECAI 2026)
- **Summary**: 神经-符号AI中集成ASP的推理管道依赖经典求解器，扩展性差。本文提出决策传播（DProp）方法，并发展为神经扩展NDProp，使用神经决策和模糊传播。NDProp能够学习高效计算稳定模型，在神经-符号基准上提升准确率和可扩展性。

<h3 id="636">[636] Trustworthy AI Suffers from Invariance Conflicts and Causality is The Solution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02640](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02640) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruta Binkyte, Ivaxi Sheth, Zhijing Jin, Mohammad Havaei, Bernhard Schölkopf, Mario Fritz
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Causal Inference, Fairness, Robustness
- **Summary**: 可信AI的公平性、鲁棒性、隐私等目标难以同时实现。本文论证因果关系是理解这些权衡并选择性不变性的统一框架。通过案例和模拟实验，展示了因果关系如何缓和或解决可信AI中的冲突。

<h3 id="637">[637] ANDRE: An Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor for Inductive Logic Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.04193](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04193) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Iman Sharifi, Peng Wei, Saber Fallah
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Inductive Logic Programming, Neurosymbolic AI
- **Summary**: 现有ILP方法在噪声和概率场景下难以扩展。本文提出ANDRE，基于注意力的神经符号可微分规则提取器，用注意力驱动的逻辑算子逼近min-max语义。在经典ILP基准和噪声数据集上，ANDRE取得竞争性或更优性能，且对中等标签噪声具有鲁棒性。

<h3 id="638">[638] The Refusal--Compliance Tradeoff: A Large-Scale Safety Behavior Audit of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.05427](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alif Al Hasan, Sumon Biswas
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Safety Evaluation, Bias Detection
- **Summary**: 拒绝率不能充分反映LLM安全性。本文审计21个开源LLM的四种安全基准，发现保守模型（如Llama）过度拒绝但安全，而宽松模型（如DeepSeek）保持有用性但容忍有害输出。模型对特定群体保护不均，且拒绝-顺从倾向在模型家族内稳定。

<h3 id="639">[639] Towards a Virtual Neuroscientist: Autonomous Neuroimaging Analysis via Multi-Agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09366](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09366) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Keqi Han, Songlin Zhao, Yao Su, Xiang Li, Yixuan Yuan, Lifang He, Carl Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multi-Agent System, Neuroimaging Analysis
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LearningKeqi/Virtual-Neuroscientist-NEXUS)
- **Summary**: 神经影像分析工作流缺乏下游目标推理和闭环适应。本文提出NEXUS，自主多智能体框架，采用代码中心执行范式，通过多专家代理协作合成程序，并引入分层验证进行质量控制。在ADHD-200和ADNI上，NEXUS在预测性能上优于标准工作流，并展现策略探索等智能行为。

<h3 id="640">[640] Causal state binding predicts action control in language agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09692) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiao Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Causal Inference, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 语言代理的状态变量与最终行动之间的绑定关系缺乏评估。本文提出因果状态绑定（CSB）框架，测量行动是否随关键状态变化。在57816条记录和SWE-bench Lite上，CSB复合指标将约束清洁命中率从0.873提升至0.935。

<h3 id="641">[641] RADAR: Redundancy-Aware Diffusion for Multi-Agent Communication Structure Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.09907](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09907) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhen Zhang, Wanjing Zhou, Juncheng Li, Hao Fei, Jun Wen, Wei Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent Coordination, Graph Generation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/cszhangzhen/RADAR)
- **Summary**: 多智能体系统的通信拓扑固定或单步生成，导致简单任务消耗过多token而复杂任务能力受限。提出RADAR，一种冗余感知的自适应生成框架，利用条件离散图扩散模型逐步生成通信拓扑，并以图的有效大小作为指导。在六个基准上一致优于基线，实现更高准确率、更低token消耗和更强鲁棒性。

<h3 id="642">[642] CVEvolve: Autonomous Algorithm Discovery for Unstructured Scientific Data Processing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.11359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11359) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ming Du, Xiangyu Yin, Yanqi Luo, Dishant Beniwal, Songyuan Tang, Hemant Sharma, Mathew J. Cherukara
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 科学数据处理需要特定算法或AI模型，成为领域专家的障碍。提出CVEvolve，一个自主智能体框架，具有零代码接口，结合多轮搜索、代码执行、评估和谱系感知采样，自动发现科学数据处理算法。在X射线荧光显微镜图像配准等任务上发现优于基线的算法。

<h3 id="643">[643] MMSkills: Towards Multimodal Skills for General Visual Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.13527](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13527) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kangning Zhang, Shuai Shao, Qingyao Li, Jianghao Lin, Lingyue Fu, Shijian Wang, Wenxiang Jiao, Yuan Lu, Weiwen Liu, Weinan Zhang, Yong Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Skill Learning
- **Summary**: 现有视觉智能体的技能包主要是文本形式，但程序知识本质上是多模态的。提出MMSkills框架，技能包含文本过程、运行时状态卡和多视角关键帧；开发轨迹到技能生成器和分支加载的多模态技能智能体。在GUI和游戏基准上一致改进前沿和较小多模态智能体的性能。

<h3 id="644">[644] Herculean: An Agentic Benchmark for Financial Intelligence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14355](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14355) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xueqing Peng, Zhuohan Xie, Yupeng Cao, Haohang Li, Lingfei Qian, Yan Wang, Vincent Jim Zhang, Huan He, Xuguang Ai, Linhai Ma, Ruoyu Xiang, Yueru He, Yi Han, Shuyao Wang, Yuqing Guo, Mingyang Jiang, Yilun Zhao, Youzhong Dong, Xiaoyu Wang, Yankai Chen, Ye Yuan, Qiyuan Zhang, Fuyuan Lyu, Haolun Wu, Yonghan Yang, Zichen Zhao, Yuyang Dai, Fan Zhang, Rania Elbadry, Ayesha Gull, Muhammad Usman Safder, Nuo Chen, Fengbin Zhu, Tianshi Cai, Zimu Wang, Polydoros Giannouris, Yuechen Jiang, Zhiwei Liu, Mohsinul Kabir, Yuyan Wang, Yixiang Zheng, Yangyang Yu, Weijin Liu, Wenbo Cao, Anke Xu, Peng Lu, Jerry Huang, Mingquan Lin, Prayag Tiwari, Yijia Zhao, Víctor Gutiérrez-Basulto, Xiao-Yang Liu, Kaleb E Smith, Jiahuan Pei, Arman Cohan, Jimin Huang, Yuehua Tang, Alejandro Lopez-Lira, Xi Chen, Xue Liu, Junichi Tsujii, Jian-Yun Nie, Sophia Ananiadou
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Financial AI, Agent Benchmark, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有金融基准主要评估静态能力，而非智能体的工作流执行。引入Herculean，第一个智能体金融智能基准，涵盖交易、对冲、市场洞察和审计四个工作流，每个实例化为MCP技能环境。前沿智能体在交易和市场洞察上表现较好，但在对冲和审计上困难重重，暴露出长时程协调和结构化验证的差距。

<h3 id="645">[645] Coding Agent Is Good As World Simulator</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.14398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14398) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongyu Wang, Jingquan Wang, Bocheng Zou, Radu Serban, Dan Negrut
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Code Generation, Physics Simulation
- **Summary**: 基于视频的世界模型生成视觉逼真但物理不合理的动态。提出基于智能体的框架，通过可执行的模拟代码构建基于物理的世界模型，协调规划、代码生成、视觉审查和物理分析智能体。在物理准确性、指令保真度和视觉质量上优于先进视频模型。

<h3 id="646">[646] Capturing LLM Capabilities via Evidence-Calibrated Query Clustering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17110](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17110) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Fangzhou Wu, Sandeep Silwal, Qiuyi Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Clustering
- **Summary**: 现有查询聚类方法依赖语义分类或嵌入，无法捕捉潜在能力需求。提出ECC算法，使用有限后验模型比较校准先验语义嵌入，通过Bradley-Terry能力参数和混合权重学习能力感知的聚类结构。在LLM能力排名质量上比人类标签和嵌入基线分别提升17.64和18.02个百分点，并在查询路由等下游任务中有效。

<h3 id="647">[647] Evaluating Deep Research Agents on Expert Consulting Work: A Benchmark with Verifiers, Rubrics, and Cognitive Traps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17554](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17554) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tanmay Asthana, Aman Saksena, Divyansh Sahu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 现有基准无法评估深度研究智能体（DRA）在专家咨询工作中的多文档决策级表现。提出包含42个专家提示的基准，使用确定性验证器和五标准专家评分（VRS 0-100），并嵌入认知陷阱。接受率低（Gemini 21.4%, o3 9.5%, Claude 9.5%）；VRS分数与其他基准一致；各个智能体有不同失败模式。

<h3 id="648">[648] Ethical Hyper-Velocity (EHV): A Hardware-Rooted Zero-Trust Runtime Enforcement Architecture for Agentic AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.17909](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.17909) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Riddhi Mohan Sharma
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LO
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Agent Security, Hardware Security
- **Summary**: 自主智能体系统缺乏基于硬件的运行时策略执行机制，策略延迟14-30天。提出EHV架构，结合语法约束解码、因果图CRDT、TEE认证和OSCAL审计，通过治理感知JIT编译器将策略执行点嵌入推理流程。TLA+模型检查在1738个状态中零违规；O(1)运行时执行，将治理延迟从天降至O(1)；在儿科肿瘤用例中验证。

<h3 id="649">[649] LLM-Guided Communication for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.18077](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18077) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sangjun Bae, Yisak Park, Sanghyeon Lee, Seungyul Han
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Multi-Agent Coordination
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 多智能体强化学习中通信低效或无法传递足够状态信息。提出LMAC，利用LLM推理能力设计通信协议，使所有智能体尽可能准确一致地重建底层状态，并通过状态感知标准迭代优化。在多个MARL基准上改进状态重建，并获得显著性能提升。

<h3 id="650">[650] Towards a General Intelligence and Interface for Wearable Health Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.22759](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22759) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Girish Narayanswamy, Maxwell A. Xu, A. Ali Heydari, Samy Abdel-Ghaffar, Marius Guerard, Kara Vaillancourt, Zhihan Zhang, Jake Garrison, Levi Albuquerque, Dimitris Spathis, Hong Yu, Hamid Palangi, Xuhai &#34;Orson&#34; Xu, David G.T. Barrett, Joseph Breda, Jed McGiffin, Yubin Kim, Yuwei Zhang, Naghmeh Rezaei, Samuel Solomon, Karan Ahuja, Tim Althoff, Jake Sunshine, Ming-Zher Poh, Benjamin Yetton, Ari Winbush, Nicholas B. Allen, James M. Rehg, Isaac Galatzer-Levy, Yun Liu, John Hernandez, Anupam Pathak, Conor Heneghan, Yuzhe Yang, Ahmed A. Metwally, Pushmeet Kohli, Mark Malhotra, Shwetak Patel, Xin Liu, Daniel McDuff
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Medical AI, Wearable Computing
- **Summary**: 将可穿戴传感器数据转化为健康洞察面临表型多样性和标签稀缺。提出基于100万亿分钟无标签传感器信号的预训练基础模型，并部署LLM智能体搜索预测头，集成到个人健康智能体。在35个健康预测任务上系统性改进，支持少样本学习；临床医生验证模型响应的相关性和安全性。

<h3 id="651">[651] LC-ERD: Mining Latent Logic for Self-Evolving Reasoning via Consistency-Regulated Reward Decomposition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24005) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yanyu Chen, Jiyue Jiang, Dianzhi Yu, Zheng Wu, Jiahong Liu, Jiaming Han, Xiao Guo, Jinhu Qi, Yu Li, Yifei Zhang, Irwin King
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, LLM Alignment, Reward Model
- **Venue**: SIGKDD 2026 Research Track
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LC-ERD-repo/LC-ERD)
- **Summary**: LLM自我对齐面临标签噪声、粗粒度监督和分布崩塌。提出LC-ERD框架，将自我对齐视为潜在结构挖掘，通过变分逻辑势和基于IGM的多智能体价值分解量化单步效用。提供稳健的自我进化路径，揭示逻辑一致性与准确性的权衡，识别标准奖励遗漏的高价值推理模式。

<h3 id="652">[652] When Does Multi-Agent RL Improve LLM Workflows? Workflow, Scale, and Policy-Sharing Tradeoffs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24202](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24202) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yifan Zeng, Yiran Wu, Yaolun Zhang, Wentian Zhao, Kun Wan, Qingyun Wu, Huazheng Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: 多智能体LLM工作流的强化学习训练不稳定，共享策略与独立策略的权衡尚不明确。系统研究共享策略和独立策略训练在不同工作流、任务和模型规模下的效果，分析角色级别梯度动态。多智能体RL通常提升性能，但增益依赖于工作流、任务和规模；独立策略峰值更高但更易崩塌；共享策略重新分配失败模式。

<h3 id="653">[653] Hypothesis Generation and Inductive Inference in Children and Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24528) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jeffrey Qin, Wasu Top Piriyakulkij, Zhuangfei Gao, Mia Radovanovic, Jessica Sommerville, Kevin Ellis, Marta Kryven
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Cognitive Science, LLM Reasoning, In-Context Learning
- **Summary**: 人类在不确定性下的推理遵循何种计算原则？LLM智能体是否表现类似？使用归纳推理箱任务，形式化为程序归纳和程序综合，比较儿童和LLM智能体的行为。儿童行为由主观证据可靠性和在线假设生成解释；LLM智能体复制儿童对证据可靠性的反应，但过度观察和过度遵从指令。

<h3 id="654">[654] Fundamental Limitation in Explaining AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24727](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24727) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Atsushi Suzuki, Jing Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.IT
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Explainable AI, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 能否从理论上提供完全忠实且可解释的AI解释？数学证明一个四难困境：AI及其解释无法同时满足环境复杂性、良好性能、可解释性和完全忠实性。在大多数应用中必须放弃完全忠实性，AI治理应基于解释的不完全忠实性。

<h3 id="655">[655] Test-Time Deep Thinking to Explore Implicit Rules</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.24828](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24828) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wentong Chen, Xin Cong, Zhong Zhang, Yaxi Lu, Siyuan Zhao, Yesai Wu, Qinyu Luo, Haotian Chen, Yankai Lin, Zhiyuan Liu, Maosong Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Test-Time Scaling, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: LLM智能体在存在隐式规则的环境中失败，陷入重复试错。提出TTExplore框架，包含思考者组件分析交互历史推断隐式规则，使用稳定的RL训练流水线（任务级分数和单思考节点）。训练Exp-Thinker（7B）在五个文本具身任务上提升基线智能体性能14-19分。

<h3 id="656">[656] Beyond the Frontier: Stochastic Backtracking for Efficient Test-Time Scaling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25143](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25143) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dao Tran, Duc Anh Le, Ngoc Luu, Quan Pham, Tung Pham, Hung Bui
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Test-Time Scaling, LLM Reasoning, Tree Search
- **Summary**: 测试时扩展中的PRM引导搜索是前沿仅存的，导致过早承诺和多样性丧失。引入随机回溯，维护历史前缀的持久池，并提出子池选择（Subpool Selection）和功率回溯顺序蒙特卡洛（Power Backtrack Sequential Monte Carlo）两种机制。在数学推理基准上，相比强PRM基线，以更少的token实现相同准确率或更高的每token准确率。

<h3 id="657">[657] FrontierOR: Benchmarking LLMs' Capacity for Efficient Algorithm Design in Large-Scale Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.25246](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25246) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Minwei Kong, Chonghe Jiang, Ao Qu, Wenbin Ouyang, Zhaoming Zeng, Xiaotong Guo, Zhekai Li, Junyi Li, Yi Fan, Xinshou Zheng, Xi Jing, Yikai Zhang, Zhiwei Liang, Seonghoo Kim, Runqing Yang, Zijian Zhou, Sirui Li, Han Zheng, Wangyang Ying, Ou Zheng, Chonghuan Wang, Jinglong Zhao, Hanzhang Qin, Cathy Wu, Paul Pu Liang, Jinhua Zhao, Hai Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Evaluation, Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Minw913/FrontierOR)
- **Summary**: 现有基准测试局限于小规模优化问题，缺乏对LLM设计可扩展算法能力的评估。提出FrontierOR基准，包含180个来自顶级运筹学期刊的任务。结果表明最强模型在31%的情况下优于Gurobi，说明LLM在高效算法设计上仍有很大提升空间。

<h3 id="658">[658] Experiments in Agentic AI for Science</h3>

<h3 id="661">[661] Agyn: An Open-Source Platform for AI Agents with Scalable On-Demand Execution, Agent Definition as a Code, and Zero-Trust Access</h3>

<h3 id="663">[663] Asking Is Not Enough: Protocol Sensitivity in LLM Confidence Calibration</h3>

<h3 id="664">[664] FundaPod: A Multi-Persona Agent Pod Platform with Knowledge Graph Memory for AI-Assisted Fundamental Investment Research</h3>

<h3 id="666">[666] Cookie-Bench: Continuous On-screen Key Interaction Evaluation for Web Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.30000](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30000) (replaced)

<h3 id="670">[670] Score Function Gradient Estimation to Widen the Applicability of Decision-Focused Learning</h3>

<h3 id="671">[671] DeepIPCv2: LiDAR-powered Robust Environmental Perception and Navigational Control for Autonomous Vehicle</h3>

<h3 id="673">[673] AutoEval Done Right: Using Synthetic Data for Model Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2403.07008](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.07008) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pierre Boyeau, Anastasios N. Angelopoulos, Nir Yosef, Jitendra Malik, Michael I. Jordan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; stat.ME
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Data Synthesis
- **Summary**: 问题：机器学习模型评估需要大量人工标注验证数据，成本高且耗时。方法：提出利用AI生成的合成数据及高效统计无偏算法（自动评估）来减少人工标注需求。结果：在GPT-4实验中，有效人工样本量提升高达50%。

<h3 id="674">[674] Perturbation Effects on Accuracy and Fairness among Similar Individuals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2404.01356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.01356) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuran Li, Hao Xue, Peng Wu, Xingjun Ma, Zhen Zhang, Huaming Chen, Flora D. Salim
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Fairness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Xuran-LI/RIFair)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有评估协议孤立考虑鲁棒性和公平性，忽略了关键的失效模式。方法：形式化鲁棒个体公平性（RIF），提出黑盒对抗框架RIFair，通过解耦扰动构造语义保持但非鲁棒或不公平的样本对。结果：实验表明仅关注鲁棒性或公平性的指标会遗漏“鲁棒偏见”和“非鲁棒公平”行为，RIFair能可靠揭示这些隐藏漏洞。

<h3 id="675">[675] DAG-Plan: Generating Directed Acyclic Dependency Graphs for Dual-Arm Cooperative Planning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2406.09953](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.09953) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zeyu Gao, Yao Mu, Jinye Qu, Mengkang Hu, Shijia Peng, Chengkai Hou, Lingyue Guo, Ping Luo, Shanghang Zhang, Yanfeng Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Robot Manipulation, Task Planning
- **Summary**: 问题：双机械臂任务规划中，现有LLM方法在建模非线性子任务依赖时存在效率与适应性的权衡。方法：提出DAG-Plan，首次采用有向无环图（DAG）作为中心表示，仅用一次LLM解析生成DAG，执行时动态分配节点给合适的手臂。结果：在双机械臂厨房基准测试中，成功率比单次线性序列方法高48%，执行效率比迭代查询方法高84.1%。

<h3 id="676">[676] Agricultural Landscape Understanding At Country-Scale</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.05359](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05359) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Radhika Dua, Aditi Agarwal, Aishwarya Jayagopal, Depanshu Sani, Alex Wilson, Hoang Tran, Ishan Deshpande, Bogdan Floristean, Neelabh Goyal, Ramya Cheruvu, Vishal Batchu, Yan Mayster, Gaurav Aggarwal, Alok Talekar, Vaibhav Rajan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Segmentation
- **Summary**: 问题：现有农业景观理解系统仅限于田地分割，且未在实际部署中验证。方法：提出首个国家级农业映射系统，能够分割田地、树木和水体等实例，并采用后处理启发式确保一致性。结果：通过严格评估，生成的高精度土地利用图通过API公开访问。

<h3 id="677">[677] Implicit Regularization for Multi-label Feature Selection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.11436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11436) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Feature Selection, Multi-Label Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：多标签学习中稀疏特征选择方法使用显式正则化项存在额外偏差。方法：提出基于阿达玛积参数化和标签嵌入的隐式正则化估计器。结果：在基准数据集上，所提估计器偏差更小，且可能导致良性过拟合。

<h3 id="678">[678] A Foundation Model for Wearable Movement Data in Mental Health Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.15240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.15240) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Franklin Y. Ruan, Aiwei Zhang, Jenny Y. Oh, SouYoung Jin, Nicholas C. Jacobson
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.HC; q-bio.QM
- **Tags**: Foundation Model, Mental Health, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/njacobsonlab/Pretrained-Actigraphy-Transformer/)
- **Summary**: 问题：可穿戴运动数据在心理健康研究中缺乏基础模型。方法：设计基于掩码自编码器的预训练变换器（PAT），对分钟级一周加速度计序列进行预训练。结果：在心理健康预测任务（如苯二氮卓使用、抑郁）上，PAT一致优于非基础模型基线，在苯二氮卓使用预测中相比LSTM提升55.6%，并提供可解释的注意力图。

<h3 id="679">[679] Self-supervised Monocular Depth and Pose Estimation for Endoscopy with Latent Priors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2411.17790](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.17790) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ziang Xu, Bin Li, Yang Hu, Chenyu Zhang, James East, Sharib Ali, Jens Rittscher
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Depth Estimation, Self-Supervised Learning, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 问题：内窥镜单目深度和姿态估计在胃肠道复杂环境中泛化性差。方法：提出自监督框架，结合生成潜在库和变分自编码器，利用自然图像深度先验提升深度预测鲁棒性，并将姿态估计重构为VAE框架以稳定尺度。结果：在SimCol和EndoSLAM数据集上优于现有自监督方法。

<h3 id="680">[680] Introduction to Graph Neural Networks for Machine Learning Engineers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2412.19419](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.19419) (replaced)
- **Authors**: James H. Tanis, Chris Giannella, Adrian V. Mariano, Daoud Meerzaman
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Graph Neural Network, Survey
- **Venue**: ACM Computing Surveys
- **Summary**: 问题：图神经网络文献增长迅速，但缺乏系统介绍。方法：通过编码器-解码器框架介绍GNN，并提供多种图分析任务的解码器示例。结果：通过理论和实验分析了不同训练规模和图复杂度下GNN的行为，重点讨论了过平滑和过挤压现象。

<h3 id="681">[681] Efficient Weighted Sampling via Score-based Generative Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.04646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.04646) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Heasung Kim, Taekyun Lee, Hyeji Kim, Gustavo de Veciana
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Sampling
- **Summary**: 问题：加权采样需要从基PDF和权重函数的乘积中采样，现有方法复杂。方法：提出基于预训练得分生成模型的无训练加权采样框架，通过轻量级近似引导和不确定性感知调度器实现。结果：在大规模设置（如Stable Diffusion XL）中，获得1.2倍至4.7倍加速，任务性能匹配或超越基线。

<h3 id="682">[682] ShapeLib: Designing a library of programmatic 3D shape abstractions with Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.08884](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08884) (replaced)
- **Authors**: R. Kenny Jones, Paul Guerrero, Niloy J. Mitra, Daniel Ritchie
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.GR
- **Tags**: 3D Vision, Program Synthesis, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 问题：3D形状抽象库的设计需要大量人工干预。方法：提出ShapeLib，利用LLM先验自动设计程序化3D形状抽象库，接受高层文本描述和少量示例形状。结果：通过LLM与几何推理结合，生成的函数库能跨形状分布泛化，并支持下游编辑和生成任务。

<h3 id="683">[683] HiFi-KPI: A Dataset for Hierarchical KPI Extraction from Earnings Filings</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2502.15411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.15411) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rasmus Aavang, Giovanni Rizzi, Rasmus Bøggild, Alexandre Iolov, Mike Zhang, Johannes Bjerva
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Information Extraction, Dataset, Financial AI
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aaunlp/HiFi-KPI)
- **Summary**: 问题：iXBRL的细粒度分类限制了关键绩效指标（KPI）的跨公司可转移性。方法：构建层次化金融KPI数据集（HiFi-KPI），包含165万段落和19.8万层次化标签。结果：基准测试显示编码器模型在分类上达到0.906 macro-F1，LLM在结构化抽取上达到0.440 F1，错误主要与日期相关。

<h3 id="684">[684] Efficient LLM Moderation with Multi-Layer Latent Prototypes</h3>

- **Tags**: LLM Safety

<h3 id="685">[685] EuroBERT: Scaling Multilingual Encoders for European Languages</h3>

Alves, André Martins, Ayoub Hammal, Caio Corro, Céline Hudelot, Emmanuel Malherbe, Etienne Malaboeuf, Fanny Jourdan, Gabriel Hautreux, João Alves, Kevin El Haddad, Manuel Faysse, Maxime Peyrard, Nuno M. Guerreiro, Patrick Fernandes, Ricardo Rei, Pierre Colombo

<h3 id="686">[686] Skill-Based Mixture-of-Experts: Adaptive Routing for Heterogeneous Reasoning via Inferred Skills</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, LLM Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：现有LLM组合方法采用粗粒度的任务级专家选择，忽略实例级差异。方法：提出Skill-MoE，基于推理出的技能进行实例级专家选择，并通过批处理策略减少模型加载开销。结果：在多个基准上平均绝对提升8.15%，泛化到未见任务，且无需多轮交互。

<h3 id="687">[687] Enhancing Layer Attention Efficiency through Pruning Redundant Retrievals</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Attention Mechanism, Model Compression, Training Efficiency
- **Summary**: 问题：现有层注意力方法存在冗余，相邻层注意力权重高度相似。方法：提出基于KL散度的冗余量化方法和增强Beta分位数映射（EBQM）来跳过冗余层。结果：所提高效层注意力（ELA）架构在图像分类和目标检测上减少30%训练时间并提升性能。

<h3 id="688">[688] Ideas in Inference-time Scaling can Benefit Generative Pre-training Algorithms</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.07154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.07154) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiaming Song, Linqi Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Inference-Time Scaling, Pre-training, Generative Model
- **Summary**: 问题：生成式预训练常错误地将自回归与扩散对立。方法：重新审视模型族、数据表示、训练目标和推理过程的关系，主张先设计推理过程再设计训练目标。结果：通过DDIM采样器的局限性、多token预测的联合分布限制等例子说明原则，并展示流映射和少步蒸馏方法的优势。

<h3 id="689">[689] A Lightweight Context-Driven Training-Free Network for Scene Text Segmentation and Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2503.15639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15639) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ritabrata Chakraborty, Shivakumara Palaiahnakote, Umapada Pal, Cheng-Lin Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: OCR, Scene Text Segmentation
- **Venue**: ICDAR 2025
- **Summary**: 本文针对实时场景下场景文本识别系统因端到端重模型导致的资源和延迟问题，提出一种无需训练、即插即用的轻量级框架，利用预训练识别器和注意力机制分割阶段优化候选文本区域，在保持与最先进系统相当性能的同时大幅降低计算开销。

<h3 id="690">[690] T1: Tool-integrated Verification for Test-time Compute Scaling in Small Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.04718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.04718) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Minki Kang, Jongwon Jeong, Jaewoong Cho
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, Tool Learning, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Summary**: 本文研究小语言模型在测试时计算扩展中进行验证的能力，发现其在记忆密集型任务上表现不佳，提出Tool-integrated verification (T1)框架，将记忆任务卸载到外部工具（如代码解释器），使1B模型在MATH基准上超越8B模型。

<h3 id="691">[691] MARFT: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Fine-Tuning</h3>

<h3 id="692">[692] GRANITE : a Byzantine-Resilient Dynamic Gossip Learning Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.17471](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.17471) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC
- **Tags**: Gossip Learning, Byzantine Resilience
- **Summary**: 本文指出动态图上的闲话学习易受拜占庭双重攻击，提出GRANITE框架，通过积累节点标识和动态调整聚合阈值，使拜占庭节点影响指数级衰减，在30%拜占庭节点下收敛精度接近非拜占庭场景，通信成本降低达9倍。

<h3 id="693">[693] Erased but Not Forgotten: How Backdoors Compromise Concept Erasure</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2504.21072](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.21072) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tobias Braun, Jonas Henry Grebe, Marcus Rohrbach, Anna Rohrbach
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Backdoor Attack, Model Security
- **Summary**: 本文揭示文本到图像扩散模型中概念擦除方法的漏洞：后门触发器与目标概念绑定后能在擦除后幸存，导致恶意输出。提出的Erasure Evasion Backdoor (EEB)在名人身份擦除中成功率高达82%，为概念擦除技术提供诊断工具。

- **Tags**: LLM Application, Benchmark, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对AI视频聊天系统缺乏系统研究，建立涵盖质量、延迟、机制和开销的基准，评测六款应用，发现网络延迟影响不大而AI能力最关键。

<h3 id="721">[721] Simultaneous Multi-objective Alignment Across Verifiable and Non-verifiable Rewards</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.01167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01167) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiran Shen, Yu Xia, Jonathan Chang, Prithviraj Ammanabrolu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL

- **Summary**: 针对多目标对齐中单个目标冲突和训练低效的问题，提出MAHALO框架，通过多头DPO和PRM引导解码实现可控多目标对齐。在数学推理、人类价值对齐等任务上同时提升多个目标，干扰有限。

<h3 id="722">[722] HRTFformer: A Spatially-Aware Transformer for Individual HRTF Upsampling in Immersive Audio Rendering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.01891](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01891) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xuyi Hu, Jian Li, Shaojie Zhang, Stefan Goetz, Lorenzo Picinali, Ozgur B. Akan, Aidan O. T. Hogg

- **Summary**: 针对个体HRTF测量复杂、难以大规模应用的问题，提出基于Transformer的HRTF上采样模型，在球谐域利用注意力机制捕捉空间相关性。在感知定位模型和谱失真指标上优于现有方法。

<h3 id="723">[723] Verifying Meta-Awareness via Predictive Rewards in Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.03259](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.03259) (replaced)

<h3 id="724">[724] Margin Adaptive DPO: Leveraging Reward Model for Granular Control in Preference Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.05342](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05342) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hyung Gyu Rho
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Preference Optimization, Reward Model

<h3 id="725">[725] Domain-Shift-Aware Conformal Prediction for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.05566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.05566) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhexiao Lin, Yuanyuan Li, Neeraj Sarna, Yuanyuan Gao, Michael von Gablenz
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Conformal Prediction, Uncertainty Estimation, OOD Generalization
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对域偏移下标准保形预测覆盖不足的问题，提出DS-CP框架，通过重加权校准样本适应域偏移。在MMLU基准上提高了覆盖可靠性，同时保持效率。

<h3 id="726">[726] Value Flows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.07650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.07650) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Perry Dong, Chongyi Zheng, Chelsea Finn, Dorsa Sadigh, Benjamin Eysenbach
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Distributional RL, Flow Matching
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/chongyi-zheng/value-flows)
- **Summary**: 针对分布强化学习中收益分布建模不精细的问题，提出Value Flows，利用流匹配目标估计收益分布并识别高不确定性状态。在37个状态和25个图像任务上平均成功率提升1.3倍。

<h3 id="727">[727] SHERLOCK: Towards Dynamic Knowledge Adaptation in LLM-enhanced E-commerce Risk Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.08948](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.08948) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, E-Commerce
- **Summary**: 针对电商风险管理中多源异构数据分析耗时的问题，提出Sherlock框架，整合结构化领域知识与LLM推理，采用两阶段RAG和数据飞轮。在线A/B测试专家接受率达82%，日调查吞吐量提升386.7%。

<h3 id="728">[728] StreamingVLM: Real-Time Understanding for Infinite Video Streams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.09608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.09608) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Long Context, KV Cache
- **Venue**: ICLR 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mit-han-lab/streaming-vlm)
- **Summary**: 针对无限视频流实时理解中延迟和内存占用高的问题，提出StreamingVLM，通过紧凑KV缓存实现流式推理。在Inf-Streams-Eval上胜率66.18%，单H100达到8 FPS。

<h3 id="729">[729] Rethinking RL Evaluation: Can Benchmarks Truly Reveal Failures of RL Methods?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.10541](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10541) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zihan Chen, Yiming Zhang, Hengguang Zhou, Zenghui Ding, Yining Sun, Cho-Jui Hsieh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对当前RL基准无法可靠评估泛化能力的问题，提出诊断套件和OPG指标。发现训练集和测试集性能接近，现有RL方法在分布偏移下泛化差。

<h3 id="730">[730] Catch-Only-One: Non-Transferable Examples for Model-Specific Authorization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.10982](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.10982) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zihan Wang, Zhiyong Ma, Zhongkui Ma, Shuofeng Liu, Akide Liu, Derui Wang, Minhui Xue, Guangdong Bai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Security, Model Security
- **Summary**: 针对数据被未授权模型滥用的问题，提出非迁移样本，在模型特定低敏感子空间内重编码数据。在多种视觉骨干上保持授权模型性能，未授权模型性能崩溃。

<h3 id="731">[731] Characterizing Web Search in The Age of Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.11560](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.11560) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Elisabeth Kirsten, Jost Grosse Perdekamp, Qinyuan Wu, Mihir Upadhyay, Krishna P. Gummadi, Muhammad Bilal Zafar
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对生成式搜索与传统搜索差异不明的问题，系统比较了五个生成搜索系统。发现生成搜索在知识来源、多样性和稳定性上差异显著，现有评估范式无法覆盖。

<h3 id="732">[732] Generative AI and Sales Productivity: Field Experiments in Online Retail</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.12049](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12049) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lu Fang, Zhe Yuan, Kaifu Zhang, Dante Donati, Miklos Sarvary
- **Subjects**: econ.GN; cs.AI
- **Tags**: E-Commerce, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 针对生成式AI对销售生产力影响缺乏因果证据的问题，在电商平台进行大规模随机实验。发现GenAI使销售额最高提升16.3%，主要通过提高转化率，且不恶化售后指标。

<h3 id="733">[733] Learning-To-Measure: In-Context Active Feature Acquisition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.12624](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12624) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuta Kobayashi, Zilin Jing, Jiayu Yao, Hongseok Namkoong, Shalmali Joshi
