<h3 id="734">[734] CaptionFormer: Unified Segmentation, Tracking, and Captioning for Spatio-Temporal Objects</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.14904](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14904) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gabriel Fiastre, Antoine Yang, Cordelia Schmid
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

- **Summary**: 针对密集视频对象描述任务标注昂贵的问题，利用VLM生成合成字幕扩展数据集，并提出端到端模型CaptionFormer。在三个基准上达到最先进结果。

<h3 id="735">[735] Video Reasoning without Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.17045](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.17045) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Deepak Sridhar, Kartikeya Bhardwaj, Jeya Pradha Jeyaraj, Nuno Vasconcelos, Ankita Nayak, Harris Teague
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="736">[736] CARES: Context-Aware Resolution Selector for VLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.19496](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19496) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Moshe Kimhi, Nimrod Shabtay, Raja Giryes, Chaim Baskin, Eli Schwartz
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Token Compression, Model Compression

<h3 id="737">[737] Symbolic Neural Generation with Applications to Lead Discovery in Drug Design</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2510.23379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.23379) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ashwin Srinivasan, Tirtharaj Dash, A Baskar, Michael Bain, Sanjay Kumar Dey, Mainak Banerjee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE; q-bio.BM
- **Tags**: Neurosymbolic AI, Drug Discovery, Inductive Logic Programming
- **Summary**: 针对符号学习与神经推理结合在药物设计中应用不足的问题，本文提出Symbolic Neural Generator (SNG)，整合归纳逻辑编程(ILP)与LLM，生成符合符号规范的新分子。在基准问题上性能与SOTA相当，在探索性问题上生成分子的结合亲和力与领先临床候选分子相当，专家认为符号规范有用。

<h3 id="738">[738] The Geometry of Grokking: Norm Minimization on the Zero-Loss Manifold</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.01938](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.01938) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tiberiu Musat
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Grokking
- **Summary**: 针对Grokking现象（延迟泛化）机制不明确的问题，本文将后记忆化学习视为零损失流形上的权值范数最小化，推导了两层网络第一层动态的闭式表达。实验确认模拟训练过程再现了延迟泛化和表征学习。

<h3 id="739">[739] Who Evaluates AI's Social Impacts? Mapping Coverage and Gaps in First and Third Party Evaluations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.05613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05613) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anka Reuel, Avijit Ghosh, Jenny Chim, Andrew Tran, Yanan Long, Jennifer Mickel, Usman Gohar, Srishti Yadav, Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi, Mowafak Allaham, Hossein A. Rahmani, Mubashara Akhtar, Felix Friedrich, Robert Scholz, Michael Alexander Riegler, Jan Batzner, Eliya Habba, Arushi Saxena, Anastassia Kornilova, Kevin Wei, Prajna Soni, Yohan Mathew, Kevin Klyman, Jeba Sania, Subramanyam Sahoo, Olivia Beyer Bruvik, Pouya Sadeghi, Sujata Goswami, Angelina Wang, Yacine Jernite, Zeerak Talat, Stella Biderman, Mykel Kochenderfer, Sanmi Koyejo, Irene Solaiman
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI Evaluation, AI Ethics
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对基础模型社交影响评估（偏见、公平、隐私等）中第一方和第三方评估不均衡的问题，本文首次全面分析186份第一方报告和248份第三方评估来源，并辅以开发者访谈。发现第一方报告稀疏且下降，第三方覆盖更广更严格，但只有开发者能报告部分关键信息，建议强制透明。

<h3 id="740">[740] Optimizing Diversity and Quality through Base-Aligned Model Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.05650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05650) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yichen Wang, Chenghao Yang, Tenghao Huang, Muhao Chen, Jonathan May, Mina Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对对齐降低LLM输出多样性的问题，本文提出Base-Aligned Model Collaboration (BACo)，在推理时基于不确定性和内容信号动态路由每个token从基础模型或对齐模型解码。在13个多样性/质量指标上超越SOTA，最佳路由器实现21.3%的联合改进。

<h3 id="741">[741] NILC: Discovering New Intents with LLM-assisted Clustering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.05913](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.05913) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongtao Wang, Renchi Yang, Wenqing Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Intent Discovery, LLM Clustering
- **Summary**: 针对新意图发现（NID）中级联流水线无法相互反馈且嵌入聚类忽视文本语义的问题，本文提出NILC迭代聚类框架，利用LLM创建语义质心、增强困难样本，并在半监督设置中注入监督。在六个数据集上显著优于多个基线。

<h3 id="742">[742] RoboBenchMart: Benchmarking Robots in Retail Environment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.10276](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.10276) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Konstantin Soshin, Alexander Krapukhin, Andrei Spiridonov, Gregorii Bukhtuev, Andrey Kuznetsov, Vlad Shakhuro, Denis Shepelev
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Benchmark, Retail Robotics
- **Summary**: 针对现有机器人操作基准未验证在零售环境中泛化性的问题，本文提出RoboBenchMart，包含零售暗仓环境的移动操作任务。评估多个SOTA VLA模型发现即使在常见零售任务上仍困难，表明尚未真正跨域泛化。

<h3 id="743">[743] Latent Reasoning in TRMs is Secretly a Policy Improvement Operator</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.16886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.16886) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Arip Asadulaev, Rayan Banerjee, Fakhri Karray, Martin Takac
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Latent Reasoning, Policy Improvement
- **Summary**: 针对小模型潜在递归推理何时有效的问题，本文证明潜在递归推理可形式化为策略改进算法，并提出强化学习和扩散训练方案。避免死计算步骤，减少总前向传递18倍且保持性能。

<h3 id="744">[744] Evaluating the Performance of Deep Learning Models in Whole-body Dynamic 3D Posture Prediction During Load-reaching Activities</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.20615](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20615) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Seyede Niloofar Hosseini, Ali Mojibi, Mahdi Mohseni, Navid Arjmand, Alireza Taheri
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Pose Estimation, Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 针对全身动态姿势预测在负载取物活动中的深度学习模型评估问题，本文使用BLSTM和Transformer，并新提出通过优化成本函数强制身体段长度恒定。新成本函数将手臂/腿部误差降低8%/21%，Transformer比BLSTM长时性能好58%，RMSE 41.4mm。

<h3 id="745">[745] Latent Collaboration in Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.20639](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.20639) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiaru Zou, Ruizhong Qiu, Gaotang Li, Xiyuan Yang, Katherine Tieu, Pan Lu, Ke Shen, Hanghang Tong, Yejin Choi, Jingrui He, James Zou, Mengdi Wang, Ling Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Latent Space Collaboration
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Gen-Verse/LatentMAS)
- **Summary**: 针对现有LLM代理依赖文本中介通信效率低的问题，本文提出LatentMAS无训练框架，使代理在连续潜在空间直接协作，通过共享潜在工作记忆保留隐层嵌入。在9个基准上准确率提升14.6%，输出token减少70.8%-83.7%，推理加速4-4.3倍。

<h3 id="746">[746] Understanding the Effects of Distractors on Reasoning Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2511.21397](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.21397) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiyun Bae, Hyunjong Ok, Sangwoo Mo, Jaeho Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Distractor Analysis
- **Summary**: 针对视觉干扰物如何影响VLM测试时扩展的问题，本文创建Idis数据集，系统变化干扰物维度，分析推理轨迹，并提出简单提示策略。发现视觉干扰物降低准确率但不增加推理长度，与文本干扰物现象不同，简单提示可缓解。

<h3 id="747">[747] SpeedAug: Policy Acceleration via Tempo-Enriched Policy and RL Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.00062](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00062) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Taewook Nam, Junmo Cho, Youngsoo Jang, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Policy Acceleration, Reinforcement Learning, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对从演示学习的机器人策略执行速度慢的问题，本文提出SpeedAug，先学习速度增强的先验策略，再通过RL微调优化执行速度。真实操作任务吞吐量提升1.8倍，仅需16分钟在线交互，成功率不降。

<h3 id="748">[748] From Segments to Scenes: Temporal Understanding in Autonomous Driving via Vision-Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.05277](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05277) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kevin Cannons, Saeed Ranjbar Alvar, Mohammad Asiful Hossain, Ahmad Rezaei, Mohsen Gholami, Alireza Heidarikhazaei, Zhou Weimin, Yong Zhang, Mohammad Akbari
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Temporal Understanding, Visual Question Answering
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/vbdi/tad_bench)
- **Summary**: 针对自动驾驶需要时间理解而现有VLM不足且缺乏专门基准的问题，本文提出TAD基准（近6000 QA对），并提Scene-CoT和TCogMap两种无训练方案。集成后TAD准确率提升17.72%，STSBench提升10.35%。

<h3 id="749">[749] ShelfAware: Real-Time Semantic Localization in Quasi-Static Environments with Low-Cost Sensors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.09065](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.09065) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shivendra Agrawal, Jake Brawer, Ashutosh Naik, Alessandro Roncone, Bradley Hayes
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Semantic Localization, Particle Filter
- **Summary**: 针对准静态室内环境中标准视觉定位失败的问题，本文提出ShelfAware语义粒子滤波器，融合深度似然和类别语义相似度，使用逆语义提议。模拟环境97%全局定位成功率，实际商店显著优于基线。

<h3 id="750">[750] VocSim: A Training-free Benchmark for Zero-shot Content Identity in Single-source Audio</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.10120](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10120) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Maris Basha, Anja Zai, Sabine Stoll, Richard Hahnloser
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Audio Representation, Zero-Shot Learning, Bioacoustics
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/vocsim/benchmark)
- **Summary**: 针对缺乏训练无关的音频零样本内容身份基准的问题，本文提出VocSim基准，包含12.5万单源片段，评估冻结嵌入的Precision@k和GSR。Whisper特征+时频池化+PCA取得强零样本性能，但低资源语音有差距；外部验证在HEAR上SOTA。

<h3 id="751">[751] InFerActive: Interactive Tree-Based Exploration of LLM Sampling for Safety Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.10234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10234) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junhyeong Hwangbo, Soohyun Lee, Hyeon Jeon, Kyochul Jang, Minsoo Cheong, Youngjae Yu, Jinwook Seo
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Interactive Visualization, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对人工评估LLM安全时随机采样产生大量近重复前缀效率低的问题，本文提出InFerActive交互系统，将采样结果可视化为短语树，使用广度优先采样。覆盖同等有害响应所需样本减少5倍，用户研究显著提升效率和覆盖。

<h3 id="752">[752] Calibrating Uncertainty for Zero-Shot Adversarial CLIP</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.12997](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.12997) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wenjing Lu, Zerui Tao, Yuning Qiu, Dongping Zhang, Yang Yang, Qibin Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Adversarial Robustness, Uncertainty Calibration, CLIP
- **Summary**: 针对CLIP零样本分类易受对抗攻击且现有微调忽视不确定性校准的问题，本文提出对抗微调目标，将CLIP输出重参数化为狄利克雷分布浓度参数，实现分布对齐。显著改善不确定性校准，同时保持干净准确率和竞争性鲁棒性。

<h3 id="753">[753] Control of a Twin Rotor using Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.13356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.13356) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zeyad Gamal, Youssef Mahran, Ayman El-Badawy
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Control Theory
- **Summary**: 问题：传统控制方法难以控制具有复杂动力学和非线性特性的双转子气动系统（TRAS）。方法：采用Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3)强化学习算法进行控制和稳定。结果：仿真表明该方法有效，在风扰下相比PID更具鲁棒性，并在实验室装置上验证了实际效果。

<h3 id="754">[754] Ev-Trust: An Evolutionarily Stable Trust Mechanism for Decentralized LLM-Based Multi-Agent Service Economies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.16167](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16167) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiye Wang, Shiduo Yang, Ting Qiao, Jiayu Qin, Jianbin Li, Yu Wang, Yuanhe Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.MA; cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 问题：去中心化LLM多代理服务经济面临信任脆弱性，可能导致信任崩溃。方法：提出Ev-Trust，一种进化稳定信任机制，通过交叉验证、方差标准化漂移过滤和将信任信号嵌入收益函数来保障合作。结果：在100轮模拟中，Ev-Trust将恶意代理参与降低约60%，欺诈服务率降低约50%，并在30%对抗突变下维持信任分化。

<h3 id="755">[755] Agent Tools Orchestration Leaks More: Dataset, Benchmark, and Mitigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.16310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.16310) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxuan Qiao, Dongqin Liu, Hongchang Yang, Wei Zhou, Songlin Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Privacy, Tool Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/1Ponder/TOP-R)
- **Summary**: 问题：LLM代理组合使用多个工具时，可能从非敏感工具返回中推断出敏感结论（工具编排隐私风险）。方法：形式化TOP-R，构建LRSE流水线生成TOP-Bench基准，并提出TOP-Align后训练缓解方法。结果：六个LLM代理的平均泄漏率达88.6%，H-score仅20.4；TOP-Align在单独评估集上提升H-score 16.2点，远超提示式缓解的4.9点。

<h3 id="756">[756] MGRegBench: A Novel Benchmark Dataset with Anatomical Landmarks for Mammography Image Registration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.17605](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.17605) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Svetlana Krasnova, Emiliya Starikova, Ilia Naletov, Andrey Krylov, Dmitry Sorokin
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Medical Imaging, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 问题：乳腺X线图像配准领域缺乏透明公开数据集和可复现的标准化基准。方法：提出MGRegBench，包含5000+图像对（100对带手动标注解剖标志），实施泄漏控制评估协议。结果：对各种配准方法进行了公平比较，并在独立数据集上外部验证，公开代码和数据。

<h3 id="757">[757] Reinforcement Learning Position Control of a Quadrotor Using Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.18333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18333) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Youssef Mahran, Zeyad Gamal, Ayman El-Badawy
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Robotics, Control Theory
- **Summary**: 问题：传统四旋翼直接控制四个旋翼转速的方法复杂。方法：提出基于Soft Actor-Critic (SAC)的推力矢量控制架构，RL代理输出推力比例和期望横滚/俯仰角，再由姿态PID转换为转速。结果：训练速度比传统RPM控制器更快，路径跟踪更平滑准确。

<h3 id="758">[758] Dynamic Entropy Tuning in Reinforcement Learning Low-Level Quadcopter Control: Stochasticity vs Determinism</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.18336](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.18336) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Youssef Mahran, Zeyad Gamal, Ayman El-Badawy
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Robotics, Control Theory
- **Summary**: 问题：强化学习中随机策略与确定性策略在四旋翼控制中的性能比较，以及动态熵调优的影响。方法：使用SAC（随机策略，静态和动态熵）与TD3（确定性策略）进行对比。结果：动态熵调优通过防止灾难性遗忘和提升探索效率，对四旋翼控制有积极效果。

<h3 id="759">[759] Uncovering Competency Gaps in Large Language Models and Their Benchmarks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.20638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.20638) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Maty Bohacek, Nino Scherrer, Nicholas Dufour, Thomas Leung, Christoph Bregler, Stephanie C. Y. Chan
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 问题：标准化基准可能掩盖模型在特定子领域的弱点和基准本身的不平衡覆盖。方法：提出利用稀疏自编码器的概念激活自动发现细粒度差距。结果：无监督方法恢复了文献中已知的模型差距（如奉承行为），并识别了新的模型差距和基准差距。

<h3 id="760">[760] Avatar Forcing: Real-Time Interactive Head Avatar Generation for Natural Conversation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.00664](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00664) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Taekyung Ki, Sangwon Jang, Jaehyeong Jo, Jaehong Yoon, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.HC; cs.MM
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Video Generation, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：现有会说话头像生成模型缺乏实时交互性和情感参与，多为单向响应。方法：提出Avatar Forcing框架，通过扩散强迫实现低延迟实时交互，并利用直接偏好优化学习富有表现力的反应。结果：实现约500ms延迟实时交互，速度提升6.8倍，在80%以上情况下优于基线。

<h3 id="761">[761] VLM4VLA: Revisiting Vision-Language-Models in Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.03309](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03309) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jianke Zhang, Xiaoyu Chen, Qiuyue Wang, Mingsheng Li, Yanjiang Guo, Yucheng Hu, Jiajun Zhang, Shuai Bai, Junyang Lin, Jianyu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Embodied AI, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉-语言模型（VLM）的选择和能力如何影响下游视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略性能？方法：提出VLM4VLA最小适配流程，仅用少量可学习参数高效比较VLM。结果：VLM初始化有持续益处，但通用能力不能预测下游任务性能；视觉模块是性能瓶颈，注入控制相关监督可提升表现。

<h3 id="762">[762] Paradoxical noise preference in RNNs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.04539](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04539) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Noah Eckstein, Manoj Srinivasan
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Recurrent Neural Network, Computational Neuroscience
- **Venue**: Transactions on Machine Learning Research (TMLR), 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：在循环神经网络（RNN）中，训练时注入噪声通常预期测试时去除噪声能保持或提升性能，但发现相反现象。方法：分析连续时间RNN（CTRNN），发现噪声偏好源于激活函数内噪声引起的固定点偏移。结果：网络对训练噪声本身过拟合，而非仅对数据，这种偏好仅出现在激活函数内噪声注入的情况。

<h3 id="763">[763] Prototypicality Bias Reveals Blindspots in Multimodal Evaluation Metrics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.04946](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04946) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Subhadeep Roy, Gagan Bhatia, Steffen Eger
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Benchmark, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：文本到图像评估指标可能偏好视觉或社会原型图像，而非语义正确的图像（原型性偏差）。方法：构建PROTOBIAS诊断基准（涉及动物、物体、人口统计），并提出PROTOSCORE作为缓解基线。结果：多种常用指标在对比测试中失败，而人类判断更忠实于语义正确性。

<h3 id="764">[764] FastSLM: Hierarchical Temporal Abstraction for Efficient Long-Form Speech Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.06199](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06199) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junseok Lee, Sangyong Lee, Chang-Jae Chun
- **Subjects**: eess.AS; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Speech Processing, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 问题：多模态大语言模型扩展到长语音时，输入令牌数量爆炸。方法：提出FastSLM，采用分层时间抽象器（HTA）渐进蒸馏非重叠声学特征，达到1.67令牌/秒的极端压缩率（减少97%）。结果：在长语音基准上以更少FLOPs和参数取得竞争性能。

<h3 id="765">[765] Safe-FedLLM: Delving into the Safety of Federated Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.07177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07177) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mingxiang Tao, Yu Tian, Wenxuan Tu, Yue Yang, Xue Yang, Xiangyan Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, LLM Security, LoRA
- **Summary**: 问题：联邦学习中大语言模型（FedLLM）面临恶意客户端攻击，现有工作多关注效率，安全性探索不足。方法：提出Safe-FedLLM，基于探测的防御框架，利用LoRA更新的高维行为特征通过轻量分类器区分恶意客户端。结果：有效提升FedLLM对恶意客户端的鲁棒性，不影响训练速度，在高恶意比例下仍有效。

<h3 id="766">[766] DSA-Tokenizer: Disentangled Semantic-Acoustic Tokenization via Flow Matching-based Hierarchical Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.09239](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09239) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hanlin Zhang, Daxin Tan, Dehua Tao, Xiao Chen, Haochen Tan, Yunhe Li, Yuchen Cao, Linqi Song
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Speech Processing, Text-to-Speech, Disentanglement
- **Summary**: 问题：现有语音分词器无法完全分离语义和声学信息，要么语义与风格纠缠，要么解纠缠不完整。方法：提出DSA-Tokenizer，通过不同优化目标显式解耦为语义令牌和声学令牌，并引入分层Flow Matching解码器和GAN蒸馏加速推理。结果：实现强解纠缠、可控语音克隆和高效高质量生成，WER/CER低。

<h3 id="767">[767] Hot-Start Chinese Language Modeling:Visual Glyphs Accelerate Sample-Efficient Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.09566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09566) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shuyang Xiang, Hao Guan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, Language Modeling, Tokenization
- **Summary**: 问题：中文语言建模中，视觉字形图像是否能作为归纳偏置？方法：将汉字渲染为图像输入，与基于索引的模型对比。结果：视觉输入产生显著的冷启动效应（第一轮精度翻倍），但最终精度与基线相同（39%），表明视觉表示加速早期学习但不提高最终容量。

<h3 id="768">[768] MASCOT: Towards Multi-Agent Socio-Collaborative Companion Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.14230](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14230) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiyang Wang, Yiqiao Jin, Alex Cabral, Josiah Hester
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Dialogue System
- **Summary**: 问题：多代理社交协作系统常出现角色崩溃和社会谄媚，导致对话冗余同质。方法：提出MASCOT框架，采用双层优化策略：个体层的RLAIF驱动行为对齐和群体层的协作对话优化。结果：角色一致性提升+14.1，社会贡献提升+10.6，产生更一致、更少冗余的多代理对话。

<h3 id="769">[769] SilentDrift: Exploiting Action Chunking for Stealthy Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.14323](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14323) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bingxin Xu, Yuzhang Shang, Binghui Wang, Emilio Ferrara
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Backdoor Attack, Embodied AI
- **Venue**: ACL Findings 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：视觉-语言-动作模型中的动作分块和增量位姿表示存在安全漏洞。方法：提出SILENTDRIFT，一种利用平滑步进函数构建连续扰动并采用关键帧攻击策略的隐蔽后门攻击。结果：在LIBERO上达到93.2%攻击成功率，低于2%投毒率，95.3%干净任务成功率。

<h3 id="770">[770] Physics-Encoded Inverse Modeling for Arctic Snow Depth Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.17074](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17074) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Akila Sampath, Vandana Janeja, Jianwu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Inverse Problem
- **Summary**: 问题：稀疏观测下时变逆问题（如雪深估计）难以准确求解。方法：提出PhysE-Inv框架，结合LSTM编码器-解码器、对比学习和物理信息约束。结果：平均MSE降低24.7%，比最强基线提升17.3%。

<h3 id="771">[771] A Monosemantic Attribution Framework for Stable Interpretability in Clinical Neuroscience Transformer-Based Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.17952](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17952) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michail Mamalakis, Tiago Azevedo, Cristian Cosentino, Chiara D&#39;Ercoli, Subati Abulikemu, Zhongtian Sun, Richard Bethlehem, Pietro Lio
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Mechanistic Interpretability, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 问题：临床语言模型解释性差，归因方法不稳定。方法：提出统一解释框架，通过单义特征提取整合归因和机械论解释。结果：生成稳定的输入级重要性分数，推进可信赖部署。

<h3 id="772">[772] Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Tactical Decision Making for Trucks in Highway Traffic</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.18783](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18783) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Deepthi Pathare, Leo Laine, Morteza Haghir Chehreghani
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Objective Optimization, Autonomous Driving, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：重型卡车高速公路决策中安全、效率成本难以平衡。方法：基于PPO的多目标强化学习框架，学习帕累托最优策略。结果：获得平滑可解释的帕累托前沿，支持策略间无缝切换。

<h3 id="773">[773] ELF: A Family of Encoder-Free ECG-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.18798](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18798) (replaced)
- **Authors**: William Han, Tony Chen, Chaojing Duan, Xiaoyu Song, Yihang Yao, Yuzhe Yang, Michael A. Rosenberg, Emerson Liu, Ding Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ELM-Research/ECG-Language-Models)
- **Summary**: 问题：现有心电语言模型依赖预训练编码器，架构复杂训练繁琐。方法：ELF系列无编码器心电语言模型，简化设计。结果：在更简单架构下达到或超越先前最佳性能。

<h3 id="774">[774] ASKD-Whisper: Adaptive Self-knowledge Distillation for Efficient and Low-Latency Automatic Speech Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.19919](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19919) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junseok Lee, Nahun Kim, Sangyong Lee, Chang-Jae Chun
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Automatic Speech Recognition, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 问题：ASR知识蒸馏导致教师过拟合和泛化下降。方法：自适应自知识蒸馏框架，动态衰减教师依赖。结果：5倍推理加速，词错误率降低1.07%。

<h3 id="775">[775] Demystifying Multi-Agent Debate: The Role of Confidence and Diversity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.19921](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19921) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xiaochen Zhu, Caiqi Zhang, Yizhou Chi, Tom Stafford, Nigel Collier, Andreas Vlachos
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Reasoning, Confidence Calibration
- **Summary**: 问题：多智能体辩论效果常不如多数投票。方法：提出多样性初始化和置信度调制辩论协议。结果：在六个推理QA基准上优于普通辩论和多数投票。

<h3 id="776">[776] How Much Progress Has There Been in NVIDIA Datacenter GPUs?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.20115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20115) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Emanuele Del Sozzo, Martin Fleming, Kenneth Flamm, Neil Thompson
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: GPU Computing, Survey
- **Summary**: 问题：分析NVIDIA数据中心GPU的技术进步。方法：量化分析性能指标（倍周期、内存、功耗等）。结果：FP16/32密集运算倍周期约1.5年，内存带宽增长较慢，出口管制可能缩小差距。

<h3 id="777">[777] APB-V: Accelerating Long-Video Understanding via Sequence-Parallelism-aware Approximate Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21444) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxiang Huang, Mingye Li, Xu Han, Chaojun Xiao, Weilin Zhao, Ao Sun, Ziqi Yuan, Hao Zhou, Fandong Meng, Zhiyuan Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Attention Mechanism, Parallel Computing
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/thunlp/APB)
- **Summary**: 问题：长视频推理因密集预填充计算效率低。方法：APB-V序列并行框架，采用近似注意力。结果：相比FlashAttn加速12.72倍，性能无明显损失。

<h3 id="778">[778] When Does Predictive Inverse Dynamics Outperform Behavior Cloning?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.21718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21718) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lukas Schäfer, Pallavi Choudhury, Abdelhak Lemkhenter, Chris Lovett, Somjit Nath, Luis França, Matheus Ribeiro Furtado de Mendonça, Alex Lamb, Riashat Islam, Siddhartha Sen, John Langford, Katja Hofmann, Sergio Valcarcel Macua
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imitation Learning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：行为克隆在专家演示不足时性能差。方法：理论解释预测逆动力学模型的偏差-方差权衡。结果：行为克隆需要最多5倍演示才能达到相同性能。

<h3 id="779">[779] GUDA: Counterfactual Group-wise Training Data Attribution for Diffusion Models via Unlearning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.22651](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22651) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Naoki Murata, Yuhta Takida, Chieh-Hsin Lai, Toshimitsu Uesaka, Bac Nguyen, Stefano Ermon, Yuki Mitsufuji
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Attribution, Diffusion Model, Machine Unlearning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/sony/guda)
- **Summary**: 问题：生成模型的分组训练数据归因计算成本高。方法：GUDA利用机器遗忘近似反事实模型，基于ELBO评分。结果：更可靠的归因，速度提升约100倍。

<h3 id="780">[780] Med-Scout: Curing MLLMs' Geometric Blindness in Medical Perception via Geometry-Aware RL Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2601.23220](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23220) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Anglin Liu, Ruichao Chen, Yi Lu, Hongxia Xu, Jintai Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Geometric Reasoning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 问题：多模态大语言模型在医学感知中存在几何盲点。方法：Med-Scout通过强化学习和三个代理任务进行后训练。结果：在基准上性能提升超过40%，泛化到其他医学VQA任务。

<h3 id="781">[781] Zero-Shot Off-Policy Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.01962](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01962) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Arip Asadulaev, Maksim Bobrin, Salem Lahlou, Dmitry Dylov, Fakhri Karray, Martin Takac
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Off-Policy RL, Zero-Shot Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 问题：离线策略学习在零样本设置下面临分布偏移和高估偏差。方法：发现后继度量与平稳密度比的理论联系，推断最优重要性采样比。结果：在多个任务上实现快速适应，无需额外训练。

<h3 id="782">[782] Probabilistic Performance Guarantees for Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02098](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02098) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yannik Schnitzer, Mathias Jackermeier, Alessandro Abate, David Parker
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Task Learning, Reinforcement Learning, Generalization Bound
- **Summary**: 问题：多任务强化学习缺乏正式性能保证。方法：组合每任务置信下界与任务级泛化的泛化界。结果：在现实样本量下保证理论有效且信息丰富。

<h3 id="783">[783] naPINN: Noise-Adaptive Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Recovering Physics from Corrupted Measurement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02547](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02547) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hankyeol Kim, Pilsung Kang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Inverse Problem
- **Summary**: 问题：物理信息神经网络在复杂噪声下性能下降。方法：naPINN嵌入能量模型学习残差分布，可训练可靠性门控和拒绝代价正则化。结果：在非高斯噪声和异常值下显著优于现有鲁棒PINN。

<h3 id="784">[784] The Alignment Curse: Modality Alignment Supercharges Audio Attacks via Text Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02557](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02557) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yupeng Chen, Junchi Yu, Aoxi Liu, Baoyuan Wu, Philip Torr, Adel Bibi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.SD
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, AI Safety, Jailbreak Attack
- **Summary**: 问题：模态对齐可能将文本安全漏洞转移到音频。方法：定义“对齐诅咒”原则，全面评估文本、文本迁移音频和音频攻击。结果：文本迁移音频攻击表现与音频攻击相当或更好，对齐程度与迁移效果正相关。

<h3 id="785">[785] Mixture of Concept Bottleneck Experts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.02886](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02886) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Francesco De Santis, Gabriele Ciravegna, Giovanni De Felice, Arianna Casanova, Francesco Giannini, Michelangelo Diligenti, Johannes Schneider, Danilo Giordano, Mateo Espinosa Zarlenga, Pietro Barbiero
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mixture-of-Experts, Symbolic Regression
- **Summary**: 现有概念瓶颈模型（CBM）使用单一表达式，限制了预测准确性和适应性。本文提出混合概念瓶颈专家（M-CBE），通过多个专家和函数形式（如线性、符号回归）泛化CBM。实验表明，该方法能有效权衡准确性与可解释性。

<h3 id="786">[786] Consistency Deep Equilibrium Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03024](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03024) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junchao Lin, Zenan Ling, Jingwen Xu, Robert C. Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Inference Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/landrarwolf/CDEQ)
- **Summary**: 深度平衡模型（DEQ）推理延迟高，因为需要迭代求解固定点。本文提出一致性DEQ（C-DEQ），利用一致性蒸馏将中间状态直接映射到固定点，实现少步推理。实验显示，相同推理预算下，准确性提升2-20倍。

<h3 id="787">[787] Lookahead Sample Reward Guidance for Test-Time Scaling of Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03211](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03211) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yeongmin Kim, Donghyeok Shin, Byeonghu Na, Minsang Park, Richard Lee Kim, Il-Chul Moon
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Inference-Time Scaling
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/aailab-kaist/Diffusion-LiDAR-Sampling)
- **Summary**: 扩散模型生成的样本可能不符合人类意图。本文提出LiDAR采样，利用边缘样本计算期望未来奖励，实现无反向传播的闭式奖励引导。在GenEval指标上达到与梯度引导相同的性能，速度提升9.5倍。

<h3 id="788">[788] Global Geometry Is Not Enough for Vision Representations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03282](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03282) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiwan Chung, Seon Joo Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Compositional Reasoning
- **Summary**: 通常认为全局嵌入几何能表征视觉表示能力，但其对组合绑定不敏感。本文发现功能敏感性（输入-输出雅可比矩阵）能可靠跟踪组合绑定，而全局几何统计量与组合绑定的相关性接近零。证明了全局几何仅捕捉部分表征能力。

<h3 id="789">[789] When Single Answer Is Not Enough: Rethinking Single-Step Retrosynthesis Benchmarks for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03554](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03554) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bogdan Zagribelnyy, Ivan Ilin, Maksim Kuznetsov, Nikita Bondarev, Mathieu Reymond, Roman Schutski, Thomas MacDougall, Rim Shayakhmetov, Zulfat Miftakhutdinov, Mikolaj Mizera, Vladimir Aladinskiy, Alex Aliper, Alex Zhavoronkov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CE; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Molecular Reasoning
- **Summary**: 现有逆合成基准依赖单一真值，无法反映真实合成规划的开放性。本文提出基于化学合理性指标ChemCensor的基准框架，并构建CREED数据集。基于CREED训练的模型在基准上优于LLM基线。

<h3 id="790">[790] Equilibrium Propagation for Non-Conservative Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03670) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Antonino Emanuele Scurria, Dimitri Vanden Abeele, Bortolo Matteo Mognetti, Serge Massar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.NE; math.DS
- **Tags**: Equilibrium Propagation, Dynamical Systems
- **Summary**: 平衡传播（EP）原限于保守系统。本文将其推广到非保守系统（如前馈网络），通过在学习阶段修改动力学来获得精确梯度。实验表明，该算法性能优于先前方法，且学习更快。

<h3 id="791">[791] Universal One-third Time Scaling in Learning Peaked Distributions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03685](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03685) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yizhou Liu, Ziming Liu, Cengiz Pehlevan, Jeff Gore
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Scaling Laws
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 训练LLM时损失呈幂律收敛，其原因存在争议。本文通过理论分析和实验证明，softmax和交叉熵学习尖峰分布（如下一个token）时，会导致损失和梯度以幂律衰减，且指数为1/3。这为神经缩放律提供了物理解释。

<h3 id="792">[792] Fixed Budget is No Harder Than Fixed Confidence in Best-Arm Identification up to Logarithmic Factors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.03972](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03972) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kapilan Balagopalan, Yinan Li, Yao Zhao, Tuan Nguyen, Anton Daitche, Houssam Nassif, Kwang-Sung Jun
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multi-Armed Bandit, Best-Arm Identification
- **Summary**: 对于最佳臂识别问题，固定预算（FB）和固定置信度（FC）设置的最优样本复杂度存在关系。本文提出FC2FB元算法，可将FC算法转化为FB算法，并证明FB的样本复杂度不超过FC的对数因子。

<h3 id="793">[793] From Evaluation to Design: Using Potential Energy Surface Smoothness Metrics to Guide Machine Learning Interatomic Potential Architectures</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.04861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04861) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ryan Liu, Eric Qu, Tobias Kreiman, Samuel M. Blau, Aditi S. Krishnapriyan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Materials Science, Physics-Informed Learning, Interatomic Potentials
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ryanliu30/bsct.git)
- **Summary**: 机器学习原子间势（MLIP）有时无法复现势能面的平滑性。本文提出键平滑性测试（BSCT），可高效检测非平滑性。通过BSCT指导模型设计，使Transformer骨干网络在保持低误差的同时实现稳定分子动力学模拟。

<h3 id="794">[794] Optimal Bayesian Stopping for Efficient Inference of Consistent LLM Answers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05395) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jingkai Huang, Will Ma, Zhengyuan Zhou
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, LLM Evaluation, Bayesian Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 为提高LLM推理准确性，可多次采样并选择一致答案。本文提出基于贝叶斯先验的L-聚合停止策略，仅需L=3即可达到渐近最优。实验证明，该策略可在不损失准确性的情况下，将LLM调用次数减少50%。

<h3 id="795">[795] Better Source, Better Flow: Learning Condition-Dependent Source Distribution for Flow Matching</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05951](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05951) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junwan Kim, Jiho Park, Seonghu Jeon, Seungryong Kim
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 流匹配通常使用高斯源分布。本文提出学习条件依赖的源分布，并采用方差正则化和方向对齐。实验表明，该方法在多个文本到图像基准上一致提升性能，FID收敛速度提升3倍。

<h3 id="796">[796] Inverse Depth Scaling From Most Layers Being Similar</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.05970](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.05970) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yizhou Liu, Sara Kangaslahti, Ziming Liu, Jeff Gore
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.DS; stat.ML
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Scaling Laws, Transformer Architecture
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文研究深度对LLM损失的影响，发现损失与深度成反比，原因在于大多数层功能相似，通过集成平均而非组合学习或离散化平滑动力学来降低误差。这提示需要架构创新以鼓励组合性地利用深度。

<h3 id="797">[797] Principle-Evolvable Scientific Discovery via Uncertainty Minimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06448) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yingming Pu, Tao Lin, Hongyu Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Scientific Discovery, Bayesian Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/amair-lab/PiEvo)
- **Summary**: 基于LLM的科学智能体因固定先验而效率低下。本文提出PiEvo框架，将科学发现视为原则空间上的贝叶斯优化，通过信息导向假设选择和异常驱动增强，实现原理演进。在四个基准上平均解决方案质量达90.81%-93.15%，收敛步数加速83.3%。

<h3 id="798">[798] "Do Not Mention This to the User": Detecting and Understanding Malicious Agent Skills</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.06547](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.06547) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yi Liu, Zhihao Chen, Yanjun Zhang, Gelei Deng, Yuekang Li, Jianting Ning, Leo Yu Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.ET
- **Tags**: LLM Security, LLM Agent, Cybersecurity
- **Venue**: USENIX Security 2026
- **Summary**: LLM编码智能体依赖第三方技能，但安全影响未研究。本文分析98,380个技能，识别出157个恶意技能，包含632个漏洞和13种攻击技术。发现两个主导攻击策略，并揭示攻击复杂性与隐藏投入的相关性。负责任披露后，所有恶意技能已被删除。

<h3 id="799">[799] Rethinking Scientific Modeling: Toward Physically Consistent and Simulation-Executable Programmatic Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07083](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07083) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yongqing Jiang, Jianze Wang, Zhiqi Shen, Zhenghong Lin, Jiayuan Wang, Yijian Yang, Kaoshan Dai, Haoran Luo
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Program Synthesis, Scientific Discovery
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Jovanqing/AutoBM)
- **Summary**: LLM生成建模代码常存在物理不一致。本文提出物理一致自动建模框架，包括领域知识构建、约束对齐和验证驱动评估。引入CivilInstruct数据集和MBEval基准，两阶段微调策略显著减少幻觉和不一致输出。

<h3 id="800">[800] Collaborative and Efficient Fine-tuning: Leveraging Task Similarity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07218) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Gagik Magakyan, Amirhossein Reisizadeh, Chanwoo Park, Pablo A. Parrilo, Asuman Ozdaglar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning, LoRA, Multi-Task Learning
- **Summary**: 针对基础模型微调数据稀缺，提出CoLoRA，利用任务相似性进行协作微调，通过共享适配器和个性化适配器提升性能。在线性回归理论上提供保证，自然语言实验表明相似任务共同训练能显著提升个体性能。

<h3 id="801">[801] Principled Synthetic Data Enables the First Scaling Laws for LLMs in Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07298](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07298) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Benyu Zhang, Qiang Zhang, Jianpeng Cheng, Hong-You Chen, Qifei Wang, Wei Sun, Shen Li, Jia Li, Jiahao Wu, Qunshu Zhang, Neeraj Bhatia, Xiangjun Fan, Hong Yan
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Data Synthesis, Scaling Laws
- **Summary**: 针对推荐系统中LLM缺乏可预测缩放定律的问题，提出了一种分层合成数据生成框架，构建教学课程。实验表明，基于合成数据的序列模型在召回率上提升130%，并首次证明了LLM在推荐领域的幂律缩放规律。

<h3 id="802">[802] SoK: DARPA's AI Cyber Challenge (AIxCC): Competition Design, Architectures, and Lessons Learned</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.07666](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.07666) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Cen Zhang, Younggi Park, Fabian Fleischer, Yu-Fu Fu, Jiho Kim, Dongkwan Kim, Youngjoon Kim, Qingxiao Xu, Andrew Chin, Ze Sheng, Hanqing Zhao, Michael Pelican, David J. Musliner, Jeff Huang, Jon Silliman, Mikel Mcdaniel, Jefferson Casavant, Isaac Goldthwaite, Nicholas Vidovich, Matthew Lehman, Taesoo Kim

<h3 id="803">[803] Picasso: Holistic Scene Reconstruction with Physics-Constrained Sampling</h3>

<h3 id="804">[804] When and How Much to Imagine: Adaptive Test-Time Scaling with World Models for Visual Spatial Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08236](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08236) (replaced)

<h3 id="805">[805] Predicting Future Utility: Global Combinatorial Optimization for Task-Agnostic KV Cache Eviction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08585](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08585) (replaced)

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, KV Cache, Cache Management
- **Summary**: 现有KV缓存淘汰方法忽略注意力头异质性。提出LU-KV，将头部预算分配建模为全局组合优化，最大化长期边际贡献。在LongBench和RULER上减少80%缓存，性能损失极小。

<h3 id="806">[806] AnomSeer: Reinforcing Multimodal LLMs to Reason for Time-Series Anomaly Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.08868](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08868) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Junru Zhang, Lang Feng, Haoran Shi, Xu Guo, Han Yu, Yabo Dong, Duanqing Xu

- **Summary**: 多模态LLM在时间序列异常检测中依赖粗启发式，缺乏细粒度推理。提出AnomSeer，利用专家思维链和基于最优传输的策略优化。3B/7B模型在分类和定位上超越GPT-4o。

<h3 id="807">[807] Learning to Remember, Learn, and Forget in Attention-Based Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.09075](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09075) (replaced)

<h3 id="808">[808] SceneSmith: Agentic Generation of Simulation-Ready Indoor Scenes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.09153](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.09153) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nicholas Pfaff, Thomas Cohn, Sergey Zakharov, Rick Cory, Russ Tedrake
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.GR

<h3 id="809">[809] Beware of the Batch Size: Hyperparameter Bias in Evaluating LoRA</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI

<h3 id="810">[810] Mitigating Reward Hacking in RLHF via Bayesian Non-negative Reward Modeling</h3>

- **Authors**: Zhibin Duan, Guowei Rong, Zhuo Li, Bo Chen, Mingyuan Zhou, Dandan Guo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RLHF, Reward Hacking, Bayesian Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/GuoweiRong/Bayesian-Non-negative-Reward-Model)
- **Summary**: 奖励模型易受奖励欺骗。提出BNRM贝叶斯非负奖励模型，通过稀疏非负潜因子建模和变分推断。有效缓解奖励过优化，提升分布偏移鲁棒性。

<h3 id="811">[811] What Do LLMs Know About Alzheimer's Disease? Multi-loss Fine-Tuning and Probing for AD Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11177) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lei Jiang, Yue Zhou, Natalie Parde
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Clinical NLP, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 阿尔茨海默病早期检测面临标注数据有限的问题。评估多种LLM架构（BERT、T5、Llama-1B）用于文本检测，在Pitt和CCC上达到新SOTA，并分析内部表示。

<h3 id="812">[812] SWE-MiniSandbox: Container-Free Reinforcement Learning for Building Software Engineering Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11210) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Danlong Yuan, Wei Wu, Enhan Zhao, Zhengren Wang, Xueliang Zhao, Huishuai Zhang, Dongyan Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Reinforcement Learning, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 基于容器的RL训练SWE智能体开销大。提出SWE-MiniSandbox无容器方法，使用内核级隔离和轻量缓存。磁盘和准备时间分别降至5%和25%，性能可比。

<h3 id="813">[813] From Noise to Order: Learning to Rank via Denoising Diffusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.11453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11453) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sajad Ebrahimi, Bhaskar Mitra, Negar Arabzadeh, Ye Yuan, Haolun Wu, Fattane Zarrinkalam, Ebrahim Bagheri
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Information Retrieval, Diffusion Model, Learning to Rank
- **Summary**: 学习排序方法局限于判别式。提出DiffusionRank扩散生成方法，对特征和标签联合建模。在四个标准数据集上优于判别式对手。

<h3 id="814">[814] You Can Learn Tokenization End-to-End with Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.13940](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.13940) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sam Dauncey, Roger Wattenhofer
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tokenization, Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 分词是硬编码压缩步骤。提出用强化学习得分函数估计端到端学习分词边界，时间折扣降低方差。在1亿参数规模上优于直通估计。

<h3 id="815">[815] DenseMLLM: Standard Multimodal LLMs for Dense Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14134](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14134) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yi Li, Hongze Shen, Lexiang Tang, Xin Li, Xinpeng Ding, Yinsong Liu, Deqiang Jiang, Xing Sun, Xiaomeng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Dense Prediction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Eli-YiLi/DenseMLLM)
- **Summary**: 多模态LLM扩展至密集预测需要额外解码器。提出DenseMLLM，标准架构配合视觉令牌监督，无需专用解码器。在多个密集预测和视觉语言基准上表现优异。

<h3 id="816">[816] Atomix: Timely, Transactional Tool Use for Reliable Agentic Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.14849](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14849) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bardia Mohammadi, Nearchos Potamitis, Lars Klein, Akhil Arora, Laurent Bindschaedler
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.MA
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Learning, Workflow Automation
- **Summary**: LLM智能体工具调用导致部分效应和冲突。提出Atomix基于进度感知事务，分离效果分组和冲突判断。在注入故障下提升恢复，隔离竞争，开销微秒级。

<h3 id="817">[817] Knowing Isn't Understanding: Re-grounding Generative Proactivity with Epistemic and Behavioral Insight</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.15259](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15259) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kirandeep Kaur, Xingda Lyu, Chirag Shah
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Ethics, Proactive Agent
- **Summary**: 针对用户无法清晰表达需求时，生成式AI代理难以主动提供帮助的问题，提出将主动行为建立在认知和行为双重基础之上，结合无知的哲学理论和主动行为研究，设计能够负责任地促进人机合作的代理。论文主要从理论层面论证，无具体定量指标。

<h3 id="818">[818] Visual Persuasion: What Influences Decisions of Vision-Language Models?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.15278](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.15278) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Manuel Cherep, Pranav M R, Pattie Maes, Nikhil Singh
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, AI Safety, Interpretability
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 研究视觉语言模型在图像选择任务中的偏好，通过系统编辑图像并优化视觉提示，发现优化编辑能显著改变模型的选择概率。提出了一个自动可解释性流程来识别驱动选择的视觉主题，为AI安全审计提供支持。

<h3 id="819">[819] APEX-SQL: Talking to the data via Agentic Exploration for Text-to-SQL</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16720](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16720) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Bowen Cao, Weibin Liao, Yushi Sun, Dong Fang, Haitao Li, Wai Lam
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-SQL, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Tencent/APEX-SQL-Project)
- **Summary**: 针对传统Text-to-SQL在大型企业数据库中依赖静态模式、无法消除语义歧义的问题，提出APEX-SQL框架，采用假设-验证循环和代理探索机制。在BIRD和Spider 2.0-Snow上分别达到70.65%和51.01%的执行准确率，优于现有基线且token消耗更少。

<h3 id="820">[820] PETS: A Principled Framework Towards Optimal Trajectory Allocation for Efficient Test-Time Self-Consistency</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.16745](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16745) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhangyi Liu, Huaizhi Qu, Xiaowei Yin, He Sun, Yanjun Han, Tianlong Chen, Zhun Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Test-Time Scaling, Self-Consistency
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZDCSlab/PETS)
- **Summary**: 针对测试时自一致性方法采样效率低的问题，提出PETS框架，通过轨迹分配优化实现高效自一致性。在GPQA上，离线设置减少75%采样预算，在线设置减少55%，同时达到完美自一致性。

<h3 id="821">[821] Are LLMs Ready for Neural-integrated Mechanistic Modeling? A Benchmark and Agentic Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.18008](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18008) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zihan Guan, Rituparna Datta, Mengxuan Hu, Shunshun Liu, Aiying Zhang, Prasanna Balachandran, Sheng Li, Anil Vullikanti
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Scientific Discovery, LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 针对当前LLM在神经-积分机械建模中探索空间复杂、稳定性差的问题，提出NIMM基准和NIMMGen树引导代理框架。实验表明NIMMGen在三个科学领域上达到最先进性能，显著提升搜索稳定性和解质量。

<h3 id="822">[822] LERD: Latent Event-Relational Dynamics for Neurodegenerative Classification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.18195](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.18195) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yicheng Feng, Hairong Chen, Ziyu Jia, Samir Bhatt, Hengguan Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, EEG Classification, Disease Diagnosis
- **Summary**: 针对阿尔茨海默病EEG诊断中黑盒分类器无法建模潜在事件时序和跨通道协调的问题，提出LERD贝叶斯潜在事件-关系动态系统。在合成数据和两个真实AD EEG数据集上持续优于强基线，生成与生理学一致的时序和图总结。

<h3 id="823">[823] IDLM: Inverse-distilled Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.19066](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.19066) (replaced)
- **Authors**: David Li, Nikita Gushchin, Dmitry Abulkhanov, Eric Moulines, Ivan Oseledets, Maxim Panov, Alexander Korotin
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Language Modeling, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 针对扩散语言模型推理慢的问题，将逆蒸馏扩展到离散文本生成，证明解的唯一性并引入梯度稳定松弛。在多个DLM上实现4-64倍推理步数减少，同时保持教师模型生成质量。

<h3 id="824">[824] Learning Discriminative and Generalizable Anomaly Detector for Dynamic Graph with Limited Supervision</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.20019](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.20019) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxing Tian, Yiyan Qi, Fengran Mo, Weixu Zhang, Jian Guo, Jian-Yun Nie
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dynamic Graph, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 针对动态图异常检测中标注少、半监督方法泛化差的问题，提出泛化框架，包含残差表示编码、限制损失和双边界优化。在多种评估设置下表现优越。

<h3 id="825">[825] On Imbalanced Regression with Hoeffding Trees</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.22101](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22101) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pantia-Marina Alchirch, Dimitrios I. Diochnos
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Imbalanced Regression, Streaming Learning, Decision Tree
- **Venue**: PAKDD 2026 special session on Data Science: Foundations and Applications (DSFA)
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/marinaAlchirch/DSFA_2026)
- **Summary**: 问题：连续数据流中的不平衡回归问题，现有Hoeffding树在流式场景下处理不平衡数据不足。方法：通过望远镜式公式将核密度估计扩展到流式设置，并将层次收缩集成到增量决策树中。结果：核密度估计持续提升早期流性能，层次收缩增益有限。

<h3 id="826">[826] Evaluating Reliability Asymmetries in Chinese Factual Search and AI Answers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.22221](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.22221) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Geng Liu, Li Feng, Mengxiao Zhu, Francesco Pierri
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Fact Checking, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 针对中文网络搜索和AI答案可靠性难以评估的问题，构建基于真实搜索日志的查询级事实验证数据集，对比九种系统。条件准确率73.2%-78.9%，但搜索系统给出确定性答案的比例高于语言模型，并发现一致性极性差距。

<h3 id="827">[827] Scaling Search Relevance: Augmenting App Store Ranking with LLM-Generated Judgments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23234](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23234) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Evangelia Christakopoulou, Vivekkumar Patel, Hemanth Velaga, Sandip Gaikwad, Sean Suchter, Venkat Sundaranatha
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Information Retrieval, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对大型商业搜索系统中文本相关性标签稀缺的问题，使用微调LLM生成数百万文本相关性标签，增强排序模型。离线和线上A/B测试表明转换率提升0.24%，尾部查询性能提升显著。

<h3 id="828">[828] Interpretable Multimodal Gesture Recognition for Drone and Mobile Robot Teleoperation via Log-Likelihood Ratio Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2602.23694](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.23694) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Seungyeol Baek, Jaspreet Singh, Lala Shakti Swarup Ray, Hymalai Bello, Paul Lukowicz, Sungho Suh
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Gesture Recognition, Multimodal Learning, Human-Robot Interaction
- **Summary**: 针对视觉手势识别在遮挡、光照变化下性能下降的问题，提出融合惯性数据和电容传感信号的多模态手势识别框架，采用对数似然比融合。在20种手势数据集上，达到与视觉基线相当的性能，大幅降低计算成本和模型大小。

<h3 id="829">[829] You Don't Need All That Attention: Surgical Memorization Mitigation in Text-to-Image Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.00133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00133) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kairan Zhao, Eleni Triantafillou, Peter Triantafillou
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Diffusion Model, AI Safety, Memorization Mitigation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对文本到图像扩散模型记忆训练数据引发隐私问题，提出GUARD框架，通过动态交叉注意力衰减引导生成远离训练图像。在两个架构上对逐字和模板记忆均取得最优缓解效果，同时保持图像质量。

<h3 id="830">[830] LookWise: Knowing When and Where to Look for Fine-Grained Visual Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.00171](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00171) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxiang Shen, Hailong Huang, Zhenkun Gao, Xueheng Li, Man Zhou, Chengjun Xie, Haoxuan Che, Xuanhua He, Jie Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Visual Reasoning, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大语言模型在细粒度视觉推理中计算成本高的问题，提出LookWise自适应框架，通过置信度模块决定何时细看，语义引导定位模块确定看哪里。在多个基准上提升准确率，推理速度比ZoomEye快约4倍。

<h3 id="831">[831] Constitutional Black-Box Monitoring for Scheming in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.00829](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.00829) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Simon Storf, Rich Barton-Cooper, James Peters-Gill, Marius Hobbhahn
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, AI Safety, LLM Agent
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对LLM代理隐藏恶意行为的监控问题，研究基于黑盒监控的宪法方法，在合成数据上优化提示，并在ControlArena中评估。结果表明基于合成数据的监控能泛化到更真实环境，但性能容易饱和且过拟合。

<h3 id="832">[832] Concept Heterogeneity-aware Representation Steering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02237](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02237) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Laziz U. Abdullaev, Noelle Y. L. Wong, Ryan T. Z. Lee, Shiqi Jiang, Khoi N. M. Nguyen, Tan M. Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Representation Intervention, Optimal Transport
- **Summary**: 针对现有表示引导方法使用全局方向忽略了表示的非均匀性问题，提出CHaRS，基于最优传输的输入相关引导映射。实验表明CHaRS比全局引导更有效。

<h3 id="833">[833] Large Electron Model: A Universal Ground State Predictor</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02346](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02346) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Timothy Zaklama, Max Geier, Liang Fu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Neural Wavefunction, Scientific Computing
- **Summary**: 本文针对密度泛函理论在处理强电子关联时的局限性，提出大电子模型（Large Electron Model），利用Fermi Sets架构构建单一神经网络，以哈密顿量参数和粒子数为条件，生成相互作用电子的变分波函数。该模型在二维谐振子势中准确预测了多达50个粒子的基态能量和实空间电荷密度，并泛化至未见过的耦合强度和粒子数，为基于变分原理的材料发现奠定了基础。

<h3 id="834">[834] Improving Diffusion Planners by Self-Supervised Action Gating with Energies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02650](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02650) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuan Lu, Dongqi Han, Yansen Wang, Dongsheng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Offline RL, Diffusion Model, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 针对扩散规划器在离线强化学习中因局部动力学不一致而失效的问题，提出SAGE（基于能量的自监督动作门控），通过联合嵌入预测架构和动作条件潜在预测器计算样本的潜在预测误差作为能量，并与价值评分结合重新排序。该方法无需环境滚动或策略重新训练，在运动、导航和操作基准上提升了扩散规划器的性能和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="835">[835] SPARC: Spatial-Aware Path Planning via Attentive Agent Communication</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.02845](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02845) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sayang Mu, Xiangyu Wu, Bo An
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Path Finding, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 针对多机器人路径规划中现有通信方法无法区分空间邻近性的问题，提出关系增强多头注意力（RMHA），将曼哈顿距离显式嵌入注意力权重计算，并结合距离约束掩码和GRU门控融合。在MAPPO框架下，从8机器人零样本泛化至128机器人，在40x40网格、30%障碍密度下达到约75%成功率，优于基线25个百分点以上。

<h3 id="836">[836] One Bias After Another: Mechanistic Reward Shaping and Persistent Biases in Language Reward Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03291](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03291) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Daniel Fein, Max Lamparth, Violet Xiang, Mykel J. Kochenderfer, Nick Haber
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Bias Mitigation, Reward Hacking
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 本文系统测量了五个高质量奖励模型中的长度、谄媚、过度自信、风格和答案顺序偏见，发现这些偏见仍然存在。提出一种后验干预方法——机械奖励塑形，通过线性干预减少低复杂度偏见，仅需少量标注数据即可在不降低奖励质量的情况下泛化至分布外场景。

<h3 id="837">[837] Escaping the BLEU Trap: A Signal-Grounded Framework with Decoupled Semantic Guidance for EEG-to-Text Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03312](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03312) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuchen Wang, Haonan Wang, Yu Guo, Honglong Yang, Xiaomeng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC; eess.AS; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: Brain-Computer Interface, EEG, LLM Hallucination
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/xmed-lab/SemKey)
- **Summary**: 针对EEG到文本解码中的语义偏差、信号忽略和BLEU陷阱问题，提出SemKey多阶段框架，从EEG嵌入中解耦出情感、主题、长度和惊奇度四个语义目标，并通过主动检索解码机制强制LLM基于神经信号生成文本。实验证明SemKey有效缓解噪声输入下的幻觉，在Fréchet距离等鲁棒指标上达到最优。

<h3 id="838">[838] HALO: Learning Human-Robot Collaboration via Heterogeneous-Agent Lyapunov Policy Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.03741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03741) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hao Zhang, Yaru Niu, Yikai Wang, Ding Zhao, H. Eric Tseng
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Human-Robot Interaction, Multi-Agent System
- **Summary**: 针对人机协作中异质性导致的理性差距和策略更新不稳定的问题，提出HALO框架，通过Lyapunov收缩条件稳定去中心化多智能体强化学习，利用最优二次投影修正梯度，确保理性差距单调收缩。仿真和真实人形机器人实验证明了该方法的泛化性和鲁棒性。

<h3 id="839">[839] Med-V1: Small Language Models for Zero-shot and Scalable Biomedical Evidence Attribution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.05308](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.05308) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiao Jin, Yin Fang, Lauren He, Yifan Yang, Guangzhi Xiong, Zhizheng Wang, Nicholas Wan, Joey Chan, Donald C. Comeau, Robert Leaman, Charalampos S. Floudas, Aidong Zhang, Michael F. Chiang, Yifan Peng, Zhiyong Lu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Small Language Model, Medical AI, LLM Hallucination, Fact Checking
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ncbi-nlp/Med-V1)
- **Summary**: 针对前沿LLM在生物医学证据归因中成本过高的问题，提出Med-V1系列3B参数小语言模型，基于高质量合成数据训练。在五个生物医学基准上性能提升27%-71%，媲美GPT-5。应用案例显示，不同引用指令显著影响LLM答案的幻觉率，且Med-V1能自动识别临床实践指南中的错误引用。

<h3 id="840">[840] Heterogeneous Decentralized Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.06741](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.06741) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhiying Jiang, Raihan Seraj, Marcos Villagra, Bidhan Roy
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Distributed Training, Decentralized Learning
- **Summary**: 针对扩散模型训练资源密集且现有去中心化方法要求同质目标的问题，提出一种异构去中心化训练框架，允许不同专家使用DDPM或Flow Matching目标，通过预训练检查点转换和高效架构，在推理时无需重训练即可统一。在LAION-Aesthetics上相比前人工作减少16倍计算和14倍数据，在FID和多样性上优于同质基线。

<h3 id="841">[841] DyLLM: Efficient Diffusion LLM Inference via Saliency-based Token Selection and Partial Attention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.08026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.08026) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Younjoo Lee, Seungkyun Dan, Junghoo Lee, Jaiyoung Park, Jung Ho Ahn
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.PF
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, LLM Inference, Token Pruning
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 针对掩码扩散语言模型迭代去噪过程中重复处理整个序列导致计算昂贵的问题，提出DyLLM，通过测量相邻去噪步注意力上下文的余弦相似度识别显著token，仅重计算这些token的前馈和注意力操作，其余重用缓存激活。在推理和代码生成基准上实现高达9.6倍吞吐量提升，同时保持准确率。

<h3 id="842">[842] ActiveUltraFeedback: Efficient Preference Data Generation using Active Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.09692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.09692) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Davit Melikidze, Marian Schneider, Jessica Lam, Martin Wertich, Ido Hakimi, Barna Pásztor, Andreas Krause
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Active Learning, Preference Optimization
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/lasgroup/ActiveUltraFeedback)
- **Summary**: 针对RLHF偏好数据获取成本高的问题，提出ACTIVEULTRAFEEDBACK主动学习流水线，利用不确定性估计动态选择最具信息量的响应对，包括双反向汤普森采样和DeltaUCB两种新方法。实验表明，仅需静态基线六分之一的标注数据即可达到相当或更优的下游性能。

<h3 id="843">[843] Geometry-Aware Probabilistic Circuits via Voronoi Tessellations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.11946](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.11946) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sahil Sidheekh, Sriraam Natarajan
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Inference, Generative Model, Probabilistic Circuits
- **Summary**: 针对概率电路使用数据无关的混合权重而无法捕捉局部几何结构的问题，提出基于Voronoi图的结构引入，但破坏了可处理性。为此开发了带有保证边界的近似推理以及恢复精确可处理性的结构条件。在标准密度估计任务上经验验证了该方法。

<h3 id="844">[844] Ethical Fairness in Ubiquitous Health Sensing without Known Attributes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.13373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13373) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Shaily Roy, Harshit Sharma, Daniel A. Adler, Srijan Sen, Tanzeem Choudhury, Asif Salekin
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Fairness, Medical AI, Bias Mitigation
- **Summary**: 针对普适健康感知中缺乏人口统计属性时难以实施公平性约束的问题，提出Flare框架，利用Fisher信息正则化曲率并发现潜在性能差异，结合“不伤害”优化策略改善子群性能。在多个移动生理和行为数据集上提升了伦理公平性，并引入BHE指标套件。

<h3 id="845">[845] ES-Merging: Biological MLLM Merging via Embedding Space Signals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.14405](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14405) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Wonbin Lee, Dongki Kim, Sung Ju Hwang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Merging, Multimodal Learning, Vision-Language Model, Computational Biology
- **Summary**: 针对现有生物多模态大模型专精于单一模态，而模型合并方法依赖参数空间启发式无法捕捉模态专长的问题，提出基于嵌入空间信号的ES-Merging框架，从粗细粒度估计层和元素级合并系数。实验表明该方法在跨模态推理和单模态知识保留上均优于现有合并方法。

<h3 id="846">[846] ExpertGen: Scalable Sim-to-Real Expert Policy Learning from Imperfect Behavior Priors</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.15956](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15956) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zifan Xu, Ran Gong, Maria Vittoria Minniti, Kausik Sivakumar, Ahmet Salih Gundogdu, Eric Rosen, Riedana Yan, Tushar Kusnur, Zixing Wang, Di Deng, Peter Stone, Xiaohan Zhang, Karl Schmeckpeper
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sim-to-Real, Imitation Learning, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对真实世界大规模专家演示数据获取昂贵的问题，提出ExpertGen框架，先用不完美演示（LLM合成或人类）训练扩散策略作为行为先验，再通过强化学习优化先验的初始噪声以实现高任务成功率，同时保持策略冻结以约束探索。在工业组装任务中达90.5%成功率，长程操作任务85%成功率，并成功迁移至真实机器人。

<h3 id="847">[847] Context Matters: Repository-Aware Security Analysis of the Agent Skill Ecosystem</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.16572](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16572) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Florian Holzbauer, David Schmidt, Gabriel Gegenhuber, Sebastian Schrittwieser, Johanna Ullrich
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Cybersecurity, Vulnerability Detection
- **Summary**: 针对现有代理技能安全扫描器高估恶意率且忽略仓库上下文的问题，对来自三个平台和GitHub的238,180个技能进行最大规模实证安全分析。通过仓库感知分析，仅0.52%的技能仍存疑（之前高达46.8%），同时发现劫持废弃仓库等真实攻击向量，强调了上下文感知安全评估的必要性。

<h3 id="848">[848] scicode-lint: Detecting Methodology Bugs in Scientific Python Code with LLM-Generated Patterns</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.17893](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.17893) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sergey V. Samsonau
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Scientific Computing, Software Testing
- **Summary**: 针对科学Python代码中传统检查器无法检测的方法学错误（如数据泄露、错误交叉验证），提出scicode-lint，采用双层架构：构建时由前沿模型生成检测模式，运行时由小型本地模型执行。在Kaggle笔记本中预处理泄露检测精度65%、召回率100%；在38篇论文中精度62%；在66个模式上准确率97.7%。

<h3 id="849">[849] MineDraft: A Framework for Batch Parallel Speculative Decoding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18016](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18016) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhenwei Tang, Arun Verma, Zijian Zhou, Zhaoxuan Wu, Alok Prakash, Daniela Rus, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.DC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Speculative Decoding, LLM Inference
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 标准推测解码因串行执行草案生成与验证阶段而受限。本文提出MineDraft，一种批量并行推测解码框架，通过维护两个请求批次实现草案生成与验证的重叠。实验表明，与标准推测解码相比，MineDraft在吞吐量上最高提升75%，端到端延迟最高降低39%，并已作为vLLM插件实现。

<h3 id="850">[850] To See or To Please: Uncovering Visual Sycophancy and Split Beliefs in VLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18373) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Rui Hong, Shuxue Quan
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Hallucination
- **Summary**: 在视觉语言模型中，正确回答可能并非真正依赖视觉信息。本文提出三层诊断框架，通过潜在异常检测、视觉必要性评分和竞争分数来解耦感知、依赖和对齐失败。实验发现72.9%的样本存在视觉谄媚现象，即内部证据保留但解码出幻觉答案。诊断分数支持免训练选择性预测，在50%覆盖率下准确率提升高达9.5个百分点。

<h3 id="851">[851] Beyond String Matching: Semantic Evaluation of PDF Table Extraction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.18652](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18652) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Pius Horn, Janis Keuper
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, LLM Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/phorn1/pdf-parse-bench), [code](https://github.com/phorn1/table-metric-study)
- **Summary**: 现有PDF表格提取评估依赖基于规则的指标，无法捕捉表格内容的语义等价性。本文提出基于合成PDF和LLM评判的语义评估框架，并与人类判断高度相关（皮尔逊r=0.93），显著高于TEDS（0.68）和GriTS（0.70）。对21个解析器的评估揭示了性能差异，为该任务提供了可复现的评估方法。

<h3 id="852">[852] AgentDS Technical Report: Benchmarking the Future of Human-AI Collaboration in Domain-Specific Data Science</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.19005](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19005) (replaced)
- **Authors**: An Luo, Jin Du, Xun Xian, Robert Specht, Fangqiao Tian, Ganghua Wang, Xuan Bi, Charles Fleming, Ashish Kundu, Jayanth Srinivasa, Mingyi Hong, Rui Zhang, Tianxi Li, Galin Jones, Jie Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ME
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 当前AI代理在领域特定数据科学任务上能否匹配人类专家尚不明确。本文提出AgentDS基准，包含6个行业的17个挑战，通过公开竞赛比较人类-AI协作与纯AI方法。结果显示纯AI基线仅低于竞争者的前四分之一，最强解决方案来自人机协作，挑战了完全自动化的说法。

<h3 id="853">[853] Graph Energy Matching: Transport-Aligned Energy-Based Modeling for Graph Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.23398](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23398) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Michal Balcerak, Suprosanna Shit, Chinmay Prabhakar, Sebastian Kaltenbach, Michael S. Albergo, Yilun Du, Bjoern Menze
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Graph Generation, Energy-Based Model
- **Summary**: 离散能量模型在高质量图生成上存在采样效率低的问题。本文提出图能量匹配（GEM），受JKO传输映射优化启发，学习排列不变势能。在分子图基准上，GEM匹配或超越离散扩散模型，并支持组合生成和属性约束采样。

<h3 id="854">[854] Failure of contextual invariance in large language models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.23485](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23485) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sagar Kumar, Ariel Flint, Luca Maria Aiello, Andrea Baronchelli
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Bias Detection
- **Summary**: 标准评估假设LLM在语境等价提示下输出稳定，但本文通过受控代词选择任务发现最小无关语境会导致输出发生大规模系统性偏移。语境性缺省分析显示，在19-52%的案例中，这种依赖关系无法归因于简单代词重复，表明LLM输出违反语境不变性。

<h3 id="855">[855] λSplit: Self-Supervised Content-Aware Spectral Unmixing for Fluorescence Microscopy</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.23647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.23647) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Federico Carrara, Talley Lambert, Mehdi Seifi, Florian Jug
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 经典光谱解混方法在面对重叠发射光谱和高噪声时性能下降。本文提出λSplit，一种基于物理信息的深度生成模型，使用分层变分自编码器和可微光谱混合器。在66个基准上达到最先进性能，在高噪声和低光谱维度下鲁棒性提升。

<h3 id="856">[856] Large Language Model Guided Incentive Aware Reward Design for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.24324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24324) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Dogan Urgun, Gokhan Gungor
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, Reward Design
- **Summary**: 协作多智能体系统的辅助奖励设计易导致次优协调。本文提出LLM引导的奖励设计框架，自动合成可执行奖励程序，使用MAPPO训练并基于稀疏任务回报选择。在Overcooked-AI环境中，该方法在任务回报和交付计数上一致提升，尤其在高交互瓶颈布局中效果显著。

<h3 id="857">[857] Claudini: Autoresearch Discovers State-of-the-Art Adversarial Attack Algorithms for LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.24511](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24511) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Alexander Panfilov, Peter Romov, Igor Shilov, Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye, Jonas Geiping, Maksym Andriushchenko
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: AI代理能够自主发现针对LLM的新型对抗攻击算法。本文部署前沿代理在自动研究循环中，结合30多种已有方法库，实现对GPT-OSS-Safeguard的CBRN查询达到80%攻击成功率（现有方法低于50%），对SecAlign达到100%成功率。该方法在无关替代模型上开发，却可直接迁移到对抗训练模型。

<h3 id="858">[858] Limits of Spatial Imagery Reasoning in Frontier LLM Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.26779](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.26779) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Sergio Y. Hayashi, Nina S. T. Hirata
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spatial Reasoning, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 前沿LLM在需要心理模拟的空间任务（如心理旋转）中表现不佳。本文尝试为LLM配备外部“意象模块”以渲染和旋转3D模型，但准确率最高仅62.5%。进一步分析表明，即使外化3D状态维护，模型仍缺乏视觉-空间基元，包括深度、运动和短时动态预测的低级敏感性，以及动态视觉聚焦能力。

<h3 id="859">[859] EuraGovExam: A Multilingual Multimodal Benchmark from Real-World Civil Service Exams</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.27223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.27223) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaeseong Kim, Chaehwan Lim, Sang Hyun Gil, Suan Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Vision-Language Model, Multilingual Learning
- **Summary**: 现有基准缺乏文化真实性、视觉复杂性和语言多样性。本文提出EuraGovExam，源自五个欧亚地区的真实公务员考试，包含8000多张扫描选择题图像。最先进的视觉语言模型仅达86%准确率，突显了高难度和当前模型的局限性。

<h3 id="860">[860] Incentives, Equilibria, and the Limits of Healthcare AI: A Game-Theoretic Perspective</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.28825](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.28825) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ari Ercole
- **Subjects**: cs.GT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Game Theory
- **Summary**: 医疗AI部署常因激励不匹配而失败。本文利用博弈论分析三种AI部署形式：减少努力型、增强可观察型和改变激励型。减少努力和可观察型不会改变个体理性行为，通常被现有均衡吸收；只有改变激励的干预能改变稳定系统行为。

<h3 id="861">[861] Understand and Accelerate Memory Processing Pipeline for Large Language Model Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2603.29002](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.29002) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zifan He, Rui Ma, Yizhou Sun, Jason Cong
- **Subjects**: cs.DC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Heterogeneous Computing
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/OswaldHe/HeteroLLM)
- **Summary**: 现代LLM推断中的长上下文处理机制（如稀疏注意力、RAG）带来22-97%的内存处理开销。本文系统分析并统一为四步内存处理流水线，提出GPU-FPGA异构系统加速稀疏、内存受限操作。在AMD MI210+Alveo U55C上，相比GPU基线速度提升2.2倍，能耗降低4.7倍。

<h3 id="862">[862] Acoustic and perceptual differences between standard and accented speech and their voice clones</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.01562](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01562) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Tianle Yang, Chengzhe Sun, Phil Rose, Siwei Lyu
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Voice Cloning, Speech Synthesis
- **Summary**: 语音克隆评估常忽略口音保留及其感知影响。本文比较标准与重口音普通话语音及其克隆，发现克隆在标准口音上评分更高，而口音语音的克隆可懂度提升更大。结果表明口音变化会影响身份匹配和可懂度，应将口音保留作为说话人身份保留的显式组成部分。

<h3 id="863">[863] Automated Conjecture Resolution with Formal Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.03789](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03789) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Haocheng Ju, Guoxiong Gao, Jiedong Jiang, Bin Wu, Zeming Sun, Shurui Liu, Leheng Chen, Yutong Wang, Yuefeng Wang, Zichen Wang, Wanyi He, Peihao Wu, Liang Xiao, Ruochuan Liu, Bryan Dai, Bin Dong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Formal Methods
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/frenzymath/Rethlas), [code](https://github.com/frenzymath/Rethlas_results), [code](https://github.com/frenzymath/Archon), [code](https://github.com/frenzymath/Anderson-Conjecture)
- **Summary**: 自然语言数学推理存在固有歧义，影响可靠求解与验证。本文提出集成非正式推理代理（Rethlas+定理搜索引擎Matlas）和形式验证代理（Archon+LeanSearch）的框架，能够自动构造并验证研究级数学证明。该框架成功解决了一个交换代数开放问题，并在Lean 4中完全自动形式化验证。

<h3 id="864">[864] CalM: A Self-Supervised Foundation Model for Population Dynamics in Calcium Imaging Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.04958](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04958) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Xinhong Xu, Yimeng Zhang, Qichen Qian, Yuanlong Zhang
- **Subjects**: q-bio.QM; cs.AI; q-bio.NC
- **Tags**: Self-Supervised Learning, Foundation Model, Neuroscience
- **Venue**: ICML
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TSuXinH/CalM)
- **Summary**: 现有钙成像数据分析方法任务特定，限制了跨任务迁移。本文提出CalM，一个自监督神经基础模型，包含高性能分词器和双轴自回归Transformer。在种群动态预测上达到竞争性能，在行为解码上优于有监督模型，且表征具有可解释功能性结构。

<h3 id="865">[865] Distributional Open-Ended Evaluation of LLM Cultural Value Alignment Based on Value Codebook</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.06210](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06210) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jaehyeok Lee, Xiaoyuan Yi, Jing Yao, Hyunjin Hwang, Roy Ka-Wei Lee, Xing Xie, JinYeong Bak
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CY; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, LLM Alignment
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 现有LLM文化价值对齐基准面临构建-组成-上下文（C³）挑战，即采用判别式选择题形式、忽略亚文化异质性、与实际开放生成场景不匹配。提出DOVE分布评估框架，利用率失真变分优化从1万篇文档构建紧凑价值码本，通过不平衡最优传输测量对齐。与下游任务相关性达31.56%，且仅需每种文化500个样本即可保持高可靠性。

<h3 id="866">[866] AtomEval: Validity-Aware Atomic Evaluation of Adversarial Claim Rewriting in Fact Verification</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.07967](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07967) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Hongyi Cen, Mingxin Wang, Yule Liu, Jingyi Zheng, Hanze Jia, Tan Tang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fact Checking, Adversarial Robustness
- **Summary**: 传统攻击成功率（ASR）因改写可能改变、弱化或纠正原始虚假命题而被高估。提出AtomEval，将声明表示为SROM原子，通过单向保留门区分有效逃避与命题改变，报告有效性感知攻击成功率（VASR）。揭示并解释了ASR膨胀，提供了细粒度诊断。

<h3 id="867">[867] Multi-Modal Learning meets Genetic Programming: Analyzing Alignment in Latent Space Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.08324](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08324) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Benjamin Léger, Kazem Meidani, Christian Gagné
- **Subjects**: cs.NE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Symbolic Regression, Multi-Modal Learning, Genetic Programming
- **Summary**: SNIP方法声称通过多模态对比学习实现符号回归的细粒度跨模态对齐，但实际对齐可能粗糙。实验分析SNIP在优化过程中的对齐质量。发现交叉模态对齐在优化中并未改善，且SNIP学习的对齐过于粗糙，无法有效指导符号空间搜索。

<h3 id="868">[868] U-Cast: A Surprisingly Simple and Efficient Frontier Probabilistic AI Weather Forecaster</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.09041](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09041) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Salva Rühling Cachay, Duncan Watson-Parris, Rose Yu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Weather Forecasting, Probabilistic Forecasting
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Rose-STL-Lab/u-cast)
- **Summary**: 现有SOTA AI天气预报模型依赖专门架构和巨大计算预算，门槛高。U-Cast基于标准U-Net，采用确定性MAE预训练+短时概率CRPS微调，利用蒙特卡洛Dropout实现随机性。在1.5°分辨率下匹配GenCast和IFS ENS的概率技能，训练计算量降低超10倍，推理延迟降低超10倍。

<h3 id="869">[869] Frequency-Enhanced Diffusion Models: Curriculum-Guided Semantic Alignment for Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.09063](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09063) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yuxi Zhou, Zhengbo Zhang, Jingyu Pan, Zhiyu Lin, Zhigang Tu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Skeleton-Based Recognition, Zero-Shot Learning, Diffusion Model
- **Venue**: The Visual Computer
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yuzhi535/FDSM)
- **Summary**: 扩散模型在零样本骨架动作识别中过度平滑高频动态，导致细粒度运动细节丢失。提出FDSM，集成语义引导频谱残差模块、时间步自适应频谱损失和课程式语义抽象。在NTU RGB+D、PKU-MMD、Kinetics-skeleton数据集上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="870">[870] Beyond Offline A/B Testing: Context-Aware Agent Simulation for Recommender System Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.09549](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09549) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Nicolas Bougie, Gian Maria Marconi, Xiaotong Ye, Narimasa Watanabe
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Agent, User Simulation
- **Summary**: 推荐系统离线评估与在线性能脱节，现有智能体模拟忽略时间、地点等上下文因素。ContextSim框架，通过日常生活活动模拟用户代理，建模内部思想并强制行为与轨迹一致性。生成的交互更符合人类行为，基于ContextSim优化的推荐参数提升真实世界参与度。

<h3 id="871">[871] Genie 4D: Semantic-Prior-Guided 4D Dynamic Scene Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.09877](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.09877) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yiru Yang, Zhuojie Wu, Nishant Kumar Singh, Max Schulthess
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: 3D Gaussian Splatting, Vision Foundation Model, 4D Reconstruction
- **Summary**: 手持手机拍摄的动态场景4D重建面临挑战。Genie 4D耦合实时视觉惯性高斯泼溅前端与4D骨干网络，利用冻结DINOv3特征作为结构先验，结合条件扩散精炼器和潜在动作头。保持线性时间复杂度，提升3D跟踪精度，在消费级GPU上交互运行。

<h3 id="872">[872] AffordGen: Generating Diverse Demonstrations for Generalizable Object Manipulation with Afford Correspondence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10579](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10579) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jiawei Zhang, Kaizhe Hu, Yingqian Huang, Yuanchen Ju, Zhengrong Xue, Huazhe Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Imitation Learning, Affordance Learning
- **Summary**: 模仿学习因数据多样性不足而受限于几何变化。AffordGen框架利用3D生成模型和视觉基础模型，通过跨3D网格的语义对应生成多样化操作轨迹。训练的策略在仿真和真实世界实现高成功率，零样本泛化至全新物体，提升数据效率。

<h3 id="873">[873] Vibe-driven model-based engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10645](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10645) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Jordi Cabot
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Code Generation, Model-Driven Engineering
- **Summary**: 模型驱动工程（MDE）模型日益复杂，而“氛围编码”存在代码漏洞、可扩展性等问题。提出氛围驱动模型驱动工程，集成LLM与MDE的优势。概述了概念、机遇和开放挑战。

<h3 id="874">[874] SCOPE: Signal-Calibrated On-Policy Distillation Enhancement with Dual-Path Adaptive Weighting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10688](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10688) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Binbin Zheng, Xing Ma, Yiheng Liang, Jingqing Ruan, Xiaoliang Fu, Kepeng Lin, Benchang Zhu, Ke Zeng, Xunliang Cai
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Summary**: 在线强化学习奖励稀疏，统一蒸馏忽略信号质量差异。SCOPE双路径自适应训练框架，对错误轨迹用教师困惑度加权KL蒸馏，对正确轨迹用学生困惑度加权MLE。在六个推理基准上，Avg@32平均提升11.42%，Pass@32提升7.30%。

<h3 id="875">[875] TInR: Exploring Tool-Internalized Reasoning in Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.10788](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.10788) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Qiancheng Xu, Yongqi Li, Fan Liu, Hongru Wang, Min Yang, Wenjie Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 现有工具集成推理依赖外部文档，导致工具掌握困难、规模限制和推理低效。TInR-U框架，通过双向知识对齐内化工具知识，再经SFT和RL训练。在域内和域外设置中均表现优越。

<h3 id="876">[876] Representation over Routing: Diagnosing Temporal Routing Pathologies in Multi-Timescale PPO</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.13517](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13517) (replaced)

- **Summary**: 多时间尺度PPO中的时序路由可能产生数值捷径，而非可靠时序抽象。识别了替代目标劫持和时间不确定性悖论，提出目标解耦策略。去除可被利用的路由路径，改善最差种子回报。

<h3 id="877">[877] Just Type It in Isabelle! AI Agents Drafting, Mechanizing, and Generalizing from Human Hints</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.15713](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15713) (replaced)

<h3 id="878">[878] Reward Score Matching: Unifying Reward-based Fine-tuning for Flow and Diffusion Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17415) (replaced)

<h3 id="879">[879] Dual-Anchoring: Addressing State Drift in Vision-Language Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.17473](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17473) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Kangyi Wu, Pengna Li, Kailin Lyu, Xi Lin, Lin Zhao, Qingrong He, Jinjun Wang, Jianyi Liu

- **Summary**: 视频LLM在视觉语言导航中存在状态漂移，导致长场景中无目的徘徊。双锚定框架，包括指令进度锚定和记忆地标锚定。成功率提升15.2%，长轨迹成功率提升24.7%。

<h3 id="880">[880] BEAT: Tokenizing and Generating Symbolic Music by Uniform Temporal Steps</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.19532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19532) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Lekai Qian, Haoyu Gu, Jingwei Zhao, Ziyu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Music Generation, Tokenization, Symbolic Music
- **Summary**: 主流符号音乐分词采用非均匀时间步进，隐含处理节拍规律性。BEAT分词以均匀节拍为基本单元，将同一时间步内所有音高事件编码为一个词元。提升音乐质量和结构连贯性，效率更高，更好捕获长程模式。

<h3 id="881">[881] Deep Interest Mining for Intent-Enriched Semantic IDs in Multimodal Generative Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.20861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20861) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yangchen Zeng, Jinze Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Multimodal Learning
- **Summary**: 现有生成式推荐中语义ID（SID）的质量受限于忽略潜在使用意图和视觉证据。提出DeepInterestGR框架，通过CMSA丰富表示、DCIM挖掘物品侧意图描述、QARM添加相关门控语义奖励。在Amazon产品数据上，NDCG@5提升15.1%，NDCG@10提升13.9%。

<h3 id="882">[882] FlowPlace: Flow Matching for Chip Placement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.23658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.23658) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Peng Xie, Ke Xue, Yunqi Shi, Ruo-Tong Chen, Chengrui Gao, Siyuan Xu, Chenjian Ding, Mingxuan Yuan, Chao Qian
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Circuit Design, Flow Matching
- **Summary**: 现有扩散模型芯片布局方法存在随机预训练、采样时间长、布局重叠等问题。提出FlowPlace，使用流匹配进行高效训练和硬约束采样。在OpenROAD和ICCAD 2015上实现更好的PPA、10-50倍采样加速和零重叠。

<h3 id="883">[883] How Can Reinforcement Learning Achieve Expert-level Placement?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.25191](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.25191) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Ruo-Tong Chen, Ke Xue, Chengrui Gao, Yunqi Shi, Tian Xu, Peng Xie, Siyuan Xu, Mingxuan Yuan, Chao Qian, Zhi-Hua Zhou
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Circuit Design
- **Summary**: RL芯片布局方法因仅优化线长而难以达到专家质量。提出直接学习专家布局，从最终布局反推轨迹并训练奖励模型。实验表明能从单个设计高效学习并泛化到未见案例。

<h3 id="884">[884] MedSynapse-V: Bridging Visual Perception and Clinical Intuition via Latent Memory Evolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26283](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26283) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Chunzheng Zhu, Jiaqi Zeng, Junyu Jiang, Jianxin Lin, Yijun Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 医学VLM因离散token化导致量化损失和长程信息消散。提出MedSynapse-V，通过元查询先验记忆、因果反事实细化和内在记忆过渡模拟临床经验调用。在多个数据集上优于现有方法，尤其是链式思维方法。

<h3 id="885">[885] Defeasible Conditional Obligation in a Two-tiered Preference-based Semantics (Extended Version)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2604.26977](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.26977) (replaced)

<h3 id="886">[886] Beyond Visual Fidelity: Benchmarking Super-Resolution Models for Large-Scale Remote Sensing Imagery via Downstream Task Integration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00310](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00310) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Zhili Li, Kangyang Chai, Zhihao Wang, Xiaowei Jia, Yanhua Li, Gengchen Mai, Sergii Skakun, Dinesh Manocha, Yiqun Xie
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Super-Resolution, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有超分辨率评测仅用保真度指标，忽略下游任务效用。提出GeoSR-Bench，集成土地覆盖分类、基础设施映射等下游任务的基准数据集。实验发现保真度提升与任务性能不相关甚至负相关。

<h3 id="887">[887] Possibilistic Predictive Uncertainty for Deep Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.00600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00600) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Yao Ni, Jeremie Houssineau, Yew Soon Ong, Piotr Koniusz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Uncertainty Estimation, Deep Learning Theory
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/MaxwellYaoNi/DAPPr)
- **Summary**: 贝叶斯不确定性估计计算量大，高效二阶预测器缺乏严格理论。提出Dirichlet近似可能性后验（DAPPr），基于可能性理论通过投影和近似得到简单闭式解。在多个基准上达到竞争或更优的不确定性量化性能且计算高效。

<h3 id="888">[888] STABLEVAL: Disagreement-Aware and Stable Evaluation of AI Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.02122](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.02122) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Akash Bonagiri, Gerard Janno Anderias, Saee Patil, Angelina Lai, Devang Borkar, Gezheng Kang, Ishant Gandhi, Setareh Rafatirad, Houman Homayoun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, AI Evaluation
- **Summary**: 人工评估中多数投票聚合忽略标注者可靠性和歧义，导致排名不稳定。提出STABLEVAL，建模潜在答案和标注者混淆模式，生成后验期望和校准分数。在合成和真实基准上获得更稳定排序。

<h3 id="889">[889] Gradients with Respect to Semantics Preserving Embeddings Tell the Uncertainty of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.04638](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04638) (replaced)
- **Authors**: Mingda Li, Rundong Lv, Xinyu Li, Weinan Zhang, Ting Liu

- **Summary**: 现有LLM不确定性量化依赖采样，成本高方差大。提出SemGrad，首个基于梯度的自由文本UQ方法，在语义空间计算梯度，并引入混合梯度。在多个有效回答场景下优于现有方法。

<h3 id="890">[890] MidSteer: Optimal Affine Framework for Steering Generative Models</h3>

<h3 id="891">[891] Do Joint Audio-Video Generation Models Understand Physics?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2605.07061](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07061) (replaced)

<h3 id="892">[892] Why Self-Inconsistency Arises in GNN Explanations and How to Exploit It</h3>

<h3 id="893">[893] Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning</h3>

- **Summary**: On-policy蒸馏扩展到长程任务时学生轨迹漂移导致奖励不可靠和计算浪费。提出Prune-OPD，动态检测前缀漂移并降权不可靠奖励。减少训练时间37.6%-68.0%，同时保持或提升推理性能。

<h3 id="894">[894] Normalization Equivariance for Arbitrary Backbones, with Application to Image Denoising</h3>

<h3 id="895">[895] Cornerstones or Stumbling Blocks? Deciphering the Rock Tokens in On-Policy Distillation</h3>
